236:     /** The timestamp a backup is stamped with. Seconds are enough, and a
237:      *  filename or a "restored from" line reads better without nanoseconds. */
238:     fun stamp(now: LocalDateTime): String = now.withNano(0).toString()
239: 
240:     /** The backup file, as text ready to be written to storage. */
241:     fun write(doc: Document): String {
242:         val tables = linkedMapOf(
243:             "categories" to doc.categories.map { c ->
244:                 obj(
245:                     "id" to Json.S(c.id),
246:                     "name" to Json.S(c.name),
247:                     "type" to Json.S(c.type),
248:                     "icon" to Json.S(c.icon),
249:                     "color" to Json.S(c.color),
250:                     "budget_amount" to num(c.budgetAmount),
251:                     "is_archived" to Json.B(c.isArchived),
252:                 )
253:             },
254:             "merchants" to doc.merchants.map { m ->
255:                 obj(
256:                     "id" to Json.S(m.id),
257:                     "canonical_name" to Json.S(m.canonicalName),
258:                     "category_id" to text(m.categoryId),
259:                 )
260:             },
261:             "learning" to doc.learning.map { l ->
262:                 obj(
263:                     "id" to Json.S(l.id),
264:                     "raw_name" to Json.S(l.rawName),
265:                     "merchant_name" to Json.S(l.merchantName),
266:                     "category_id" to text(l.categoryId),
267:                     "confirmation_count" to Json.N(l.confirmationCount.toString()),
268:                     "correction_count" to Json.N(l.correctionCount.toString()),
269:                     "sample_count" to Json.N(l.sampleCount.toString()),
270:                     "avg_amount" to Json.N(l.avgAmount.toString()),
271:                     "amount_min" to Json.N(l.amountMin.toString()),
272:                     "amount_max" to Json.N(l.amountMax.toString()),
273:                     "hour_histogram" to Json.S(l.hourHistogram),
274:                     "last_seen_at" to text(l.lastSeenAt?.toString()),
275:                 )
276:             },
277:             "transactions" to doc.transactions.map { t ->
278:                 obj(
279:                     "id" to Json.S(t.id),
280:                     "amount" to Json.N(t.amount.toString()),
281:                     "type" to Json.S(t.type),
282:                     "category_id" to text(t.categoryId),
283:                     "raw_merchant" to text(t.rawMerchant),
284:                     "merchant_name" to text(t.merchantName),
285:                     "notes" to text(t.notes),
286:                     "reference_number" to text(t.referenceNumber),
287:                     "occurred_at" to Json.S(t.occurredAt.toString()),
288:                     "source" to Json.S(t.source),
289:                     "confidence" to num(t.confidence?.toDouble()),
290:                     "status" to Json.S(t.status),
291:                     "category_prompted" to Json.B(t.categoryPrompted),
292:                     "dedup_key" to text(t.dedupKey),
293:                     "is_deleted" to Json.B(t.isDeleted),
294:                     "created_at" to Json.S(t.createdAt.toString()),
295:                 )
296:             },
297:             "sms_senders" to doc.senders.map { s ->
298:                 obj(
299:                     "id" to Json.S(s.id),
300:                     "sender" to Json.S(s.sender),
301:                     "display" to text(s.display),
302:                     "kind" to Json.S(s.kind),
303:                     "entity" to text(s.entity),
304:                     "bank" to text(s.bank),
305:                     "trust" to Json.S(s.trust),
306:                     "message_count" to Json.N(s.messageCount.toString()),
307:                     "captured_count" to Json.N(s.capturedCount.toString()),
308:                     "confirmed_count" to Json.N(s.confirmedCount.toString()),
309:                     "quarantined_count" to Json.N(s.quarantinedCount.toString()),
310:                     "last_risk" to Json.N(s.lastRisk.toString()),
311:                     "first_seen_at" to Json.S(s.firstSeenAt.toString()),
312:                     "last_seen_at" to Json.S(s.lastSeenAt.toString()),
313:                 )
314:             },
315:         )
316: 
317:         val p = doc.prefs
318:         val settings = if (p == null) Json.O(linkedMapOf()) else obj(
319:             "currency" to text(p.currency),
320:             "theme" to text(p.theme),
321:             "auto_save_threshold" to num(p.autoSaveThreshold?.toDouble()),
322:             "confirm_threshold" to num(p.confirmThreshold?.toDouble()),
323:             "high_value_amount" to num(p.highValueAmount),
324:         )
325: 
326:         val root = obj(
327:             "format" to Json.N(FORMAT.toString()),
328:             "app" to Json.S(APP),
329:             "created_at" to Json.S(doc.createdAt),
330:             "tables" to Json.O(LinkedHashMap<String, Json>().also { m ->
331:                 tables.forEach { (name, rows) -> m[name] = Json.A(rows) }
332:             }),
333:             "settings" to settings,
334:             // For a human opening the file. Never read back: a count in a
335:             // document the user can edit is a claim, not a fact.
336:             "counts" to Json.O(LinkedHashMap<String, Json>().also { m ->
337:                 tables.forEach { (name, rows) -> m[name] = Json.N(rows.size.toString()) }
338:             }),
339:         )
340:         return Json.write(root)
341:     }
342: 
343:     // ── reading a file back ──────────────────────────────────────────────
344: 
345:     /** UTF-8 bytes from storage. Separate from the [String] entry point so
346:      *  that a file which is not text at all — a photo, a zip — is refused
347:      *  with a sentence about the file rather than mojibake reaching the
348:      *  parser. */
349:     fun read(bytes: ByteArray, now: LocalDateTime = LocalDateTime.now()): Read {
350:         val decoder = StandardCharsets.UTF_8.newDecoder()
351:             .onMalformedInput(CodingErrorAction.REPORT)
352:             .onUnmappableCharacter(CodingErrorAction.REPORT)
353:         val text = try {
354:             decoder.decode(ByteBuffer.wrap(bytes)).toString()
355:         } catch (e: Exception) {
356:             return Read.Rejected(
357:                 "That file isn't a SpendWise backup — it isn't text this app can read.")
358:         }
359:         return read(text, now)
360:     }
361: 
362:     /**
363:      * Turn a file into a validated backup document, or say why it is not
364:      * one. This is the only place that decides whether a file is acceptable;
365:      * [plan] assumes it has already run.
366:      *
367:      * [now] is passed in rather than read off the clock, so the whole path
368:      * is a function of its inputs and can be tested.
369:      */
370:     fun read(raw: String, now: LocalDateTime = LocalDateTime.now()): Read {
371:         val text = raw.removePrefix("\uFEFF")
372:         if (text.isBlank()) return Read.Rejected("That file is empty.")
373: 
374:         val root = try {
375:             Json.parse(text)
376:         } catch (e: Json.Malformed) {
377:             return Read.Rejected(
378:                 "That file isn't a SpendWise backup — it isn't valid JSON. " +
379:                     "If you meant to import a CSV, use Import instead.")
380:         }
381:         if (root !is Json.O) return Read.Rejected("That file isn't a SpendWise backup.")
382: 
383:         val format = root.fields["format"]
384:         if (format == null || root.fields["app"].asText(MAX_TEXT) != APP) {
385:             return Read.Rejected("That file isn't a SpendWise backup.")
386:         }
387:         val version = (format as? Json.N)?.raw?.toDoubleOrNull()?.toInt()
388:             ?: return Read.Rejected("That file isn't a SpendWise backup.")
389:         if (version > FORMAT) {
390:             return Read.Rejected(
391:                 "That backup was written by a newer version of SpendWise " +
392:                     "(format $version). Update the app, then restore.")
393:         }
394: 
395:         val tables = root.fields["tables"] as? Json.O
396:             ?: return Read.Rejected("That backup is missing its data.")
397: 
398:         // A section that is present but is not a list is damage, not an
399:         // empty ledger, and restoring "nothing" from it would look like a
400:         // success. A section that is simply absent is fine.
401:         val sections = LinkedHashMap<String, List<Json>>()
402:         for ((key, label) in SECTIONS) {
403:             val value = tables.fields[key]
404:             if (value == null || value is Json.Null) { sections[key] = emptyList(); continue }
405:             sections[key] = (value as? Json.A)?.items
406:                 ?: return Read.Rejected("The $label section of that backup is damaged.")
407:         }
408: 
409:         val dropped = LinkedHashMap<String, Int>()
410:         fun <T> rows(key: String, coerce: (Json.O) -> T?): List<T> {
411:             val out = ArrayList<T>()
412:             var lost = 0
413:             for (row in sections.getValue(key)) {
414:                 val value = (row as? Json.O)?.let(coerce)
415:                 if (value == null) lost++ else out.add(value)
416:             }
417:             dropped[key] = lost
418:             return out
419:         }
420: 
421:         val doc = Document(
422:             createdAt = root.fields["created_at"].asText(40) ?: "",
423:             categories = rows("categories") { category(it) },
424:             merchants = rows("merchants") { merchant(it) },
425:             learning = rows("learning") { learned(it) },
426:             transactions = rows("transactions") { txn(it) },
427:             senders = rows("sms_senders") { sender(it, now) },
428:             prefs = (root.fields["settings"] as? Json.O)?.let { prefs(it) },
429:             dropped = dropped,
430:         )
431:         return Read.Ok(doc, summarise(doc))
432:     }
433: 
434:     fun summarise(doc: Document): Summary {
435:         val dates = doc.transactions.map { it.occurredAt.toLocalDate() }.sorted()
436:         return Summary(
437:             createdAt = doc.createdAt,
438:             transactions = doc.transactions.size,
439:             categories = doc.categories.size,
440:             merchants = doc.merchants.size,
441:             learning = doc.learning.size,
442:             senders = doc.senders.size,
443:             first = dates.firstOrNull(),
444:             last = dates.lastOrNull(),
445:             dropped = doc.dropped.values.sum(),
446:         )
447:     }
448: 
449:     // ── the restore plan ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
450: 
451:     /**
452:      * What restoring [doc] into a ledger already holding [existing] would
453:      * write.
454:      *
455:      * Merging (the default) keeps everything already here and adds only rows
456:      * this ledger does not have. It is the safe mode and it is idempotent:
457:      * planning the same file against the ledger that file produced adds
458:      * nothing.
459:      *
460:      * A category id pointing at a category neither the file nor the ledger
461:      * has is dropped to null rather than failing the row: a transaction
462:      * filed under a category that does not exist is a worse outcome than an
463:      * uncategorised one, and the user can re-file that in a tap.
464:      */
465:     fun plan(doc: Document, existing: Existing = Existing(),
466:              replace: Boolean = false): Plan {
467:         // Replacing empties the ledger first, so nothing in it now can
468:         // collide with what the file carries.
469:         val base = if (replace) Existing() else existing
470: 
471:         val added = LinkedHashMap<String, Int>()
472:         val skipped = LinkedHashMap<String, Int>()
473:         SECTIONS.keys.forEach { skipped[it] = doc.dropped[it] ?: 0 }
474:         fun skip(key: String) { skipped[key] = (skipped[key] ?: 0) + 1 }
475: 
476:         val categoryIds = base.categoryIds.toMutableSet()
477:         val categoryNames = base.categoryNames.toMutableSet()
478:         val categories = doc.categories.filter { c ->
479:             if (c.id in categoryIds || c.name in categoryNames) {
480:                 skip("categories"); false
481:             } else {
482:                 categoryIds.add(c.id); categoryNames.add(c.name); true
483:             }
484:         }
485:         added["categories"] = categories.size
486: 
487:         // Resolved against the ledger AND this plan, because a row may
488:         // legitimately point at a category the same file is adding.
489:         fun categoryRef(id: String?): String? = if (id != null && id in categoryIds) id else null
490: 
491:         val merchantIds = base.merchantIds.toMutableSet()
492:         val merchantNames = base.merchantNames.toMutableSet()
493:         val merchants = doc.merchants.mapNotNull { m ->
494:             if (m.id in merchantIds || m.canonicalName in merchantNames) {
495:                 skip("merchants"); null
496:             } else {
497:                 merchantIds.add(m.id); merchantNames.add(m.canonicalName)
498:                 m.copy(categoryId = categoryRef(m.categoryId))
499:             }
500:         }
501:         added["merchants"] = merchants.size
502: 
503:         val learningIds = base.learningIds.toMutableSet()
504:         val rawNames = base.learningRawNames.toMutableSet()
505:         val learning = doc.learning.mapNotNull { l ->
506:             if (l.id in learningIds || l.rawName in rawNames) {
507:                 skip("learning"); null
508:             } else {
509:                 learningIds.add(l.id); rawNames.add(l.rawName)
510:                 l.copy(categoryId = categoryRef(l.categoryId))
511:             }
512:         }
513:         added["learning"] = learning.size
514: 
515:         val txIds = base.transactionIds.toMutableSet()
516:         val dedupKeys = base.dedupKeys.toMutableSet()
517:         val transactions = doc.transactions.mapNotNull { t ->
518:             val key = t.dedupKey
519:             if (t.id in txIds || (key != null && key in dedupKeys)) {
520:                 skip("transactions"); null
521:             } else {
522:                 txIds.add(t.id)
523:                 if (key != null) dedupKeys.add(key)
524:                 t.copy(categoryId = categoryRef(t.categoryId))
525:             }
526:         }
527:         added["transactions"] = transactions.size
528: 
529:         val senderIds = base.senderIds.toMutableSet()
530:         val senderNames = base.senderNames.toMutableSet()
531:         val senders = doc.senders.filter { s ->
532:             if (s.id in senderIds || s.sender in senderNames) {
533:                 skip("sms_senders"); false
534:             } else {
535:                 senderIds.add(s.id); senderNames.add(s.sender); true
536:             }
537:         }
538:         added["sms_senders"] = senders.size
539: 
540:         return Plan(
541:             clearFirst = replace,
542:             categories = categories,
543:             merchants = merchants,
544:             learning = learning,
545:             transactions = transactions,
546:             senders = senders,
547:             prefs = doc.prefs,
548:             added = added,
549:             skipped = skipped,
550:         )
551:     }
552: 
553:     /** Read a file and plan its restore in one step. Either the file is
554:      *  acceptable and there is a complete plan, or it is refused and there
555:      *  is none — there is no third answer in which some of it was used. */
556:     fun planFrom(raw: String, existing: Existing = Existing(),
557:                  replace: Boolean = false,
558:                  now: LocalDateTime = LocalDateTime.now()): Restore =
559:         when (val result = read(raw, now)) {
560:             is Read.Rejected -> Restore.Rejected(result.reason)
561:             is Read.Ok -> Restore.Ready(plan(result.doc, existing, replace), result.summary)
562:         }
563: 
564:     // ── one row at a time ────────────────────────────────────────────────
565:     // Nothing below trusts a single value in the file. A row that cannot be
566:     // made sense of returns null and is counted as dropped; none of it ever
567:     // throws, because one bad row must not cost the user the other four
568:     // hundred.
569: 
570:     /** Section key in the file, and what to call it when telling the user
571:      *  that section is damaged. */
572:     private val SECTIONS = linkedMapOf(
573:         "categories" to "categories",
574:         "merchants" to "merchants",
575:         "learning" to "learning",
576:         "transactions" to "transactions",
577:         "sms_senders" to "senders",
578:     )
579: 
580:     private fun id(row: Json.O): String? {
581:         val value = (row.fields["id"] as? Json.S)?.v?.trim() ?: return null
582:         return if (value.isEmpty() || value.length > MAX_ID) null else value
583:     }
584: 
585:     private fun category(row: Json.O): Category? {
586:         val id = id(row) ?: return null
587:         val name = row.fields["name"].asText(MAX_NAME) ?: return null
588:         return Category(
589:             id = id,
590:             name = name,
591:             type = row.fields["type"].asText(20) ?: "expense",
592:             icon = row.fields["icon"].asText(40) ?: "tag",
593:             color = row.fields["color"].asText(20) ?: "#6366f1",
594:             budgetAmount = Parsing.safeAmount(row.fields["budget_amount"].asNumber()),
595:             isArchived = row.fields["is_archived"].asBool(),
596:         )
597:     }
598: 
599:     private fun merchant(row: Json.O): Merchant? {
600:         val id = id(row) ?: return null
601:         val name = row.fields["canonical_name"].asText(MAX_MERCHANT) ?: return null
602:         return Merchant(id, name, row.fields["category_id"].asText(MAX_ID))
603:     }
604: 
605:     private fun learned(row: Json.O): Learned? {
606:         val id = id(row) ?: return null
607:         // raw_name is what a learned mapping is keyed by, and a mapping is
608:         // worthless without the name it maps to.
609:         val rawName = row.fields["raw_name"].asText(MAX_MERCHANT) ?: return null
610:         val merchantName = row.fields["merchant_name"].asText(MAX_MERCHANT) ?: return null
611:         return Learned(
612:             id = id,
613:             rawName = rawName,
614:             merchantName = merchantName,
615:             categoryId = row.fields["category_id"].asText(MAX_ID),
616:             confirmationCount = row.fields["confirmation_count"].asInt(),
617:             correctionCount = row.fields["correction_count"].asInt(),
618:             sampleCount = row.fields["sample_count"].asInt(),
619:             avgAmount = Parsing.safeAmount(row.fields["avg_amount"].asNumber()) ?: 0.0,
620:             amountMin = Parsing.safeAmount(row.fields["amount_min"].asNumber()) ?: 0.0,
621:             amountMax = Parsing.safeAmount(row.fields["amount_max"].asNumber()) ?: 0.0,
622:             hourHistogram = (row.fields["hour_histogram"] as? Json.S)?.v?.take(200) ?: "",
623:             lastSeenAt = time(row.fields["last_seen_at"]),
624:         )
625:     }
626: 
627:     private fun txn(row: Json.O): Txn? {
628:         val id = id(row) ?: return null
629:         // The same gate the SMS parser uses: no zero, no negative, no
630:         // 1e300 grocery bill, no "abc".
631:         val amount = Parsing.safeAmount(row.fields["amount"].asNumber()) ?: return null
632:         val type = row.fields["type"].asText(20)
633:         if (type != "expense" && type != "income") return null
634:         // A date that cannot be read is not a date, and a transaction with
635:         // no date lands at an arbitrary point in the ledger — worse than
636:         // losing the row and saying so.
637:         val occurred = time(row.fields["occurred_at"]) ?: return null
638:         return Txn(
639:             id = id,
640:             amount = amount,
641:             type = type,
642:             occurredAt = occurred,
643:             createdAt = time(row.fields["created_at"]) ?: occurred,
644:             categoryId = row.fields["category_id"].asText(MAX_ID),
645:             rawMerchant = row.fields["raw_merchant"].asText(MAX_TEXT),
646:             merchantName = row.fields["merchant_name"].asText(MAX_MERCHANT),
647:             notes = row.fields["notes"].asText(MAX_TEXT),
648:             referenceNumber = row.fields["reference_number"].asText(MAX_TEXT),
649:             source = row.fields["source"].asText(20) ?: "restore",
650:             confidence = row.fields["confidence"].asIntOrNull()?.coerceIn(0, 100),
651:             status = row.fields["status"].asText(30) ?: "confirmed",
652:             categoryPrompted = row.fields["category_prompted"].asBool(),
653:             dedupKey = row.fields["dedup_key"].asText(MAX_TEXT),
654:             isDeleted = row.fields["is_deleted"].asBool(),
655:         )
656:     }
657: 
658:     private fun sender(row: Json.O, now: LocalDateTime): Sender? {
659:         val id = id(row) ?: return null
660:         val name = row.fields["sender"].asText(MAX_SENDER) ?: return null
661:         // A sender with no dates is still worth its trust setting, so it
662:         // falls back to now rather than being dropped.
663:         val first = time(row.fields["first_seen_at"]) ?: now
664:         return Sender(
665:             id = id,
666:             sender = name,
667:             firstSeenAt = first,
668:             lastSeenAt = time(row.fields["last_seen_at"]) ?: first,
669:             display = row.fields["display"].asText(MAX_SENDER),
670:             kind = row.fields["kind"].asText(20) ?: "other",
671:             entity = row.fields["entity"].asText(MAX_SENDER),
672:             bank = row.fields["bank"].asText(MAX_SENDER),
673:             trust = row.fields["trust"].asText(20) ?: "unknown",
674:             messageCount = row.fields["message_count"].asInt(),
675:             capturedCount = row.fields["captured_count"].asInt(),
676:             confirmedCount = row.fields["confirmed_count"].asInt(),
677:             quarantinedCount = row.fields["quarantined_count"].asInt(),
678:             lastRisk = row.fields["last_risk"].asInt(),
679:         )
680:     }
681: 
682:     /** Preferences carry the same clamps the settings screen applies — a
683:      *  hand-edited backup must not be able to set a 900% auto-save
684:      *  threshold, which would bank every message without asking. */
685:     private fun prefs(row: Json.O): Prefs {
686:         val theme = row.fields["theme"].asText(20)
687:         return Prefs(
688:             currency = row.fields["currency"].asText(MAX_CURRENCY),
689:             theme = if (theme == "system" || theme == "light" || theme == "dark") theme else null,
690:             autoSaveThreshold = row.fields["auto_save_threshold"].asIntOrNull()?.coerceIn(0, 100),
691:             confirmThreshold = row.fields["confirm_threshold"].asIntOrNull()?.coerceIn(0, 100),
692:             highValueAmount = Parsing.safeAmount(row.fields["high_value_amount"].asNumber()),
693:         )
694:     }
695: 
696:     /** ISO-8601 local time, as [write] emits it. A bare date, a space
697:      *  instead of the T and a trailing zone are accepted too: a file the
698:      *  user may have edited by hand is worth being generous with. */
699:     private fun time(value: Json?): LocalDateTime? {
700:         var s = (value as? Json.S)?.v?.trim() ?: return null
701:         if (s.isEmpty()) return null
702:         s = s.replace(' ', 'T')
703:         if (s.endsWith("Z") || s.endsWith("z")) s = s.dropLast(1)
704:         val offset = s.indexOf('+')
705:         if (offset > 10) s = s.substring(0, offset)
706:         return try {
707:             if (s.length == 10) LocalDate.parse(s).atStartOfDay() else LocalDateTime.parse(s)
708:         } catch (e: Exception) {
709:             null
710:         }
711:     }
712: 
713:     private fun Json?.asText(limit: Int): String? {
714:         val s = when (this) {
715:             is Json.S -> v
716:             is Json.N -> raw
717:             else -> return null
718:         }.trim()
719:         return if (s.isEmpty()) null else s.take(limit)
720:     }
721: 
722:     /** Handed to [Parsing.safeAmount], which is the only thing in the app
723:      *  allowed to decide that a number is a usable amount. */
724:     private fun Json?.asNumber(): Any? = when (this) {
725:         is Json.N -> raw
726:         is Json.S -> v
727:         else -> null
728:     }
729: 
730:     private fun Json?.asIntOrNull(): Int? = when (this) {
731:         is Json.N -> raw.toDoubleOrNull()?.takeIf { !it.isNaN() }?.toInt()
732:         is Json.S -> v.trim().toDoubleOrNull()?.takeIf { !it.isNaN() }?.toInt()
733:         is Json.B -> if (v) 1 else 0
734:         else -> null
735:     }
736: 
737:     private fun Json?.asInt(): Int = asIntOrNull() ?: 0
738: 
739:     private fun Json?.asBool(): Boolean = when (this) {
740:         is Json.B -> v
741:         is Json.N -> raw.toDoubleOrNull()?.let { it != 0.0 } ?: false
742:         is Json.S -> v.trim().lowercase() == "true" || v.trim() == "1"
743:         else -> false
744:     }
745: 
746:     private fun text(value: String?): Json = if (value == null) Json.Null else Json.S(value)
747: 
748:     private fun num(value: Double?): Json =
749:         if (value == null) Json.Null else Json.N(value.toString())
750: 
751:     private fun obj(vararg pairs: Pair<String, Json>): Json.O =
752:         Json.O(LinkedHashMap<String, Json>().also { m -> pairs.forEach { m[it.first] = it.second } })
753: 
754:     // ── JSON, by hand ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
755:     /**
756:      * A minimal JSON reader and writer.
757:      *
758:      * :core deliberately has no dependencies — that is what lets the whole
759:      * domain layer be verified on a bare JVM in seconds — and one document
760:      * format is a small price next to a serialisation library in the module
761:      * that has to stay portable.
762:      */
763:     internal sealed class Json {
764:         object Null : Json()
765: 
766:         data class B(val v: Boolean) : Json()
767: 
768:         /** Kept as the text that was in the file: reading a number into a
769:          *  Double and writing it back out is a lossy round trip for anything
770:          *  carrying more digits than a Double holds. */
771:         data class N(val raw: String) : Json()
772: 
773:         data class S(val v: String) : Json()
774: 
775:         data class A(val items: List<Json>) : Json()
776: 
777:         data class O(val fields: Map<String, Json>) : Json()
778: 
779:         class Malformed(message: String) : Exception(message)
780: 
781:         companion object {
782:             // Deep enough for any backup this app writes, shallow enough
783:             // that a file built to nest ten thousand arrays cannot exhaust
784:             // the stack of whatever reads it.
785:             const val MAX_DEPTH = 32
786: 
787:             fun parse(text: String): Json {
788:                 val p = Parser(text)
789:                 val value = p.value(0)
790:                 p.skipSpace()
791:                 if (!p.atEnd()) throw Malformed("trailing text")
792:                 return value
793:             }
794: 
795:             fun write(value: Json): String =
796:                 StringBuilder().also { render(value, it) }.toString()
797: 
798:             private fun render(value: Json, out: StringBuilder) {
799:                 when (value) {
800:                     is Null -> out.append("null")
801:                     is B -> out.append(if (value.v) "true" else "false")
802:                     is N -> out.append(value.raw)
803:                     is S -> escape(value.v, out)
804:                     is A -> {
805:                         out.append('[')
806:                         value.items.forEachIndexed { i, item ->
807:                             if (i > 0) out.append(',')
808:                             render(item, out)
809:                         }
810:                         out.append(']')
811:                     }
812:                     is O -> {
813:                         out.append('{')
814:                         var first = true
815:                         for ((k, v) in value.fields) {
816:                             if (!first) out.append(',')
817:                             first = false
818:                             escape(k, out)
819:                             out.append(':')
820:                             render(v, out)
821:                         }
822:                         out.append('}')
823:                     }
824:                 }
825:             }
826: 
827:             private fun escape(s: String, out: StringBuilder) {
828:                 out.append('"')
829:                 for (c in s) {
830:                     when {
831:                         c == '"' -> out.append("\\\"")
832:                         c == '\\' -> out.append("\\\\")
833:                         c == '\n' -> out.append("\\n")
834:                         c == '\r' -> out.append("\\r")
835:                         c == '\t' -> out.append("\\t")
836:                         c.code < 0x20 -> out.append("\\u").append("%04x".format(c.code))
837:                         // Anything above ASCII is written as itself: the file
838:                         // is UTF-8, and a merchant name in Devanagari should
839:                         // be readable when the user opens it.
840:                         else -> out.append(c)
841:                     }
842:                 }
843:                 out.append('"')
844:             }
845:         }
846: 
847:         private class Parser(private val src: String) {
848:             private var i = 0
849: 
850:             fun atEnd(): Boolean = i >= src.length
851: 
852:             fun skipSpace() {
853:                 while (i < src.length && (src[i] == ' ' || src[i] == '\t' ||
854:                             src[i] == '\n' || src[i] == '\r')) i++
855:             }
856: 
857:             fun value(depth: Int): Json {
858:                 if (depth > MAX_DEPTH) throw Malformed("nested too deeply")
859:                 skipSpace()
860:                 if (atEnd()) throw Malformed("unexpected end of file")
861:                 val c = src[i]
862:                 return when {
863:                     c == '{' -> obj(depth)
864:                     c == '[' -> arr(depth)
865:                     c == '"' -> S(string())
866:                     c == 't' -> literal("true", B(true))
867:                     c == 'f' -> literal("false", B(false))
868:                     c == 'n' -> literal("null", Null)
869:                     c == '-' || c in '0'..'9' -> number()
870:                     else -> throw Malformed("unexpected character")
871:                 }
872:             }
873: 
874:             private fun literal(word: String, value: Json): Json {
875:                 if (!src.startsWith(word, i)) throw Malformed("unexpected word")
876:                 i += word.length
877:                 return value
878:             }
879: 
880:             private fun obj(depth: Int): Json {
881:                 i++
882:                 val fields = LinkedHashMap<String, Json>()
883:                 skipSpace()
884:                 if (!atEnd() && src[i] == '}') { i++; return O(fields) }
885:                 while (true) {
886:                     skipSpace()
887:                     if (atEnd() || src[i] != '"') throw Malformed("expected a field name")
888:                     val key = string()
889:                     skipSpace()
890:                     if (atEnd() || src[i] != ':') throw Malformed("expected ':'")
891:                     i++
892:                     fields[key] = value(depth + 1)
893:                     skipSpace()
894:                     if (atEnd()) throw Malformed("unexpected end of file")
895:                     when (src[i]) {
896:                         ',' -> i++
897:                         '}' -> { i++; return O(fields) }
898:                         else -> throw Malformed("expected ',' or '}'")
899:                     }
900:                 }
901:             }
902: 
903:             private fun arr(depth: Int): Json {
904:                 i++
905:                 val items = ArrayList<Json>()
906:                 skipSpace()
907:                 if (!atEnd() && src[i] == ']') { i++; return A(items) }
908:                 while (true) {
909:                     items.add(value(depth + 1))
910:                     skipSpace()
911:                     if (atEnd()) throw Malformed("unexpected end of file")
912:                     when (src[i]) {
913:                         ',' -> i++
914:                         ']' -> { i++; return A(items) }
915:                         else -> throw Malformed("expected ',' or ']'")
916:                     }
917:                 }
918:             }
919: 
920:             private fun string(): String {
921:                 i++
922:                 val out = StringBuilder()
923:                 while (true) {
924:                     if (atEnd()) throw Malformed("unterminated text")
925:                     val c = src[i]
926:                     when (c) {
927:                         '"' -> { i++; return out.toString() }
928:                         '\\' -> {
929:                             i++
930:                             if (atEnd()) throw Malformed("unterminated escape")
931:                             when (src[i]) {
932:                                 '"' -> out.append('"')
933:                                 '\\' -> out.append('\\')
934:                                 '/' -> out.append('/')
935:                                 'b' -> out.append('\b')
936:                                 'f' -> out.append('\u000C')
937:                                 'n' -> out.append('\n')
938:                                 'r' -> out.append('\r')
939:                                 't' -> out.append('\t')
940:                                 'u' -> {
941:                                     if (i + 4 >= src.length) throw Malformed("bad unicode escape")
942:                                     val code = src.substring(i + 1, i + 5).toIntOrNull(16)
943:                                         ?: throw Malformed("bad unicode escape")
944:                                     out.append(code.toChar())
945:                                     i += 4
946:                                 }
947:                                 else -> throw Malformed("bad escape")
948:                             }
949:                             i++
950:                         }
951:                         else -> { out.append(c); i++ }
952:                     }
953:                 }
954:             }
955: 
956:             private fun number(): Json {
957:                 val start = i
958:                 if (!atEnd() && src[i] == '-') i++
959:                 while (!atEnd() && src[i] in '0'..'9') i++
960:                 if (!atEnd() && src[i] == '.') {
961:                     i++
962:                     while (!atEnd() && src[i] in '0'..'9') i++
963:                 }
964:                 if (!atEnd() && (src[i] == 'e' || src[i] == 'E')) {
965:                     i++
966:                     if (!atEnd() && (src[i] == '+' || src[i] == '-')) i++
967:                     while (!atEnd() && src[i] in '0'..'9') i++
968:                 }
969:                 val raw = src.substring(start, i)
970:                 if (raw.toDoubleOrNull() == null) throw Malformed("bad number")
971:                 return N(raw)
972:             }
973:         }
974:     }
975: }

FILE core/src/main/kotlin/com/jeevavibeapp/spendwise/core/Senders.kt
1: package com.jeevavibeapp.spendwise.core
2: 
3: import kotlin.math.min
4: 
5: /**
6:  * Sender identity, trust and content-based phishing detection. Pure Kotlin.
7:  *
8:  * The premise is that in India the SENDER SHAPE carries real information.
9:  * TRAI's DLT rails mean a bank cannot legally send a transactional SMS from a
10:  * personal mobile number, so "bank alert from a 10-digit mobile" is a forgery
11:  * or, at best, someone forwarding one. That single structural fact does more
12:  * work than any amount of content analysis.
13:  */
14: object Senders {
15: 
16:     const val SENDER_MODEL_VERSION = "2026.08.1-kt"
17: 
18:     const val TRUST_TRUSTED = "trusted"
19:     const val TRUST_KNOWN = "known"
20:     const val TRUST_UNKNOWN = "unknown"
21:     const val TRUST_SUSPICIOUS = "suspicious"
22:     const val TRUST_BLOCKED = "blocked"
23: 
24:     const val ACTION_ACCEPT = "accept"
25:     const val ACTION_REVIEW = "review"
26:     const val ACTION_QUARANTINE = "quarantine"
27: 
28:     /** DLT transactional header: two-letter operator prefix, hyphen, then the
29:      *  principal entity. Some circles add a trailing route tag. */
30:     private val DLT = Regex("^([A-Z]{2})-([A-Z0-9]{5,8})(?:-[A-Z])?$")
31:     private val BARE_HEADER = Regex("^([A-Z]{5,8})$")
32:     private val MOBILE = Regex("^(?:\\+?91|0)?([6-9]\\d{9})$")
33:     private val SHORTCODE = Regex("^\\d{3,8}$")
34: 
35:     /** Zero-width and bidi controls that spoofed senders use to defeat exact
36:      *  matching. Written as escapes so the class is reviewable in a diff —
37:      *  the entire point is that these characters are invisible. */
38:     private val SENDER_NOISE = Regex("[\\s\\u200b-\\u200f\\u202a-\\u202e\\u2060\\ufeff]")
39: 
40:     val KNOWN_ENTITIES: Map<String, String> = mapOf(
41:         // Public sector banks
42:         "SBIINB" to "State Bank of India", "SBIUPI" to "State Bank of India",
43:         "SBICRD" to "SBI Card", "SBIPSG" to "State Bank of India",
44:         "PNBSMS" to "Punjab National Bank", "CANBNK" to "Canara Bank",
45:         "UNIONB" to "Union Bank of India", "BOIIND" to "Bank of India",
46:         "BOBTXN" to "Bank of Baroda", "BOBSMS" to "Bank of Baroda",
47:         "IOBCHN" to "Indian Overseas Bank", "CBSSBI" to "State Bank of India",
48:         "INDBNK" to "Indian Bank", "UCOBNK" to "UCO Bank",
49:         // Private sector banks
50:         "HDFCBK" to "HDFC Bank", "HDFCBN" to "HDFC Bank",
51:         "ICICIB" to "ICICI Bank", "ICICIT" to "ICICI Bank",
52:         "AXISBK" to "Axis Bank", "AXISBN" to "Axis Bank",
53:         "KOTAKB" to "Kotak Mahindra Bank", "INDUSB" to "IndusInd Bank",
54:         "YESBNK" to "Yes Bank", "IDFCFB" to "IDFC First Bank",
55:         "RBLBNK" to "RBL Bank", "FEDBNK" to "Federal Bank",
56:         "SIBSMS" to "South Indian Bank", "KVBANK" to "Karur Vysya Bank",
57:         "CITIBK" to "Citibank", "SCBANK" to "Standard Chartered",
58:         "HSBCIN" to "HSBC India", "DBSBNK" to "DBS Bank",
59:         "AUBANK" to "AU Small Finance Bank", "BANDHN" to "Bandhan Bank",
60:         // Payments banks / wallets / UPI apps
61:         "PYTMBK" to "Paytm Payments Bank", "PAYTMB" to "Paytm",
62:         "PHONPE" to "PhonePe", "PHNPAY" to "PhonePe",
63:         "GPAYIN" to "Google Pay", "GOOGPY" to "Google Pay",
64:         "AMZNPY" to "Amazon Pay", "BHIMUP" to "BHIM UPI",
65:         "AIRTLB" to "Airtel Payments Bank", "JIOPAY" to "Jio Payments Bank",
66:         "MOBIKW" to "MobiKwik", "FREECH" to "Freecharge",
67:         // Cards / NBFCs
68:         "ONECRD" to "OneCard", "SLICEI" to "Slice", "BAJFIN" to "Bajaj Finance",
69:         "HDFCCC" to "HDFC Credit Card", "AMEXIN" to "American Express",
70:     )
71: 
72:     /** Canonical registry key. Mobile numbers reduce to their ten national
73:      *  digits, so +919812345678, 09812345678 and 9812345678 are one sender
74:      *  rather than three. The hyphen is deliberately kept: it separates the
75:      *  DLT operator prefix from the entity header, and removing it would
76:      *  destroy the very shape this module classifies on. */
77:     fun normalizeSender(raw: String?): String {
78:         val s = SENDER_NOISE.replace((raw ?: "").trim().uppercase(), "")
79:         return MOBILE.find(s)?.groupValues?.get(1) ?: s
80:     }
81: 
82:     /** Classify by shape alone — no user history involved. */
83:     fun identify(raw: String?): SenderIdentity {
84:         val norm = normalizeSender(raw)
85:         if (norm.isEmpty()) {
86:             // Some OEM stacks lose the originating address on multipart
87:             // messages. Crucially this is NOT attacker-controlled — an
88:             // attacker sends from some number and cannot make the field
89:             // vanish — so absence carries no hostile signal and must not
90:             // downgrade an otherwise-clean message.
91:             return SenderIdentity(raw ?: "", norm, "missing",
92:                 base = 70, reasons = listOf("sender_missing"))
93:         }
94: 
95:         DLT.find(norm)?.let { m ->
96:             val entity = m.groupValues[2]
97:             val bank = KNOWN_ENTITIES[entity]
98:             // Unrecognised but correctly-shaped headers score as
99:             // accept-worthy: India has roughly 1,500 registered entities and
100:             // this table holds about 50, so an unknown header is
101:             // overwhelmingly a real bank we simply do not list. Treating them
102:             // all as suspect floods the review queue with real transactions.
103:             return SenderIdentity(raw ?: "", norm, "dlt", entity, bank,
104:                 if (bank != null) 90 else 75,
105:                 listOf(if (bank != null) "dlt_known_entity" else "dlt_unknown_entity"))
106:         }
107: 
108:         BARE_HEADER.find(norm)?.let { m ->
109:             val entity = m.groupValues[1]
110:             val bank = KNOWN_ENTITIES[entity]
111:             return SenderIdentity(raw ?: "", norm, "header", entity, bank,
112:                 if (bank != null) 75 else 55,
113:                 listOf(if (bank != null) "header_known_entity" else "header_unknown_entity"))
114:         }
115: 
116:         if (MOBILE.matches(norm) || Regex("^[6-9]\\d{9}$").matches(norm)) {
117:             // The strongest structural signal available. DLT forbids banks
118:             // from sending transactional SMS from a personal number.
119:             return SenderIdentity(raw ?: "", norm, "mobile",
120:                 base = 10, reasons = listOf("personal_mobile_number"))
121:         }
122: 
123:         if (SHORTCODE.matches(norm)) {
124:             return SenderIdentity(raw ?: "", norm, "shortcode",
125:                 base = 30, reasons = listOf("numeric_shortcode"))
126:         }
127: 
128:         return SenderIdentity(raw ?: "", norm, "other",
129:             base = 35, reasons = listOf("unrecognised_sender_format"))
130:     }
131: 
132:     /** Content indicators. Weights are additive, capped at 100, and chosen so
133:      *  no single indicator can quarantine a message on its own: it takes one
134:      *  severe signal plus a weak sender, or two independent signals. */
135:     private val INDICATORS: List<Triple<String, Regex, Int>> = listOf(
136:         // A genuine bank alert DOES say "Not you? Call 18002586161" — a
137:         // toll-free line. The fraud says "call 9812345678". The discriminator
138:         // is the number FORM, not the instruction to call. The lookarounds
139:         // matter: without them the toll-free 18002586161 matches [6-9]\d{9}
140:         // on the substring 8002586161, and every real HDFC alert scores 95.
141:         Triple("callback_mobile_number", Regex(
142:             "\\b(?:call|contact|dial|whatsapp|sms)\\b[^.\\n]{0,40}?" +
143:             "(?<!\\d)(?:\\+?91[\\-\\s]?)?[6-9]\\d{9}(?!\\d)", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE), 45),
144:         // URL shorteners: the defining feature of SMS phishing — they hide
145:         // the destination inside a 160-character limit.
146:         Triple("url_shortener", Regex(
147:             "\\b(?:bit\\.ly|tinyurl|t\\.co|goo\\.gl|rb\\.gy|cutt\\.ly|is\\.gd|" +
148:             "ow\\.ly|shorturl|tiny\\.cc|rebrand\\.ly)\\b", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE), 40),
149:         Triple("apk_download", Regex(
150:             "\\.apk\\b|download.{0,20}app.{0,20}http", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE), 60),
151:         Triple("credential_request", Regex(
152:             "\\b(?:enter|share|send|confirm|update|verify)\\b[^.\\n]{0,30}" +
153:             "\\b(?:otp|pin|cvv|password|mpin|upi\\s*pin|card\\s*(?:no|number)|" +
154:             "aadha?ar|pan\\s*card|kyc)\\b", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE), 55),
155:         Triple("account_block_threat", Regex(
156:             "\\b(?:account|a/?c|card|sim|kyc)\\b[^.\\n]{0,30}" +
157:             "\\b(?:will\\s+be\\s+)?(?:block|suspend|deactivat|freez|expir|clos)\\w*",
158:             RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE), 40),
159:         Triple("urgency_pressure", Regex(
160:             "\\b(?:immediately|within\\s+\\d+\\s*(?:hour|hrs|minute|min)|" +
161:             "urgent(?:ly)?|last\\s+chance|act\\s+now|expires\\s+today)\\b",
162:             RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE), 20),
163:         // "Not you? Reverse it" — the hook that makes a victim call. Weighted
164:         // LOW on purpose: HDFC, ICICI and SBI all ship "Not you? Call
165:         // <toll-free>" in genuine alerts, so it is only meaningful alongside a
166:         // mobile callback. Weighted as a strong signal it quarantined every
167:         // real HDFC message in testing.
168:         Triple("reversal_bait", Regex(
169:             "\\b(?:if\\s+)?not\\s+(?:you|done\\s+by\\s+you)\\b|" +
170:             "\\breverse\\s+(?:this|the)?\\s*(?:transaction|txn|payment)\\b|" +
171:             "\\bto\\s+cancel\\b[^.\\n]{0,30}\\bcall\\b", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE), 15),
172:         Triple("prize_or_refund_lure", Regex(
173:             "\\b(?:you\\s+have\\s+won|lucky\\s+winner|cash\\s*prize|lottery|" +
174:             "claim\\s+your\\s+(?:refund|reward|cashback)|congratulations)\\b",
175:             RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE), 45),
176:         // A bare link is mild alone — real bank messages link to statements —
177:         // but it compounds with everything above.
178:         Triple("contains_link", Regex("https?://|\\bwww\\.", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE), 12),
179:         Triple("suspicious_domain", Regex(
180:             "https?://[^\\s]*\\.(?:xyz|top|club|online|site|icu|buzz|link|" +
181:             "tk|ml|ga|cf|gq|ru|cn)\\b", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE), 45),
182:         // An alert never asks you to send money out.
183:         Triple("payment_request", Regex(
184:             "\\b(?:pay|send|transfer|deposit)\\b[^.\\n]{0,25}" +
185:             "(?:rs\\.?|inr|₹)\\s*[\\d,]+[^.\\n]{0,25}" +
186:             "\\b(?:to\\s+(?:this|the\\s+following)|immediately|now)\\b",
187:             RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE), 40),
188:     )
189: 
190:     /** A message claiming to be from a bank while arriving from a personal
191:      *  mobile is a compound indicator: neither half is conclusive alone. */
192:     private val BANK_CLAIM = Regex(
193:         "\\b(?:bank|a/?c|account|upi|atm|debit\\s*card|credit\\s*card|netbanking)\\b",
194:         RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
195: 
196:     fun phishingIndicators(body: String?): Pair<Int, List<String>> {
197:         val text = body ?: ""
198:         var risk = 0
199:         val found = mutableListOf<String>()
200:         for ((name, pattern, weight) in INDICATORS) {
201:             if (pattern.containsMatchIn(text)) { found += name; risk += weight }
202:         }
203:         return min(risk, 100) to found
204:     }
205: 
206:     /** Combine sender shape, learned trust and content risk into one verdict.
207:      *
208:      * A user decision always wins over the heuristics. They can see things
209:      * the patterns cannot, and overriding them makes the app untrustworthy. */
210:     fun assess(sender: String?, body: String?, registry: SenderRegistry? = null): RiskAssessment {
211:         val ident = identify(sender)
212:         var (risk, indicators) = phishingIndicators(body)
213:         val mutableIndicators = indicators.toMutableList()
214:         val reasons = ident.reasons.toMutableList()
215: 
216:         if (ident.kind == "mobile" && BANK_CLAIM.containsMatchIn(body ?: "")) {
217:             risk = min(100, risk + 35)
218:             mutableIndicators += "bank_claim_from_mobile"
219:         }
220: 
221:         val storedTrust = registry?.trust
222:         val confirmed = registry?.confirmedCount ?: 0
223: 
224:         // 1. Explicit user decisions are final.
225:         if (storedTrust == TRUST_BLOCKED) {
226:             return RiskAssessment(ident, risk, mutableIndicators, TRUST_BLOCKED,
227:                 ACTION_QUARANTINE, -100, reasons + "user_blocked_sender")
228:         }
229:         if (storedTrust == TRUST_TRUSTED) {
230:             // Still quarantine on a severe signal: a trusted header can be
231:             // spoofed by an SMS gateway, and "trusted once" must not become a
232:             // blanket bypass of phishing detection.
233:             if (risk >= 85) {
234:                 return RiskAssessment(ident, risk, mutableIndicators, TRUST_TRUSTED,
235:                     ACTION_QUARANTINE, -60, reasons + "trusted_sender_severe_content_risk")
236:             }
237:             return RiskAssessment(ident, risk, mutableIndicators, TRUST_TRUSTED,
238:                 if (risk >= 45) ACTION_REVIEW else ACTION_ACCEPT,
239:                 if (risk >= 45) -20 else 0, reasons + "user_trusted_sender")
240:         }
241: 
242:         // 2. Structural prior plus observed history.
243:         val score = ident.base + min(confirmed * 5, 25)
244:         if (confirmed >= 3) reasons += "history_confirmed_transactions"
245: 
246:         // 3. Content risk pulls effective trust down.
247:         val effective = score - risk
248: 
249:         return when {
250:             risk >= 70 || (risk >= 50 && ident.base < 60) -> RiskAssessment(
251:                 ident, risk, mutableIndicators, TRUST_SUSPICIOUS, ACTION_QUARANTINE,
252:                 -100, reasons + "high_content_risk")
253:             effective >= 70 -> RiskAssessment(
254:                 ident, risk, mutableIndicators,
255:                 if (ident.bank != null) TRUST_KNOWN else TRUST_UNKNOWN,
256:                 ACTION_ACCEPT, 0, reasons)
257:             effective >= 30 -> RiskAssessment(
258:                 ident, risk, mutableIndicators, TRUST_UNKNOWN, ACTION_REVIEW,
259:                 -25, reasons + "unverified_sender_needs_review")
260:             else -> RiskAssessment(
261:                 ident, risk, mutableIndicators, TRUST_SUSPICIOUS, ACTION_QUARANTINE,
262:                 -100, reasons + "low_sender_trust")
263:         }
264:     }
265: 
266:     /** One line the user can act on, shown beside a held message. */
267:     fun explain(a: RiskAssessment): String = when {
268:         "user_blocked_sender" in a.reasons -> "You blocked this sender."
269:         a.indicators.contains("credential_request") ->
270:             "This message asks for an OTP, PIN or card detail. A bank never does."
271:         a.indicators.contains("apk_download") -> "This message links to an app download."
272:         a.indicators.contains("url_shortener") ->
273:             "This message hides its link behind a shortener."
274:         a.indicators.contains("bank_claim_from_mobile") ->
275:             "This claims to be from a bank but came from a personal mobile number."
276:         a.indicators.contains("account_block_threat") ->
277:             "This threatens to block your account to make you act quickly."
278:         a.indicators.contains("prize_or_refund_lure") ->
279:             "This offers a prize or refund to get you to respond."
280:         a.trust == TRUST_SUSPICIOUS -> "We could not verify this sender."
281:         a.action == ACTION_REVIEW -> "We have not seen this sender before."
282:         else -> "Looks fine."
283:     }
284: }
285: 
286: data class SenderIdentity(
287:     val raw: String,
288:     val normalized: String,
289:     val kind: String,                 // dlt | header | mobile | shortcode | missing | other
290:     val entity: String? = null,
291:     val bank: String? = null,
292:     val base: Int = 0,                // 0..100 prior trust from shape alone
293:     val reasons: List<String> = emptyList(),
294: )
295: 
296: /** What the app has stored about this sender, or null if never seen. */
297: data class SenderRegistry(
298:     val trust: String? = null,
299:     val confirmedCount: Int = 0,
300:     val quarantinedCount: Int = 0,
301: )
302: 
303: data class RiskAssessment(
304:     val sender: SenderIdentity,
305:     val risk: Int,                    // 0..100 content risk
306:     val indicators: List<String>,
307:     val trust: String,
308:     val action: String,               // accept | review | quarantine
309:     val confidenceDelta: Int,         // added to merchant confidence (<= 0)
310:     val reasons: List<String>,
311: )
312: 
313: /**
314:  * The sender registry as a screen shows and edits it.
315:  *
316:  * Split from [Senders] because nothing here judges a message. It orders and
317:  * labels rows the app has already stored, and decides what a tap on "Block"
318:  * or "Always trust" writes back — which is the verdict [Senders.assess] then
319:  * honours over every heuristic it owns. Without this the registry is
320:  * write-only: the app records who keeps messaging and the user can never
321:  * answer.
322:  */
323: object SenderBook {
324: 
325:     /** A registry row, as stored. */
326:     data class Row(
327:         val sender: String,
328:         val display: String? = null,
329:         val kind: String = "other",
330:         val bank: String? = null,
331:         val trust: String = Senders.TRUST_UNKNOWN,
332:         val messageCount: Int = 0,
333:         val quarantinedCount: Int = 0,
334:     )
335: 
336:     /** A control on a sender row, and the trust a tap on it stores. */
337:     data class Choice(val label: String, val trust: String)
338: 
339:     /** One row, finished. Nothing below this is decided on screen. */
340:     data class Entry(
341:         val sender: String,
342:         val title: String,
343:         val detail: String,
344:         val status: String,
345:         val trust: String,
346:         val choices: List<Choice>,
347:     )
348: 
349:     /**
350:      * The three verdicts a person may store.
351:      *
352:      * [Senders.TRUST_KNOWN] and [Senders.TRUST_SUSPICIOUS] are conclusions the
353:      * heuristics reached, and writing one through this path would put a
354:      * machine verdict where [Senders.assess] looks for a human one — which it
355:      * treats as final and refuses to revise.
356:      */
357:     fun isUserDecision(trust: String?): Boolean =
358:         trust == Senders.TRUST_TRUSTED ||
359:         trust == Senders.TRUST_BLOCKED ||
360:         trust == Senders.TRUST_UNKNOWN
361: 
362:     /** The sender's shape in words. "personal mobile" is the one that earns
363:      *  its place: a bank cannot legally send a transactional SMS from one, so
364:      *  naming the shape lets the user see the forgery for themselves rather
365:      *  than taking the app's word for it. */
366:     fun kindLabel(kind: String): String = when (kind) {
367:         "dlt" -> "bank header"
368:         "header" -> "short header"
369:         "mobile" -> "personal mobile"
370:         "shortcode" -> "short number"
371:         "missing" -> "no sender"
372:         else -> "unrecognised"
373:     }
374: 
375:     fun statusLabel(trust: String?): String = when (trust) {
376:         Senders.TRUST_TRUSTED -> "Trusted"
377:         Senders.TRUST_BLOCKED -> "Blocked"
378:         Senders.TRUST_KNOWN -> "Recognised"
379:         Senders.TRUST_SUSPICIOUS -> "Looks unsafe"
380:         else -> "Unverified"
381:     }
382: 
383:     /**
384:      * What a row offers.
385:      *
386:      * A blocked sender offers only "Unblock". One tap turning the sender a
387:      * user distrusts most into the one that waves messages through is too
388:      * much authority for a mis-tap on a scrolling list, so that journey is
389:      * deliberately two taps by way of the unjudged state.
390:      */
391:     fun choices(trust: String?): List<Choice> = when (trust) {
392:         Senders.TRUST_BLOCKED -> listOf(
393:             Choice("Unblock", Senders.TRUST_UNKNOWN))
394:         Senders.TRUST_TRUSTED -> listOf(
395:             Choice("Stop trusting", Senders.TRUST_UNKNOWN),
396:             Choice("Block", Senders.TRUST_BLOCKED))
397:         else -> listOf(
398:             Choice("Always trust", Senders.TRUST_TRUSTED),
399:             Choice("Block", Senders.TRUST_BLOCKED))
400:     }
401: 
402:     /**
403:      * Blocked first, then whoever messages most.
404:      *
405:      * Blocked at the top because it is the decision a user returns to undo,
406:      * and the only one with no other route back: a blocked sender is held
407:      * silently from then on, so nothing else in the app will ever mention it
408:      * again. The name breaks ties so two senders on the same count cannot
409:      * swap places between redraws.
410:      */
411:     fun list(rows: List<Row>): List<Entry> = rows
412:         .filter { it.sender.isNotEmpty() }
413:         .sortedWith(
414:             compareByDescending<Row> { it.trust == Senders.TRUST_BLOCKED }
415:                 .thenByDescending { it.messageCount }
416:                 .thenBy { it.sender })
417:         .map { row ->
418:             val title = row.bank?.trim()?.ifEmpty { null }
419:                 ?: row.display?.trim()?.ifEmpty { null }
420:                 ?: row.sender
421:             Entry(row.sender, title, detail(row, title),
422:                 statusLabel(row.trust), row.trust, choices(row.trust))
423:         }
424: 
425:     private fun detail(row: Row, title: String): String {
426:         val parts = mutableListOf<String>()
427:         // The raw key is worth a line only when the title is not already it.
428:         // "HDFCBK · HDFCBK · 4 messages" reads as a rendering fault.
429:         if (title != row.sender) parts += row.sender
430:         parts += kindLabel(row.kind)
431:         parts += if (row.messageCount == 1) "1 message" else "${row.messageCount} messages"
432:         if (row.quarantinedCount > 0) parts += "${row.quarantinedCount} held"
433:         return parts.joinToString(" · ")
434:     }
435: }

FILE android/nativeapp/src/main/java/com/jeevavibeapp/spendwise/sms/SmsIntake.kt
1: package com.jeevavibeapp.spendwise.sms
2: 
3: import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
4: import android.content.Context
5: import android.content.Intent
6: import android.net.Uri
7: import android.telephony.SmsMessage
8: import android.util.Log
9: import com.jeevavibeapp.spendwise.data.AppGraph
10: import com.jeevavibeapp.spendwise.data.Repo
11: import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
12: import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
13: import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
14: import java.time.LocalDateTime
15: 
16: /**
17:  * Live capture and launch-time catch-up.
18:  *
19:  * The old version POSTed each message to a web server running inside the same
20:  * app, and when that server was not up — which was often, because the app was
21:  * closed — it wrote the message to a queue file to be replayed later. Two
22:  * moving parts and a durable queue, to move data between two objects in one
23:  * process. This writes to the database.
24:  */
25: class SmsIntakeReceiver : BroadcastReceiver() {
26: 
27:     override fun onReceive(context: Context, intent: Intent) {
28:         if (intent.action != "android.provider.Telephony.SMS_RECEIVED") return
29:         val bundle = intent.extras ?: return
30: 
31:         @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
32:         val pdus = bundle.get("pdus") as? Array<*> ?: return
33:         val format = bundle.getString("format")
34: 
35:         // Multipart messages arrive as several PDUs and a bank alert is
36:         // routinely longer than one, so the parts are joined before parsing.
37:         val body = StringBuilder()
38:         var sender: String? = null
39:         for (pdu in pdus) {
40:             val sms = try {
41:                 if (format != null) SmsMessage.createFromPdu(pdu as ByteArray, format)
42:                 else @Suppress("DEPRECATION") SmsMessage.createFromPdu(pdu as ByteArray)
43:             } catch (t: Throwable) { continue }   // malformed PDU on some OEM stacks
44:             sms?.messageBody?.let { body.append(it) }
45:             if (sender == null) sender = sms?.originatingAddress
46:         }
47:         if (body.isEmpty()) return
48: 
49:         val pending = goAsync()
50:         val app = context.applicationContext
51:         CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.IO).launch {
52:             try {
53:                 AppGraph.repo(app).ingestSms(sender, body.toString())
54:             } catch (t: Throwable) {
55:                 Log.e("SpendWiseSms", "ingest failed", t)
56:             } finally {
57:                 pending.finish()
58:             }
59:         }
60:     }
61: }
62: 
63: /**
64:  * Rescans the inbox on launch.
65:  *
66:  * A manifest receiver does not fire on MIUI, ColorOS or EMUI without
67:  * Autostart, and never fires at all while the process is dead — so without
68:  * this, auto-capture silently does nothing on a large share of Indian
69:  * phones.
70:  *
71:  * Rescanning is safe AND cheap: every message is offered as history rather
72:  * than as an arrival, so anything already banked or already held is
73:  * recognised by one indexed lookup and skipped before the expensive part —
74:  * merchant resolution retrains the categoriser over the whole ledger, and
75:  * paying that once per known message on every launch is a stall the user
76:  * would feel.
77:  */
78: class InboxScanner(private val context: Context, private val repo: Repo) {
79: 
80:     companion object {
81:         private const val MAX_MESSAGES = 500
82:         private const val FIRST_RUN_LOOKBACK_DAYS = 90L
83:     }
84: 
85:     suspend fun scan(): Int {
86:         val since = System.currentTimeMillis() -
87:             FIRST_RUN_LOOKBACK_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
88:         var seen = 0
89:         var captured = 0
90:         try {
91:             context.contentResolver.query(
92:                 Uri.parse("content://sms/inbox"),
93:                 arrayOf("address", "body", "date"),
94:                 "date > ?", arrayOf(since.toString()), "date DESC",
95:             )?.use { cur ->
96:                 val iAddr = cur.getColumnIndex("address")
97:                 val iBody = cur.getColumnIndex("body")
98:                 val iDate = cur.getColumnIndex("date")
99:                 while (cur.moveToNext() && seen < MAX_MESSAGES) {
100:                     seen++
101:                     val body = if (iBody >= 0) cur.getString(iBody) else null
102:                     if (body.isNullOrEmpty()) continue
103:                     val addr = if (iAddr >= 0) cur.getString(iAddr) else null
104:                     // When the bank did not put a date in the message, the
105:                     // time it was delivered is the transaction's time — and,
106:                     // because the dedup key falls back to it, the only thing
107:                     // that keeps that key the same on the next rescan.
108:                     // Reading the clock instead would file the same purchase
109:                     // again every day the app was opened.
110:                     val at = if (iDate >= 0) cur.getLong(iDate) else 0L
111:                     val result = repo.ingestSms(
112:                         sender = addr,
113:                         body = body,
114:                         // History being walked past again, not a new arrival.
115:                         live = false,
116:                         receivedAt = if (at > 0) Repo.localTime(at) else LocalDateTime.now(),
117:                     )
118:                     if (result is Repo.Ingest.Captured) captured++
119:                 }
120:             }
121:         } catch (t: Throwable) {
122:             // Missing READ_SMS, an OEM-restricted provider, or a malformed
123:             // row. A scan failure must never take the app down with it.
124:             Log.w("SpendWiseSms", "inbox scan skipped", t)
125:         }
126:         return captured
127:     }
128: }
