71: /**
72:  * The whole app.
73:  *
74:  * What is NOT here is the point: no embedded interpreter, no WSGI server, no
75:  * loopback socket, no readiness poll, no "starting your money engine" splash.
76:  * onCreate opens a database and sets content. Every failure this replaced —
77:  * "the app engine didn't respond", "Address already in use", a twenty-second
78:  * startup timeout — was a property of the architecture, not a bug in it.
79:  *
80:  * Navigation is four tabs over one back stack of full-page destinations,
81:  * held in composition rather than delegated to a navigation library. With
82:  * five pages and no deep links, a graph would be more machinery than the
83:  * thing it routes.
84:  */
85: class NativeMainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
86: 
87:     private val query = MutableStateFlow("")
88: 
89:     /** Read off the manifest and the permission state rather than assumed,
90:      *  and re-read in onResume because both can change while the app is in
91:      *  the background. */
92:     private val smsAccess = MutableStateFlow(SmsAccess.UNAVAILABLE)
93: 
94:     private val smsPermission = registerForActivityResult(
95:         ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions()
96:     ) { granted ->
97:         smsAccess.value = readSmsAccess()
98:         // Only over a ledger that opened: the scanner would otherwise start
99:         // a recovery on the main thread, or throw from inside this callback.
100:         if (granted.values.any { it } && ledger.value is AppGraph.Ledger.Ready) {
101:             // Catch up on anything that arrived while the app was closed. On
102:             // IO because the scan walks up to 500 inbox rows through the SMS
103:             // provider's cursor, which is SQLite in another process, and its
104:             // window fills block whichever thread asks.
105:             lifecycleScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
106:                 AppGraph.inboxScanner(this@NativeMainActivity).scan()
107:             }
108:         }
109:     }
110: 
111:     /** Null while the open runs, which can take seconds when it recovers,
112:      *  so nothing on screen can reach the ledger before it exists. */
113:     private val ledger = MutableStateFlow<AppGraph.Ledger?>(null)
114: 
115:     /** Cleared here the moment it is acknowledged, so the dialog does not
116:      *  wait on the file being deleted behind it. */
117:     private val notice = MutableStateFlow<AppGraph.Notice?>(null)
118: 
119:     /** So a second tap on "Try again" cannot queue another run behind the
120:      *  first, each of which may copy the whole ledger. */
121:     private val retrying = MutableStateFlow(false)
122: 
123:     override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
124:         super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
125:         smsAccess.value = readSmsAccess()
126:         setContent { Root() }
127:         // Off the main thread: the check, a weekly snapshot or a restore can
128:         // each outlast a frame, and a restore can outlast the ANR timeout.
129:         lifecycleScope.launch {
130:             arrive(withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { AppGraph.open(this@NativeMainActivity) })
131:         }
132:     }
133: 
134:     override fun onResume() {
135:         super.onResume()
136:         smsAccess.value = readSmsAccess()
137:     }
138: 
139:     private fun arrive(result: AppGraph.Ledger) {
140:         ledger.value = result
141:         if (result !is AppGraph.Ledger.Ready) return
142:         val repo = result.repo
143: 
144:         // Engine.SEED_MERCHANTS files its built-in merchants under category
145:         // NAMES, and resolveMerchant turns a name into an id by looking in
146:         // this table — so with no rows here every captured transaction lands
147:         // uncategorised and the budgets screen has nothing to show.
148:         lifecycleScope.launch { repo.ensureDefaultCategories() }
149: 
150:         // Only when the permission is already held. The introduction explains
151:         // what is read before asking, and a request fired from onCreate would
152:         // put the system dialog in front of that screen — which is the one
153:         // thing the screen exists to prevent.
154:         if (smsAccess.value == SmsAccess.GRANTED) {
155:             lifecycleScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) {
156:                 AppGraph.inboxScanner(this@NativeMainActivity).scan()
157:             }
158:         }
159: 
160:         lifecycleScope.launch {
161:             notice.value = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { AppGraph.notice(this@NativeMainActivity) }
162:         }
163:     }
164: 
165:     private fun retry() {
166:         if (retrying.value) return
167:         retrying.value = true
168:         lifecycleScope.launch {
169:             val result = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { AppGraph.retry(this@NativeMainActivity) }
170:             retrying.value = false
171:             arrive(result)
172:         }
173:     }
174: 
175:     private fun dismissNotice() {
176:         notice.value = null
177:         lifecycleScope.launch(Dispatchers.IO) { AppGraph.dismissNotice(this@NativeMainActivity) }
178:     }
179: 
180:     @Composable
181:     private fun Root() {
182:         when (val opened = ledger.collectAsState().value) {
183:             null -> SpendWiseTheme { Opening() }
184:             is AppGraph.Ledger.Ready -> Themed(opened.repo)
185:             is AppGraph.Ledger.Unavailable -> SpendWiseTheme {
186:                 val busy by retrying.collectAsState()
187:                 LedgerFailure(opened, retrying = busy, onRetry = ::retry)
188:             }
189:         }
190:     }
191: 
192:     @Composable
193:     private fun Themed(repo: Repo) {
194:         val stored by remember(repo) { repo.settings() }.collectAsState(initial = null)
195:         val settings = stored
196:         val untold by notice.collectAsState()
197:         val access by smsAccess.collectAsState()
198: 
199:         SpendWiseTheme(
200:             dark = when (settings?.theme) {
201:                 THEME_LIGHT -> false
202:                 THEME_DARK -> true
203:                 else -> isSystemInDarkTheme()
204:             }
205:         ) {
206:             // Room has not answered yet. One empty frame is better than
207:             // flashing the introduction at someone who finished it months
208:             // ago, which is what rendering the default would do.
209:             if (settings == null) Surface(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {}
210:             else App(repo, settings)
211: 
212:             untold?.let {
213:                 RecoveryNotice(it, rescans = access == SmsAccess.GRANTED,
214:                     onDismiss = ::dismissNotice)
215:             }
216:         }
217:     }
218: 
219:     @OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
220:     @Composable
221:     private fun App(repo: Repo, settings: SettingsEntity) {
222:         var tab by remember { mutableStateOf(Tab.HOME) }
223:         var stack by remember { mutableStateOf(emptyList<Page>()) }
224:         var sheet by remember { mutableStateOf<Sheet?>(null) }
225:         var reportMonth by remember { mutableStateOf(YearMonth.now()) }
226:         var flash by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
227:         var restoreOffer by remember { mutableStateOf<Backup.Read.Ok?>(null) }
228:         var editingCategories by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
229:         // The name Page.MERCHANT shows. Beside the stack rather than in it,
230:         // because pages are an enum and only one merchant page is ever open.
231:         var merchant by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
232: 
233:         val transactions by remember(repo) { repo.stream() }.collectAsState(initial = emptyList())
234:         val held by remember(repo) { repo.heldMessages() }.collectAsState(initial = emptyList())
235:         // Bank messages the parser could not read, as masked shapes. Without
236:         // this read, a bank whose format is unsupported shows "0 captured"
237:         // and nothing else.
238:         val unreadable by remember(repo) { repo.unreadable() }
239:             .collectAsState(initial = emptyList())
240:         // The Fraud protection card promises that a charge at or above the
241:         // high-value amount raises an alert. Repo.raiseFraudAlerts writes
242:         // them as transactions are filed; this is the read that finally puts
243:         // them in front of the person who asked to be told.
244:         val alerts by remember(repo) { repo.fraudAlerts() }.collectAsState(initial = emptyList())
245:         // Who has been messaging, so a sender that keeps being quarantined
246:         // can be trusted once and one that keeps bothering the user can be
247:         // blocked. Without this read the register is write-only.
248:         val senderRows by remember(repo) { repo.senders() }.collectAsState(initial = emptyList())
249:         val categories by remember(repo) { repo.categories() }.collectAsState(initial = emptyList())
250:         // Deleted categories as well: what is already filed under one keeps
251:         // its name on the report, the merchant page and its own edit sheet,
252:         // which is what the delete confirmation promises. Nothing new is
253:         // offered from this list.
254:         val everyCategory by remember(repo) { repo.everyCategory() }
255:             .collectAsState(initial = emptyList())
256:         val q by query.collectAsState()
257:         val access by smsAccess.collectAsState()
258:         // A count, not the queue: the badge is on screen constantly, and
259:         // building the grouped queue retrains the categoriser.
260:         val waiting by remember(repo) { repo.reviewCount() }.collectAsState(initial = 0)
261: 
262:         // A key wherever it is used, not just an argument: the app can be
263:         // left open across midnight, and a cached figure computed against
264:         // yesterday would quietly stay on screen.
265:         val today = LocalDate.now()
266:         val page = stack.lastOrNull()
267: 
268:         // Aggregates come from the same :core functions the JVM tests cover,
269:         // so what is on screen is what was verified.
270:         val ledger = remember(transactions) {
271:             transactions.map {
272:                 Tx(it.id, it.amount, it.type, Repo.localTime(it.occurredAt),
273:                    it.merchantName ?: it.rawMerchant, it.categoryId)
274:             }
275:         }
276:         val recurring = remember(ledger, today) { Analytics.detectRecurring(ledger, today) }
277:         val flow = remember(ledger) { Analytics.moneyFlow(ledger) }
278:         val insights = remember(ledger, recurring, today) { headlines(ledger, recurring, today) }
279: 
280:         val expenseBudgets = remember(categories) { categories.toBudgets("expense") }
281:         val incomeBudgets = remember(categories) { categories.toBudgets("income") }
282:         // The lookup, not a lambda over it: :core takes the naming function
283:         // by value, and a fresh closure on every recomposition would be a new
284:         // remember key each time and recompute the whole report with it.
285:         val categoryNames = remember(everyCategory) {
286:             everyCategory.associateBy({ c -> c.id }, { c -> c.name })
287:         }
288: 
289:         val sms = remember(access, transactions, held, unreadable) {
290:             smsStatus(access, transactions, held, unreadable)
291:         }
292:         val prefs = remember(settings) {
293:             AppPrefs(settings.currency, settings.theme, settings.autoSaveThreshold,
294:                 settings.confirmThreshold, settings.highValueAmount)
295:         }
296: 
297:         val snackbar = remember { SnackbarHostState() }
298: 
299:         /** The settings screen shows the last result in place; anywhere else
300:          *  it has to be said out loud, or an export that opened a file picker
301:          *  and then finished silently looks like it did nothing. */
302:         val announce: (String) -> Unit = { message ->
303:             flash = message
304:             if (stack.lastOrNull() != Page.SETTINGS) {
305:                 lifecycleScope.launch { snackbar.showSnackbar(message) }
306:             }
307:         }
308: 
309:         // No INTERNET permission: everything in and out of this app
310:         // goes through a picker the user drove, and lands where they chose.
311: 
312:         val saveBackup = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
313:             ActivityResultContracts.CreateDocument("application/json")
314:         ) { uri: Uri? ->
315:             if (uri != null) lifecycleScope.launch {
316:                 announce(
317:                     if (writeDocument(uri, repo.backupText()))
318:                         "Backup saved. Keep a copy somewhere that isn't this phone."
319:                     else "Couldn't write to that file. Try another folder.")
320:             }
321:         }
322: 
323:         val saveCsv = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
324:             ActivityResultContracts.CreateDocument("text/csv")
325:         ) { uri: Uri? ->
326:             if (uri != null) lifecycleScope.launch {
327:                 announce(
328:                     if (writeDocument(uri, repo.csvExport()))
329:                         "CSV saved. It's a report, not a backup — it can't be restored."
330:                     else "Couldn't write to that file. Try another folder.")
331:             }
332:         }
333: 
334:         val openBackup = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
335:             ActivityResultContracts.OpenDocument()
336:         ) { uri: Uri? ->
337:             if (uri != null) lifecycleScope.launch {
338:                 val bytes = readDocument(uri)
339:                 if (bytes == null) {
340:                     announce("Couldn't open that file.")
341:                 } else when (val read = repo.readBackup(bytes)) {
342:                     // The file is refused with a sentence about the file, and
343:                     // nothing at all is written until the user has seen what
344:                     // is in it and said yes.
345:                     is Backup.Read.Rejected -> announce(read.reason)
346:                     is Backup.Read.Ok -> restoreOffer = read
347:                 }
348:             }
349:         }
350: 
351:         val onBackup: () -> Unit = { saveBackup.launch(backupFilename()) }
352:         val onExportCsv: () -> Unit = { saveCsv.launch(csvFilename()) }
353:         // The masked list, through the system share sheet. This used to save
354:         // the held messages' full text to a file under the same "unreadable"
355:         // label — a stand-in from before the parser's misses were stored,
356:         // and raw SMS leaving the app for a purpose the shapes now serve.
357:         val onShareUnreadable: () -> Unit = {
358:             lifecycleScope.launch {
359:                 if (!shareText(UNREADABLE_SUBJECT, repo.unreadableExport())) {
360:                     announce("Couldn't open the share sheet on this phone.")
361:                 }
362:             }
363:         }
364:         val onClearUnreadable: () -> Unit = {
365:             lifecycleScope.launch {
366:                 val n = repo.clearUnreadable()
367:                 announce("Cleared $n from the list. Your ledger is unchanged, and new " +
368:                          "messages SpendWise can't read will still be listed.")
369:             }
370:         }
371:         // Every file type, because a file manager that reports a backup as
372:         // octet-stream would otherwise grey out the only file that works.
373:         val onRestore: () -> Unit = { openBackup.launch(arrayOf("*/*")) }
374:         val onAllowSms: () -> Unit = { requestSms() }
375:         // From the report's merchant rows and from any payment's edit sheet.
376:         // At most one merchant page is on the stack: `merchant` is a single
377:         // name, so a second entry would show the same merchant as the first,
378:         // and Back from it would appear to do nothing.
379:         val openMerchant: (String) -> Unit = { name ->
380:             merchant = name
381:             stack = stack.filterNot { it == Page.MERCHANT } + Page.MERCHANT
382:         }
383:         // From Home, Activity, the add sheet, Settings and Help alike, so
384:         // every route to the page leaves the same back stack: Back from it
385:         // returns to whatever it was opened over.
386:         val onPaste: () -> Unit = {
387:             sheet = null
388:             stack = stack + Page.PASTE
389:         }
390: 
391:         // Back never exits from a page or an inner tab: it unwinds the stack,
392:         // then returns to Home, and only then falls through to the system.
393:         BackHandler(enabled = page != null || tab != Tab.HOME) {
394:             if (stack.isNotEmpty()) stack = stack.dropLast(1) else tab = Tab.HOME
395:         }
396: 
397:         if (!settings.onboardingSeen) {
398:             // No tabs and no top bar: nothing behind them is set up yet.
399:             // Privacy is the one place this can lead, and it gets the same
400:             // working export handlers it has everywhere else. The snackbar
401:             // host is here because those handlers report through it.
402:             Scaffold(snackbarHost = { SnackbarHost(snackbar) }) { pad ->
403:                 Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(pad)) {
404:                     if (page == Page.PRIVACY) {
405:                         PrivacyScreen(onBackup = onBackup, onExportCsv = onExportCsv)
406:                     } else {
407:                         WelcomeScreen(
408:                             sms = sms,
409:                             onAllowSms = onAllowSms,
410:                             onPrivacy = { stack = listOf(Page.PRIVACY) },
411:                             onDone = { lifecycleScope.launch { repo.markOnboardingSeen() } },
412:                         )
413:                     }
414:                 }
415:             }
416:             return
417:         }
418: 
419:         Scaffold(
420:             topBar = {
421:                 TopAppBar(
422:                     title = { Text(page?.title ?: tab.label) },
423:                     // Words, not glyphs. There is no icon set anywhere in
424:                     // this app — the whole design is type and space — and a
425:                     // labelled control is also the one a screen reader can
426:                     // read without a contentDescription to maintain.
427:                     navigationIcon = {
428:                         if (page != null) TextButton(
429:                             onClick = { stack = stack.dropLast(1) },
430:                             modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
431:                         ) { Text("Back") }
432:                     },
433:                     actions = {
434:                         if (page == null) {
435:                             if (tab == Tab.BUDGETS) TextButton(
436:                                 onClick = { editingCategories = true },
437:                                 modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
438:                             ) { Text("Categories") }
439:                             if (tab == Tab.HOME) TextButton(
440:                                 onClick = { stack = stack + Page.REPORT },
441:                                 modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
442:                             ) { Text("Report") }
443:                             // Only when there is something to sort. The
444:                             // ledger is where the "Needs review" rows are
445:                             // read, so it is where the way to settle them all
446:                             // at once belongs.
447:                             if (tab == Tab.ACTIVITY && waiting > 0) TextButton(
448:                                 onClick = { stack = stack + Page.REVIEW },
449:                                 modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
450:                             ) { Text("Review $waiting") }
451:                             TextButton(
452:                                 onClick = { stack = stack + Page.SETTINGS },
453:                                 modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
454:                             ) { Text("Settings") }
455:                         }
456:                     },
457:                 )
458:             },
459:             bottomBar = {
460:                 if (page == null) NavigationBar {
461:                     // An alert on a tab nobody opens has not alerted anyone.
462:                     // The number is in the label because there is no icon set
463:                     // in this app to hang a badge off — and a screen reader
464:                     // reads it either way. It counts BOTH kinds the tab
465:                     // holds: "Alerts (2)" over two flagged charges and five
466:                     // held messages is a worse lie than no number at all.
467:                     val waitingAlerts = alerts.size + held.size
468:                     Tab.entries.forEach { entry ->
469:                         NavigationBarItem(
470:                             selected = tab == entry,
471:                             onClick = { tab = entry },
472:                             // The label is the whole affordance. The dot only
473:                             // gives the selection indicator something to sit
474:                             // behind — an empty icon slot leaves it drawing an
475:                             // empty pill over the label.
476:                             icon = { SelectionDot(selected = tab == entry) },
477:                             label = {
478:                                 Text(
479:                                     if (entry == Tab.ALERTS && waitingAlerts > 0)
480:                                         "${entry.label} ($waitingAlerts)"
481:                                     else entry.label
482:                                 )
483:                             },
484:                         )
485:                     }
486:                 }
487:             },
488:             snackbarHost = { SnackbarHost(snackbar) },
489:             floatingActionButton = {
490:                 if (page == null && (tab == Tab.HOME || tab == Tab.ACTIVITY)) {
491:                     FloatingActionButton(
492:                         onClick = { sheet = Sheet.New },
493:                         modifier = Modifier.semantics {
494:                             contentDescription = "Add a transaction"
495:                         },
496:                     ) { Text("+", style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineLarge) }
497:                 }
498:             },
499:         ) { pad ->
500:             Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(pad)) {
501:                 when (page) {
502:                     null -> when (tab) {
503:                         Tab.HOME -> DashboardScreen(
504:                             balance = flow.income - flow.expense,
505:                             income = flow.income, expense = flow.expense,
506:                             insights = insights, recurring = recurring,
507:                             capture = access.capture,
508:                             onAdd = { sheet = Sheet.New },
509:                             onPaste = onPaste,
510:                         )
511: 
512:                         Tab.ACTIVITY -> {
513:                             // The same four fields the DAO's search covers.
514:                             // Filtered here because the whole ledger is
515:                             // already collected for the aggregates above.
516:                             val matches = remember(transactions, q) { transactions.matching(q) }
517:                             TransactionsScreen(
518:                                 transactions = matches,
519:                                 query = q,
520:                                 onQuery = { query.value = it },
521:                                 onEdit = { tx -> sheet = Sheet.Edit(tx) },
522:                                 onDelete = { id ->
523:                                     lifecycleScope.launch {
524:                                         repo.delete(id)
525:                                         // The row is only soft-deleted, so
526:                                         // undo restores the same transaction
527:                                         // rather than rebuilding a copy.
528:                                         val choice = snackbar.showSnackbar(
529:                                             "Transaction deleted",
530:                                             actionLabel = "Undo",
531:                                             duration = SnackbarDuration.Long)
532:                                         if (choice == SnackbarResult.ActionPerformed) {
533:                                             repo.undelete(id)
534:                                         }
535:                                     }
536:                                 },
537:                                 onConfirm = { id, name ->
538:                                     lifecycleScope.launch {
539:                                         // What the confirmation bought, not
540:                                         // "done": the point of naming a payee
541:                                         // is that the next message from it is
542:                                         // filed without asking.
543:                                         if (repo.confirm(id, name)) {
544:                                             announce("Confirmed. The next one from " +
545:                                                      "${name.trim()} will be filed for you.")
546:                                         }
547:                                     }
548:                                 },
549:                                 capture = access.capture,
550:                                 onAdd = { sheet = Sheet.New },
551:                                 onPaste = onPaste,
552:                             )
553:                         }
554: 
555:                         Tab.BUDGETS -> {
556:                             val month = YearMonth.from(today)
557:                             val status = remember(ledger, expenseBudgets, month, today) {
558:                                 Budgets.budgetStatus(ledger, expenseBudgets, month, today)
559:                             }
560:                             BudgetsScreen(
561:                                 categories = expenseBudgets,
562:                                 status = status,
563:                                 month = month,
564:                                 incomeCategories = incomeBudgets,
565:                                 onSetBudget = { id, amount ->
566:                                     lifecycleScope.launch { repo.setBudget(id, amount) }
567:                                 },
568:                             )
569:                         }
570: 
571:                         Tab.ALERTS -> AlertsScreen(
572:                             alerts = alerts,
573:                             held = held,
574:                             // Ordered and labelled by :core, so the row a
575:                             // finger lands on is the one the JVM checks
576:                             // describe.
577:                             senders = remember(senderRows) {
578:                                 SenderBook.list(senderRows.map {
579:                                     SenderBook.Row(
580:                                         sender = it.sender, display = it.display,
581:                                         kind = it.kind, bank = it.bank, trust = it.trust,
582:                                         messageCount = it.messageCount,
583:                                         quarantinedCount = it.quarantinedCount,
584:                                     )
585:                                 })
586:                             },
587:                             onApprove = { id ->
588:                                 lifecycleScope.launch { announce(describe(repo.approveHeld(id))) }
589:                             },
590:                             onReject = { id ->
591:                                 lifecycleScope.launch {
592:                                     repo.rejectHeld(id)
593:                                     announce("Discarded. It stays on your phone, out of " +
594:                                              "your ledger.")
595:                                 }
596:                             },
597:                             onBlock = { id ->
598:                                 lifecycleScope.launch {
599:                                     val who = repo.rejectHeldAndBlock(id)
600:                                     // The button only exists on a message with a
601:                                     // sender, so null means the block half failed.
602:                                     // Leaving that unsaid lets the user assume it
603:                                     // worked until the sender's next scam is filed.
604:                                     announce(if (who != null)
605:                                         "Discarded, and $who is blocked. Anything else it " +
606:                                         "sends is held without asking."
607:                                     else "Discarded, but the sender couldn't be blocked, " +
608:                                          "so its next message is checked like any other.")
609:                                 }
610:                             },
611:                             onDismiss = { id ->
612:                                 lifecycleScope.launch { repo.dismissAlert(id) }
613:                             },
614:                             onTrust = { sender, trust ->
615:                                 lifecycleScope.launch {
616:                                     if (repo.setSenderTrust(sender, trust)) {
617:                                         announce(when (trust) {
618:                                             Senders.TRUST_BLOCKED ->
619:                                                 "$sender is blocked. Anything else it sends " +
620:                                                 "is held without asking."
621:                                             Senders.TRUST_TRUSTED ->
622:                                                 "$sender is trusted. Its messages are filed " +
623:                                                 "unless one reads like a scam."
624:                                             else ->
625:                                                 "$sender is back to being checked like any " +
626:                                                 "other sender."
627:                                         })
628:                                     } else {
629:                                         // Nothing matched, so the row keeps its old
630:                                         // label. Silence beside it reads as a tap that
631:                                         // did not register, and a user who believes a
632:                                         // sender is blocked stops watching for it.
633:                                         announce("Couldn't change $sender, so nothing was " +
634:                                                  "saved. Its messages are handled as before.")
635:                                     }
636:                                 }
637:                             },
638:                         )
639:                     }
640: 
641:                     // Every figure here is decided in :core. The screen is
642:                     // given finished values rather than a ledger, so nothing
643:                     // on it is a second, untested implementation of
644:                     // arithmetic that already exists.
645:                     Page.REPORT -> {
646:                         // Names and colours only — buildReport reads no
647:                         // budget — so deleted categories are in it: their
648:                         // past spending stays under their own names instead
649:                         // of turning into Uncategorised.
650:                         val all = remember(everyCategory) {
651:                             everyCategory.toBudgets("expense") + everyCategory.toBudgets("income")
652:                         }

654:                             report = remember(ledger, reportMonth, all, today) {
655:                                 Budgets.buildReport(ledger, reportMonth, today, all)
656:                             },
657:                             today = today,
658:                             forecast = remember(ledger, reportMonth, recurring, today) {
659:                                 Insights.forecast(ledger, reportMonth, today, recurring)
660:                             },
661:                             anomalies = remember(ledger, reportMonth) {
662:                                 Insights.anomalies(ledger, reportMonth)
663:                             },
664:                             savings = remember(ledger, reportMonth, recurring, categoryNames) {
665:                                 Insights.savingsOpportunities(
666:                                     ledger, reportMonth, recurring,
667:                                     categoryName = { id ->

669:                                     })

672:                             trends = remember(ledger, categoryNames, today) {

674:                                     categoryNames[id] ?: Budgets.UNCATEGORISED
675:                                 })

689:                     // One merchant, and what the engine has learned about it.
690:                     // The rules are MerchantBook in :core; this branch only

693:                         MerchantScreen(
694:                             name = name,
695:                             ledger = ledger,
696:                             transactions = transactions,
697:                             categories = categories,
698:                             everyCategory = everyCategory,
699:                             today = today,
700:                             filingFor = { keys -> repo.merchantFiling(name, keys) },
701:                             onTransaction = { tx -> sheet = Sheet.Edit(tx) },
702:                             onFileUnder = { categoryId, keys, moveIds ->
703:                                 lifecycleScope.launch {
704:                                     announce(filedUnder(name,
705:                                         repo.fileMerchantUnder(name, keys, categoryId, moveIds)))
706:                                 }
707:                             },
708:                             onForget = { mapping ->
709:                                 lifecycleScope.launch {
710:                                     val gone = repo.forgetMapping(mapping.rawName)
711:                                     if (gone == null) {
712:                                         announce("That was already forgotten. Nothing changed.")
713:                                         return@launch
714:                                     }
715:                                     // Undo rather than an "are you sure": the
716:                                     // row is held here exactly as it was, and
717:                                     // nothing in the ledger was touched.
718:                                     val choice = snackbar.showSnackbar(
719:                                         "Forgot “${mapping.rawName}”. New payments are worked " +
720:                                         "out afresh; past ones are unchanged.",
721:                                         actionLabel = "Undo",
722:                                         duration = SnackbarDuration.Long)
723:                                     if (choice == SnackbarResult.ActionPerformed) {
724:                                         repo.restoreMapping(gone)
725:                                     }
726:                                 }
727:                             },
728:                         )
729:                     }

731:                     // One question per payee, answered for every capture
732:                     // behind it. The grouping and the pre-filled suggestions
733:                     // are Review in :core; nothing on the screen decides
734:                     // anything the JVM checks do not cover.
735:                     Page.REVIEW -> {
736:                         // Collected inside the page rather than beside the
737:                         // ledger above: building the queue retrains the
738:                         // categoriser over every categorised row, which is
739:                         // not work to repeat on each change to the ledger
740:                         // while this screen is closed.
741:                         val queue by remember(repo) { repo.reviewQueue() }
742:                             .collectAsState(initial = emptyList())
743:                         ReviewScreen(
744:                             groups = queue,
745:                             categories = categories,
746:                             onCategorise = { group, categoryId ->
747:                                 lifecycleScope.launch {
748:                                     announce(sorted(
749:                                         repo.categoriseReviewGroup(group, categoryId = categoryId),
750:                                         categoryNames[categoryId]))
751:                                 }
752:                             },
753:                             onCreateCategory = { group, name ->
754:                                 lifecycleScope.launch {
755:                                     announce(sorted(
756:                                         repo.categoriseReviewGroup(group, newCategoryName = name),
757:                                         name.trim()))
758:                                 }
759:                             },
760:                             onDiscard = { group ->
761:                                 lifecycleScope.launch {
762:                                     val n = repo.discardReviewGroup(group)
763:                                     announce("Removed $n " +
764:                                              (if (n == 1) "capture." else "captures.") +
765:                                              " Nothing else was touched.")
766:                                 }
767:                             },
768:                         )
769:                     }

780:                                         autoSaveThreshold = edited.autoSaveThreshold,

783:                                     )

786:                             }

793:                         unreadable = unreadable,

795:                         onClearUnreviewed = {

799:                                          (if (n == 1) "capture." else "captures."))
800:                             }
801:                         },
802:                         onPrivacy = { stack = stack + Page.PRIVACY },
803:                         onHelp = { stack = stack + Page.HELP },

814:                     Page.HELP -> HelpScreen(
815:                         sms = sms,
816:                         onAllowSms = onAllowSms,
817:                         onExportUnreadable = onShareUnreadable,
818:                         onImport = onPaste,
819:                     )
820: 
821:                     // Read, shown, corrected, and only then written. The
822:                     // save runs on the activity's scope rather than the
823:                     // page's, so leaving straight after Save cannot cancel
824:                     // it halfway.
825:                     Page.PASTE -> PasteScreen(
826:                         categories = categories,
827:                         preview = { body -> repo.previewPaste(body) },
828:                         // Only after the preview has said it cannot be read,
829:                         // and only its masked shape: the preview itself writes
830:                         // nothing, and this is the same evidence a captured
831:                         // unreadable message leaves.
832:                         onUnreadable = { body -> repo.recordUnreadable(null, body, pasted = true) },
833:                         banked = { key -> repo.bankedAs(key) },
834:                         onSave = { commit ->
835:                             lifecycleScope.launch { announce(describe(repo.savePaste(commit))) }
836:                         },
837:                         onRestore = { row ->
838:                             lifecycleScope.launch {
839:                                 repo.undelete(row.id)
840:                                 announce("Restored. It's back in your ledger as it was.")
841:                             }
842:                         },
843:                     )
