845:                     // The introduction, reopened deliberately from settings.
846:                     // Done closes the page rather than re-recording something
847:                     // that was recorded the first time.
848:                     Page.WELCOME -> WelcomeScreen(
849:                         sms = sms,
850:                         onAllowSms = onAllowSms,
851:                         onPrivacy = { stack = stack + Page.PRIVACY },
852:                         onDone = { stack = stack.dropLast(1) },
853:                     )
854:                 }
855:             }
856:         }
857: 
858:         val editing = sheet
859:         if (editing != null) {
860:             // The row is carried in the sheet's own state rather than looked
861:             // up by id: a soft delete takes it out of the stream, and a
862:             // lookup would drop the transaction out from under the undo
863:             // panel the moment the user pressed Delete.
864:             val existing = (editing as? Sheet.Edit)?.tx
865:             AddEditSheet(
866:                 categories = remember(everyCategory, existing) {
867:                     everyCategory.filter { Categories.pickable(it.asRow(), existing?.categoryId) }
868:                 },
869:                 resolveMerchant = { typed -> repo.resolveMerchant(typed, null, null).merchantName },
870:                 onSave = { tx -> lifecycleScope.launch { repo.save(tx) } },
871:                 onDismiss = { sheet = null },
872:                 existing = existing,
873:                 onDelete = { tx -> lifecycleScope.launch { repo.delete(tx.id) } },
874:                 onRestore = { tx -> lifecycleScope.launch { repo.undelete(tx.id) } },
875:                 // In every build: in the SMS one this is the way to a message
876:                 // capture missed, which is where the web app's import page was
877:                 // reachable from too.
878:                 onPaste = onPaste,
879:                 // Not over the merchant page, whose every payment is already
880:                 // that merchant's: the link would reopen the page beneath it.
881:                 onMerchant = if (page == Page.MERCHANT) null else { name ->
882:                     sheet = null
883:                     openMerchant(name)
884:                 },
885:             )
886:         }
887: 
888:         if (editingCategories) {
889:             CategoryDialog(
890:                 categories = categories,
891:                 ledger = ledger,
892:                 onCreate = { name, type ->
893:                     lifecycleScope.launch {
894:                         announce(describe(repo.createCategory(name, type), name.trim()))
895:                     }
896:                 },
897:                 onRename = { id, name ->
898:                     lifecycleScope.launch {
899:                         announce(describe(repo.renameCategory(id, name), name.trim()))
900:                     }
901:                 },
902:                 onDelete = { id, notice ->
903:                     lifecycleScope.launch {
904:                         // The count the user agreed to, said back to them —
905:                         // or, if nothing was written, that nothing was.
906:                         announce(if (repo.deleteCategory(id)) notice.done
907:                                  else describe(Repo.CategoryResult.GONE, ""))
908:                     }
909:                 },
910:                 onDismiss = { editingCategories = false },
911:             )
912:         }
913: 
914:         restoreOffer?.let { offer ->
915:             RestoreDialog(
916:                 summary = offer.summary,
917:                 onDismiss = { restoreOffer = null },
918:                 onConfirm = { replace ->
919:                     restoreOffer = null
920:                     lifecycleScope.launch { announce(describe(repo.applyRestore(offer.doc, replace))) }
921:                 },
922:             )
923:         }
924:     }
925: 
926:     // Here rather than in ui/ because each one exists to reach a repository
927:     // call that no screen in that package has a parameter for.
928: 
929:     /**
930:      * Creating, renaming and deleting categories.
931:      *
932:      * Renaming is an edit of the row transactions already point at, never a
933:      * new category plus a move: the id is what the ledger stores, so a rename
934:      * cannot orphan a single transaction.
935:      *
936:      * Deleting asks first, in place of the list rather than in a second
937:      * dialog stacked over it, so Back from the question returns to the list
938:      * instead of closing both.
939:      */
940:     @Composable
941:     private fun CategoryDialog(
942:         categories: List<CategoryEntity>,
943:         ledger: List<Tx>,
944:         onCreate: (name: String, type: String) -> Unit,
945:         onRename: (id: String, name: String) -> Unit,
946:         onDelete: (id: String, notice: Categories.DeleteNotice) -> Unit,
947:         onDismiss: () -> Unit,
948:     ) {
949:         var newName by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
950:         var newType by remember { mutableStateOf("expense") }
951:         var renamingId by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
952:         var renameTo by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
953:         var deletingId by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
954: 
955:         // Looked up in the live list rather than held, so a category that has
956:         // left it meanwhile cannot still be the subject of the question.
957:         val deleting = categories.firstOrNull { it.id == deletingId }
958:         if (deleting != null) {
959:             ConfirmCategoryDelete(
960:                 category = deleting,
961:                 ledger = ledger,
962:                 onConfirm = { notice ->
963:                     onDelete(deleting.id, notice)
964:                     deletingId = null
965:                 },
966:                 onCancel = { deletingId = null },
967:             )
968:             return
969:         }
970: 
971:         AlertDialog(
972:             onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
973:             title = { Text("Categories") },
974:             text = {
975:                 Column(Modifier.verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())) {
976:                     Text("Transactions are filed under these. Renaming or deleting one " +
977:                          "keeps everything already filed under it.",
978:                         style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
979:                         color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
980:                     Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
981: 
982:                     OutlinedTextField(
983:                         value = newName,
984:                         onValueChange = { newName = it },
985:                         label = { Text("New category") },
986:                         singleLine = true,
987:                         modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
988:                     )
989:                     Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
990:                     Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
991:                         FilterChip(
992:                             selected = newType == "expense",
993:                             onClick = { newType = "expense" },
994:                             label = { Text("Expense") },
995:                             modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
996:                         )
997:                         FilterChip(
998:                             selected = newType == "income",
999:                             onClick = { newType = "income" },
1000:                             label = { Text("Income") },
1001:                             modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
1002:                         )
1003:                         Spacer(Modifier.width(4.dp))
1004:                         TextButton(
1005:                             onClick = { onCreate(newName, newType); newName = "" },
1006:                             modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
1007:                         ) { Text("Add") }
1008:                     }
1009: 
1010:                     Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
1011:                     HorizontalDivider(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outline)
1012: 
1013:                     for (c in categories) {
1014:                         if (renamingId == c.id) {
1015:                             Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
1016:                             OutlinedTextField(
1017:                                 value = renameTo,
1018:                                 onValueChange = { renameTo = it },
1019:                                 label = { Text("Rename ${c.name}") },
1020:                                 singleLine = true,
1021:                                 modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
1022:                                     .heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
1023:                             )
1024:                             Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
1025:                                 TextButton(
1026:                                     onClick = { onRename(c.id, renameTo); renamingId = null },
1027:                                     modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
1028:                                 ) { Text("Save") }
1029:                                 TextButton(
1030:                                     onClick = { renamingId = null },
1031:                                     modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
1032:                                 ) { Text("Cancel") }
1033:                             }
1034:                         } else {
1035:                             Row(
1036:                                 verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
1037:                                 modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
1038:                                     .heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
1039:                             ) {
1040:                                 // Name over direction rather than beside it, so
1041:                                 // a long name keeps the width two buttons take.
1042:                                 Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
1043:                                     Text(c.name, style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium)
1044:                                     Text(if (c.type == "income") "INCOME" else "EXPENSE",
1045:                                         style = MicroLabel,
1046:                                         color = if (c.type == "income") Income else Expense)
1047:                                 }
1048:                                 // Each button says which category it acts on: a
1049:                                 // screen reader walking this list otherwise hears
1050:                                 // "Rename, Delete" a dozen times over.
1051:                                 TextButton(
1052:                                     onClick = { renamingId = c.id; renameTo = c.name },
1053:                                     modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget)
1054:                                         .semantics { contentDescription = "Rename ${c.name}" },
1055:                                 ) { Text("Rename") }
1056:                                 // Quiet, because it repeats on every row. The
1057:                                 // warning colour is saved for the question it
1058:                                 // opens, where it means something.
1059:                                 TextButton(
1060:                                     onClick = { renamingId = null; deletingId = c.id },
1061:                                     colors = ButtonDefaults.textButtonColors(
1062:                                         contentColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant),
1063:                                     modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget)
1064:                                         .semantics { contentDescription = "Delete ${c.name}" },
1065:                                 ) { Text("Delete") }
1066:                             }
1067:                         }
1068:                     }
1069:                 }
1070:             },
1071:             confirmButton = {
1072:                 TextButton(
1073:                     onClick = onDismiss,
1074:                     modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
1075:                 ) { Text("Done") }
1076:             },
1077:         )
1078:     }
1079: 
1080:     /**
1081:      * The question before a category is deleted.
1082:      *
1083:      * "Delete" beside a folder of someone's own history reads as losing that
1084:      * history, so the first thing said is what happens to the transactions in
1085:      * it — kept, still filed under it — and only then what changes. Every
1086:      * sentence is :core's, decided and checked there, so the dialog cannot
1087:      * promise something the code does not do.
1088:      */
1089:     @Composable
1090:     private fun ConfirmCategoryDelete(
1091:         category: CategoryEntity,
1092:         ledger: List<Tx>,
1093:         onConfirm: (Categories.DeleteNotice) -> Unit,
1094:         onCancel: () -> Unit,
1095:     ) {
1096:         val notice = remember(category, ledger) {
1097:             Categories.deleteNotice(category.asRow(), ledger)
1098:         }
1099:         AlertDialog(
1100:             onDismissRequest = onCancel,
1101:             title = { Text("Delete ${category.name}?") },
1102:             text = {
1103:                 Column(Modifier.verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())) {
1104:                     Text(notice.kept, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium)
1105:                     Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
1106:                     Text(notice.hidden, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
1107:                         color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
1108:                     // The one thing adding the name back does not undo.
1109:                     notice.budget?.let {
1110:                         Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
1111:                         Text(it, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, color = Expense)
1112:                     }
1113:                     Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))
1114:                     Text(notice.undo, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
1115:                         color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
1116:                 }
1117:             },
1118:             confirmButton = {
1119:                 TextButton(
1120:                     onClick = { onConfirm(notice) },
1121:                     colors = ButtonDefaults.textButtonColors(contentColor = Expense),
1122:                     modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
1123:                 ) { Text("Delete ${category.name}") }
1124:             },
1125:             dismissButton = {
1126:                 TextButton(
1127:                     onClick = onCancel,
1128:                     modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
1129:                 ) { Text("Keep it") }
1130:             },
1131:         )
1132:     }
1133: 
1134:     /**
1135:      * What is in the file, before anything is written.
1136:      *
1137:      * Replace is the only mode that can lose data, so it is a switch the user
1138:      * has to reach for and the button under it changes its word. Nobody
1139:      * should be able to empty their ledger by pressing the default.
1140:      */
1141:     @Composable
1142:     private fun RestoreDialog(
1143:         summary: Backup.Summary,
1144:         onDismiss: () -> Unit,
1145:         onConfirm: (replace: Boolean) -> Unit,
1146:     ) {
1147:         var replace by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
1148:         AlertDialog(
1149:             onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
1150:             title = { Text("Restore this backup?") },
1151:             text = {
1152:                 Column {
1153:                     Text("${summary.transactions} transactions, ${summary.categories} " +
1154:                          "categories, ${summary.learning} learned merchants.",
1155:                         style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium)
1156:                     if (summary.first != null && summary.last != null) {
1157:                         Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
1158:                         Text("${summary.first} to ${summary.last}",
1159:                             style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
1160:                             color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
1161:                     }
1162:                     if (summary.dropped > 0) {
1163:                         Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
1164:                         Text("${summary.dropped} rows in the file couldn't be read and " +
1165:                              "will be skipped.",
1166:                             style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, color = Warn)
1167:                     }
1168:                     Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
1169:                     Row(
1170:                         verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
1171:                         modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
1172:                             .heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
1173:                     ) {
1174:                         Text("Replace everything on this phone",
1175:                             modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
1176:                             style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium)
1177:                         Switch(checked = replace, onCheckedChange = { replace = it })
1178:                     }
1179:                     Text(
1180:                         if (replace)
1181:                             "Your current transactions, categories and learning are " +
1182:                             "deleted first. Use this on a new phone."
1183:                         else
1184:                             "Anything already here is kept. Only what this ledger does " +
1185:                             "not have is added, so restoring the same file twice is safe.",
1186:                         style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
1187:                         color = if (replace) Expense else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
1188:                     )
1189:                 }
1190:             },
1191:             confirmButton = {
1192:                 TextButton(
1193:                     onClick = { onConfirm(replace) },
1194:                     colors = ButtonDefaults.textButtonColors(
1195:                         contentColor = if (replace) Expense else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary),
1196:                     modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
1197:                 ) { Text(if (replace) "Replace everything" else "Merge into my ledger") }
1198:             },
1199:             dismissButton = {
1200:                 TextButton(
1201:                     onClick = onDismiss,
1202:                     modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
1203:                 ) { Text("Cancel") }
1204:             },
1205:         )
1206:     }
1207: 
1208:     // Through the system picker, both ways. The app has no INTERNET
1209:     // permission and no folder of its own that anything else can reach, so a
1210:     // file only exists where the user put it.
1211: 
1212:     private fun writeDocument(uri: Uri, text: String): Boolean = try {
1213:         contentResolver.openOutputStream(uri)?.use { it.write(text.toByteArray()) } != null
1214:     } catch (t: Throwable) {
1215:         // A revoked grant, a full disk, a provider that lied about being
1216:         // writable. The caller says so; nothing here is worth a crash.
1217:         false
1218:     }
1219: 
1220:     /** Text out through the system share sheet. The app sends nothing
1221:      *  itself — it has no INTERNET permission — the person picks where the
1222:      *  text goes, or backs out. */
1223:     private fun shareText(subject: String, text: String): Boolean = try {
1224:         val send = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND)
1225:             .setType("text/plain")
1226:             .putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_SUBJECT, subject)
1227:             .putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, text)
1228:         startActivity(Intent.createChooser(send, subject))
1229:         true
1230:     } catch (t: Throwable) {
1231:         // No activity to resolve the chooser on a stripped-down ROM. The
1232:         // caller says so rather than looking like a tap that did nothing.
1233:         false
1234:     }
1235: 
1236:     private fun readDocument(uri: Uri): ByteArray? = try {
1237:         contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.use { it.readBytes() }
1238:     } catch (t: Throwable) {
1239:         null
1240:     }
1241: 
1242:     /** Three states, not two: the no-SMS flavour declares neither permission,
1243:      *  and asking for one the manifest does not carry is denied instantly by
1244:      *  the system — leaving the user tapping a button that can never work. */
1245:     private fun readSmsAccess(): SmsAccess {
1246:         val declared = declaredPermissions()
1247:         val wanted = SMS_PERMISSIONS.filter { it in declared }
1248:         if (wanted.isEmpty()) return SmsAccess.UNAVAILABLE
1249:         val granted = wanted.all {
1250:             ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, it) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
1251:         }
1252:         return if (granted) SmsAccess.GRANTED else SmsAccess.DENIED
1253:     }
1254: 
1255:     private fun requestSms() {
1256:         val declared = declaredPermissions()
1257:         val missing = SMS_PERMISSIONS.filter {
1258:             it in declared &&
1259:             ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, it) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED
1260:         }
1261:         if (missing.isNotEmpty()) smsPermission.launch(missing.toTypedArray())
1262:     }
1263: 
1264:     private fun declaredPermissions(): Set<String> = try {
1265:         packageManager.getPackageInfo(packageName, PackageManager.GET_PERMISSIONS)
1266:             .requestedPermissions?.toSet().orEmpty()
1267:     } catch (t: Throwable) {
1268:         emptySet()
1269:     }
1270: 
1271:     private companion object {
1272:         val SMS_PERMISSIONS = listOf(Manifest.permission.RECEIVE_SMS, Manifest.permission.READ_SMS)
1273:     }
1274: }
1275: 
1276: /** Material draws a selection pill behind a navigation item's icon. With an
1277:  *  empty slot that pill is an empty blob over the label, which reads as a
1278:  *  missing asset rather than as restraint. */
1279: @Composable
1280: private fun SelectionDot(selected: Boolean) {
1281:     Box(
1282:         Modifier
1283:             .size(8.dp)
1284:             .clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
1285:             .background(
1286:                 if (selected) MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary
1287:                 else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant
1288:             )
1289:     )
1290: }
1291: 
1292: private enum class Tab(val label: String) {
1293:     HOME("Home"), ACTIVITY("Activity"), BUDGETS("Budgets"), ALERTS("Alerts")
1294: }
1295: 
1296: /** Full pages, reached over a back stack rather than by swapping a tab: each
1297:  *  one covers the bars, and back returns to whatever it covered. */
1298: private enum class Page(val title: String) {
1299:     REPORT("Report"),
1300:     MERCHANT("Merchant"),
1301:     REVIEW("Review"),
1302:     SETTINGS("Settings"),
1303:     PRIVACY("Privacy"),
1304:     HELP("Help"),
1305:     PASTE("Paste a bank message"),
1306:     WELCOME("How SpendWise works"),
1307: }
1308: 
1309: private sealed interface Sheet {
1310:     data object New : Sheet
1311:     data class Edit(val tx: TransactionEntity) : Sheet
1312: }
1313: 
1314: private val CaptureDate: DateTimeFormatter =
1315:     DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("d MMM", Locale.ENGLISH)
1316: 
1317: private fun List<CategoryEntity>.toBudgets(type: String): List<Budget> =
1318:     filter { if (type == "income") it.type == "income" else it.type != "income" }
1319:         .map { Budget(categoryId = it.id, name = it.name, amount = it.budgetAmount, color = it.color) }
1320: 
1321: /** The same four fields the DAO's search query covers, so switching between
1322:  *  the two would not change what a search finds. */
1323: private fun List<TransactionEntity>.matching(query: String): List<TransactionEntity> {
1324:     if (query.isBlank()) return this
1325:     return filter { t ->
1326:         listOfNotNull(t.merchantName, t.rawMerchant, t.notes, t.referenceNumber)
1327:             .any { it.contains(query, ignoreCase = true) }
1328:     }
1329: }
1330: 
1331: private const val UNREADABLE_SUBJECT = "Bank messages SpendWise couldn't read"
1332: 
1333: /**
1334:  * What the settings and help screens say about capture.
1335:  *
1336:  * `unreadable` is the parser's misses, folded by :core into one group per
1337:  * format per sender — the same list "Share" sends, so the count on screen
1338:  * and the file someone receives cannot disagree. Held messages are not
1339:  * counted there: they were read, and held because of who sent them or how
1340:  * they were worded, which `queued` already says.
1341:  */
1342: private fun smsStatus(
1343:     access: SmsAccess,
1344:     transactions: List<TransactionEntity>,
1345:     held: List<QuarantineEntity>,
1346:     unreadable: List<ParseMisses.Group>,
1347: ): SmsStatus {
1348:     val captured = transactions.filter { it.source == "sms" }
1349:     return SmsStatus(
1350:         access = access,
1351:         captured = captured.size,
1352:         queued = held.size,
1353:         lastCapture = captured.maxByOrNull { it.occurredAt }
1354:             ?.let { Repo.localTime(it.occurredAt).toLocalDate().format(CaptureDate) },
1355:         unreadableKinds = unreadable.size,
1356:         unreadableTotal = unreadable.sumOf { it.messages },
1357:         unreviewed = captured.count { it.status != "confirmed" },
1358:     )
1359: }
1360: 
1361: /** Short, checkable statements. Anything that cannot cite the number behind
1362:  *  it does not belong on the home screen. */
1363: private fun headlines(
1364:     ledger: List<Tx>,
1365:     recurring: List<Recurring>,
1366:     today: LocalDate,
1367: ): List<String> {
1368:     if (ledger.isEmpty()) return emptyList()
1369:     val month = YearMonth.from(today)
1370:     val out = mutableListOf<String>()
1371:     Insights.forecast(ledger, month, today, recurring)?.let { f ->
1372:         out += "On track for about ${Insights.money(f.projected)} this month."
1373:     }
1374:     Insights.anomalies(ledger, month).firstOrNull()?.let { out += it.explanation }
1375:     Insights.savingsOpportunities(ledger, month, recurring).firstOrNull()
1376:         ?.let { out += it.detail }
1377:     return out
1378: }
1379: 
1380: private fun backupFilename(): String = "spendwise-backup-${LocalDate.now()}.json"
1381: 
1382: private fun csvFilename(): String = "spendwise-${LocalDate.now()}.csv"
1383: 
1384: private fun describe(result: Repo.Ingest): String = when (result) {
1385:     is Repo.Ingest.Captured ->
1386:         if (result.needsCategory) "Added. Give it a category when you have a moment."
1387:         else "Added to your ledger."
1388:     is Repo.Ingest.Duplicate -> "That one is already in your ledger."
1389:     is Repo.Ingest.Held -> "Held for you to check: ${result.reason}"
1390:     Repo.Ingest.NotFinancial -> "That doesn't read as a transaction, so nothing was added."
1391:     Repo.Ingest.Unreadable -> "SpendWise couldn't read this one, so nothing was added. A " +
1392:         "copy with every number and name masked is in Settings, if you want to send it on."
1393: }
1394: 
1395: /** Rows written, not "done": the whole point of the screen is that one tap
1396:  *  settled more than one row, and a bare confirmation hides the number that
1397:  *  makes it worth using. */
1398: private fun sorted(rows: Int, category: String?): String = when {
1399:     rows == 0 -> "Nothing was sorted — those captures are no longer waiting."
1400:     category == null -> "Sorted $rows ${if (rows == 1) "capture" else "captures"}."
1401:     else -> "Sorted $rows ${if (rows == 1) "capture" else "captures"} into $category."
1402: }
1403: 
1404: /** What the merchant page wrote, in the two halves the user decided
1405:  *  separately: where new payments go, and whether any past ones moved. */
1406: private fun filedUnder(name: String, result: Repo.Refiled?): String {
1407:     if (result == null) return "That category has been deleted, so nothing was changed."
1408:     val head = if (result.mappings == 0)
1409:         "New payments from $name were already filed under ${result.categoryName}."
1410:     else "New payments from $name will be filed under ${result.categoryName}."
1411:     val tail = when (result.moved) {
1412:         0 -> " Past payments were left as they were."
1413:         1 -> " 1 past payment was moved."
1414:         else -> " ${result.moved} past payments were moved."
1415:     }
1416:     return head + tail
1417: }
1418: 
1419: private fun describe(result: Repo.CategoryResult, name: String): String = when (result) {
1420:     Repo.CategoryResult.CREATED -> "Added $name."
1421:     Repo.CategoryResult.RESTORED ->
1422:         "Brought back $name, with everything that was filed under it. Set its budget " +
1423:         "again on Budgets if it had one."
1424:     Repo.CategoryResult.RENAMED -> "Renamed to $name."
1425:     Repo.CategoryResult.DUPLICATE -> "There is already a category called $name."
1426:     Repo.CategoryResult.HELD_BY_DELETED ->
1427:         "$name is the name of a deleted category. Add it as a new category to bring it " +
1428:         "back, or choose another name."
1429:     Repo.CategoryResult.GONE -> "That category is no longer there, so nothing was changed."
1430:     Repo.CategoryResult.INVALID -> "A category needs a name."
1431: }
1432: 
1433: /** What actually landed, not "done". The counts come off the plan :core
1434:  *  produced, so they describe the rows that were written rather than the rows
1435:  *  the file claimed to contain. */
1436: private fun describe(plan: Backup.Plan): String {
1437:     val added = plan.totalAdded
1438:     val skipped = plan.totalSkipped
1439:     val head = if (plan.clearFirst) "Replaced your ledger with $added rows."
1440:                else "Added $added rows."
1441:     return if (skipped == 0) head
1442:            else "$head Skipped $skipped already here or unreadable."
1443: }
1444: 
1445: private val SnapshotStamp: DateTimeFormatter =
1446:     DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("d MMM yyyy 'at' HH:mm", Locale.ENGLISH)
1447: 
1448: private fun stamp(snapshot: String?): String? =
1449:     snapshot?.let(Recovery::takenAt)?.format(SnapshotStamp)
1450: 
1451: /** Only once opening has taken longer than a glance. A label on every
1452:  *  ordinary launch would flicker, and a blank screen through a recovery that
1453:  *  takes seconds reads as a hang. */
1454: @Composable
1455: private fun Opening() {
1456:     var slow by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
1457:     LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
1458:         delay(700)
1459:         slow = true
1460:     }
1461:     Surface(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
1462:         if (slow) Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
1463:             Text("Opening your ledger…", style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
1464:                 color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
1465:         }
1466:     }
1467: }
1468: 
1469: /**
1470:  * Instead of a crash, when the ledger will not open.
1471:  *
1472:  * In the order a frightened person needs it: what happened, that the data
1473:  * still exists and where, and a way to try again. A lock gets different words
1474:  * from damage, because telling someone whose data is fine that it is damaged
1475:  * is a harm of its own.
1476:  */
1477: @Composable
1478: private fun LedgerFailure(
1479:     failure: AppGraph.Ledger.Unavailable,
1480:     retrying: Boolean,
1481:     onRetry: () -> Unit,
1482: ) {
1483:     val stopped = failure.stopped
1484:     val where = failure.where
1485:     val untouched = stopped.keptAs.isEmpty() && stopped.restoredFrom == null
1486:     val files = if (untouched) "Nothing was changed or deleted."
1487:                 else "Nothing has been deleted: every file SpendWise moved is kept, listed below."
1488:     val (title, body) = when {
1489:         stopped.outcome == Recovery.Outcome.BUSY -> "Your ledger is busy" to
1490:             "Something else on this phone was still writing to it — usually a bank message " +
1491:             "being saved — so it couldn't be opened just now. That is not damage. Give it a " +
1492:             "moment, then try again. $files"
1493:         stopped.failure == Recovery.Failure.MIGRATION -> "SpendWise couldn't finish updating" to
1494:             "This version couldn't bring your ledger up to date, so it stopped rather than " +
1495:             "risk it. Your transactions are on this phone, as the previous version left " +
1496:             "them. $files An update to SpendWise is the likely fix."
1497:         stopped.failure == Recovery.Failure.CORRUPTION -> "Your ledger couldn't be read" to
1498:             "The file your transactions are kept in is damaged, and SpendWise couldn't " +
1499:             "repair it from a saved copy. $files"
1500:         else -> "SpendWise couldn't open your ledger" to
1501:             "Opening it failed for a reason SpendWise doesn't recognise. A phone that is " +
1502:             "out of storage is the usual one, so freeing some space is worth trying " +
1503:             "first. $files"
1504:     }
1505: 
1506:     Scaffold { pad ->
1507:         Column(
1508:             Modifier
1509:                 .fillMaxSize()
1510:                 .padding(pad)
1511:                 .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState())
1512:                 .padding(horizontal = Tokens.screenPadding, vertical = 24.dp),
1513:             verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(Tokens.gutter),
1514:         ) {
1515:             FailureCard {
1516:                 Text("SPENDWISE", style = MicroLabel,
1517:                     color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
1518:                 Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
1519:                 Text(title, style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineLarge)
1520:                 Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
1521:                 Text(body, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
1522:                     color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
1523:                 Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
1524:                 Button(
1525:                     onClick = onRetry,
1526:                     enabled = !retrying,
1527:                     modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
1528:                 ) { Text(if (retrying) "Trying again…" else "Try again") }
1529:             }
1530: 
1531:             Text("WHERE YOUR DATA IS", style = SectionHeading,
1532:                 color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
1533:                 modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 4.dp, top = 8.dp))
1534:             FailureCard {
1535:                 PathRow("Your ledger", null, where.ledger)
1536:                 HorizontalDivider(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outline,
1537:                     modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 14.dp))
1538:                 val copies = stopped.snapshots
1539:                 PathRow(
1540:                     "Saved copies",
1541:                     if (copies.isEmpty()) "None yet."
1542:                     else "${copies.size} on this phone, the newest from ${stamp(copies.first())}.",
1543:                     where.snapshots,
1544:                 )
1545:                 for (path in where.kept) {
1546:                     HorizontalDivider(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outline,
1547:                         modifier = Modifier.padding(vertical = 14.dp))
1548:                     PathRow("Kept as it was when it failed", null, path)
1549:                 }
1550:             }
1551: 
1552:             // Busy is not a state anyone needs warning off a reinstall for.
1553:             if (stopped.outcome == Recovery.Outcome.SAFE_MODE) FailureCard {
1554:                 Text("Don't uninstall SpendWise or clear its storage. Either one deletes " +
1555:                      "these files, and there is no other copy of them.",
1556:                     style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, color = Expense)
1557:             }
1558: 
1559:             stopped.detail?.let { detail ->
1560:                 Text("WHAT THE PHONE REPORTED", style = SectionHeading,
1561:                     color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
1562:                     modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 4.dp, top = 8.dp))
1563:                 Text(detail, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
1564:                     color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
1565:                     modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 4.dp))
1566:             }
1567:         }
1568:     }
1569: }
1570: 
1571: /**
1572:  * A recovery that let the app open, said once. A restore means entries may be
1573:  * missing and a fresh start means all of them are, so neither can be silent —
1574:  * and only the button clears it, because a tap beside the dialog is not
1575:  * someone having read that their history changed.
1576:  */
1577: @Composable
1578: private fun RecoveryNotice(notice: AppGraph.Notice, rescans: Boolean, onDismiss: () -> Unit) {
1579:     val (title, body) = when {
1580:         notice.outcome == Recovery.Outcome.STARTED_FRESH -> "Your ledger was started again" to
1581:             "The file your transactions were kept in couldn't be read, and there was no " +
1582:             "working saved copy to go back to, so SpendWise started a new, empty one."
1583:         notice.withoutLoss -> "An update was retried" to
1584:             "Updating your ledger failed the first time, so SpendWise put back the copy it " +
1585:             "had saved just before and tried again. It worked, and nothing was lost."
1586:         else -> "Your ledger was restored" to
1587:             "The file your transactions were kept in was damaged, so SpendWise put back the " +
1588:             "copy it saved on ${stamp(notice.restoredFrom) ?: "an earlier date"}. Anything " +
1589:             "recorded after that is missing" +
1590:             (if (rescans) ". Bank messages still in your inbox are read again, but anything " +
1591:                 "you entered yourself will need adding again." else ".")
1592:     }
1593:     AlertDialog(
1594:         onDismissRequest = {},
1595:         title = { Text(title) },
1596:         text = {
1597:             Column {
1598:                 Text(body, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium)
1599:                 if (!notice.withoutLoss && notice.where.kept.isNotEmpty()) {
1600:                     Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
1601:                     Text("The unreadable file was kept, not deleted. Uninstalling or clearing " +
1602:                          "storage would delete it.",
1603:                         style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
1604:                         color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
1605:                     for (path in notice.where.kept) {
1606:                         Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
1607:                         Text(path, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
1608:                             color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
1609:                     }
1610:                 }
1611:             }
1612:         },
1613:         confirmButton = {
1614:             TextButton(
1615:                 onClick = onDismiss,
1616:                 modifier = Modifier.heightIn(min = Tokens.minTouchTarget),
1617:             ) { Text("OK") }
1618:         },
1619:     )
1620: }
1621: 
1622: @Composable

1624:     Surface(
1625:         color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface,
1626:         shape = RoundedCornerShape(Tokens.cardRadius),
1627:         modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
1628:     ) { Column(Modifier.padding(18.dp), content = content) }
1629: }
1630: 
1631: @Composable
1632: private fun PathRow(label: String, detail: String?, path: String) {
1633:     Text(label, style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium)
1634:     if (detail != null) {
1635:         Spacer(Modifier.height(2.dp))
1636:         Text(detail, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
1637:             color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
1638:     }
1639:     Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
1640:     Text(path, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
1641:         color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
1642: }

21: /**
22:  * The one place the verified core logic meets storage.
23:  *
24:  * Everything decision-shaped lives in :core and is tested on the JVM; this
25:  * class only reads rows, hands them to that logic, and writes the result. If
26:  * a rule looks like it is being decided here, it is in the wrong file.
27:  */
28: class Repo(private val dao: SpendDao, private val misses: ParseMissDao) {

36:

45:                 .joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }

75:          *  line break both break a spreadsheet that only quotes when it
76:          *  thinks it has to. */
77:         private fun csvCell(value: String?): String =
78:             "\"" + (value ?: "").replace("\"", "\"\"") + "\""

81:     /**
82:      * Serialises the read-modify-write on the single settings row.
83:      *
84:      * A restore merges the file's preferences onto whatever is stored, and
85:      * the settings screen writes the same row. Both are read-then-write, so
86:      * without this one of them can silently overwrite the other's change with
87:      * a value it read before that change existed.
88:      */
89:     private val settingsGate = Mutex()
