91:     /** Streams rather than one-shot reads, so a screen re-renders when a row
92:      *  changes instead of when something remembers to reload it. */
93:     fun stream(): Flow<List<TransactionEntity>> = dao.stream()
94: 
95:     fun heldMessages(): Flow<List<QuarantineEntity>> = dao.held()
96: 
97:     /** Every sender that has ever sent something financial-looking. The DAO
98:      *  orders by recency; the order the screen shows is SenderBook's. */
99:     fun senders(): Flow<List<SenderEntity>> = dao.senders()
100: 
101:     /**
102:      * Record what the user decided about a sender.
103:      *
104:      * [Senders.assess] treats this as final — it outranks every heuristic the
105:      * module owns — so the value is held to the three SenderBook permits. A
106:      * caller passing `known` or `suspicious` would be writing a machine
107:      * verdict into the column reserved for a person's, and the capture path
108:      * would then stop revising it.
109:      *
110:      * The key goes through [Senders.normalizeSender] because that is the key
111:      * [ingestSms] stores a row under and looks it up by. A decision filed
112:      * under any other spelling would sit on a row the next message never
113:      * reads.
114:      *
115:      * @return true only when a stored row now carries [trust]. False when no
116:      *   row matched — a screen showing a sender a restore has since replaced,
117:      *   a row stored under a spelling the capture path would not produce —
118:      *   because then the next message from that sender is judged exactly as
119:      *   before, and a caller that announced the decision would be lying.
120:      */
121:     suspend fun setSenderTrust(sender: String, trust: String): Boolean =
122:         withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
123:             val key = Senders.normalizeSender(sender)
124:             if (key.isEmpty() || !SenderBook.isUserDecision(trust)) return@withContext false
125:             dao.setSenderTrust(key, trust) > 0
126:         }
127: 
128:     /** Open only. The alert list is a queue of things to look at, not a log
129:      *  of things that were looked at. */
130:     fun fraudAlerts(): Flow<List<FraudAlertEntity>> = dao.openAlerts()
131: 
132:     /**
133:      * The user has looked at an alert.
134:      *
135:      * One action, not the Python build's dismissed-or-resolved pair. This app
136:      * cannot freeze a card or call a bank, so both of those buttons produced
137:      * exactly the same outcome on screen — a choice with no consequence,
138:      * asked of someone who has just been told they may have been charged
139:      * twice.
140:      */
141:     suspend fun dismissAlert(id: String) = dao.dismissAlert(id)
142: 
143:     fun categories(): Flow<List<CategoryEntity>> = dao.categories()
144: 
145:     /** Deleted categories too, for naming what is already filed under them.
146:      *  Anything that offers a category for a new entry reads [categories]. */
147:     fun everyCategory(): Flow<List<CategoryEntity>> = dao.everyCategory()
148: 
149:     /** An install with no settings row yet is the ordinary first launch, not
150:      *  an error, so the defaults stand in for it and every reader downstream
151:      *  gets a row rather than a null it has to think about. */
152:     fun settings(): Flow<SettingsEntity> =
153:         dao.settingsStream().map { it.firstOrNull() ?: SettingsEntity() }
154: 
155:     /** Soft, like every delete here, so the undo behind it stays possible. */
156:     suspend fun delete(id: String) = dao.setDeleted(id, true)
157: 
158:     /** The other half of [delete]. The row was never removed, so this brings
159:      *  back the same transaction rather than a rebuilt copy of it. */
160:     suspend fun undelete(id: String) = dao.setDeleted(id, false)
161: 
162:     /**
163:      * Add or edit one transaction.
164:      *
165:      * Insert or update is decided from what is already stored rather than
166:      * from a flag the caller passes: the sheet composes an edit as a copy of
167:      * the original row, so the id is the only reliable witness to which of
168:      * the two this is.
169:      */
170:     suspend fun save(tx: TransactionEntity) = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
171:         val existing = dao.byId(tx.id)
172:         if (existing == null) {
173:             dao.insert(tx)
174:             raiseFraudAlerts(tx)
175:         } else dao.update(tx)
176: 
177:         // Saving a payee with a category is the user stating what that payee
178:         // is, which is the only thing that ever teaches the engine. A changed
179:         // category on a row that already had one is a correction, and the
180:         // scoring weights the two differently.
181:         val raw = tx.rawMerchant ?: tx.merchantName
182:         val display = tx.merchantName ?: tx.rawMerchant
183:         if (raw != null && display != null && tx.categoryId != null) {
184:             recordConfirmation(
185:                 rawMerchant = raw,
186:                 merchantName = display,
187:                 categoryId = tx.categoryId,
188:                 amount = tx.amount,
189:                 occurredAt = localTime(tx.occurredAt),
190:                 wasCorrection = existing != null &&
191:                     existing.categoryId != null && existing.categoryId != tx.categoryId,
192:             )
193:         }
194:     }
195: 
196:     /**
197:      * The user naming the payee on a capture the app filed but would not
198:      * vouch for, and the engine learning from it.
199:      *
200:      * [save] cannot do this job: it carries the stored status forward, which
201:      * is right for an edit — correcting an amount is not a review — and
202:      * leaves a low-confidence capture reading "Needs review" no matter how
203:      * often it is edited. This is the one path that moves a stored row to
204:      * confirmed.
205:      *
206:      * @return false when the row is gone or the name is blank, so the caller
207:      *   can say nothing happened rather than claim a confirmation.
208:      */
209:     suspend fun confirm(id: String, merchantName: String,
210:                         categoryId: String? = null): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
211:         val row = dao.byId(id) ?: return@withContext false
212:         val decided = Pipeline.confirm(row.merchantName, row.categoryId, merchantName, categoryId)
213:             ?: return@withContext false
214: 
215:         dao.update(row.copy(
216:             merchantName = decided.merchantName,
217:             categoryId = decided.categoryId,
218:             status = decided.status,
219:             confidence = decided.confidence,
220:         ))
221: 
222:         // Teach from the RAW payee the bank sent, which is the string the
223:         // next message will arrive with — learning under the display name
224:         // would train a key no incoming SMS ever matches. A manually entered
225:         // row has no raw payee, and there the confirmed name is that string,
226:         // which is the same fallback [save] uses.
227:         recordConfirmation(
228:             rawMerchant = row.rawMerchant ?: decided.merchantName,
229:             merchantName = decided.merchantName,
230:             categoryId = decided.categoryId,
231:             amount = row.amount,
232:             occurredAt = localTime(row.occurredAt),
233:             wasCorrection = decided.isCorrection,
234:         )
235:         true
236:     }
237: 
238:     /**
239:      * "It's real" on a held message: the transaction it describes is banked
240:      * and the message leaves the alert list, in one transaction.
241:      *
242:      * The status is `confirmed` rather than whatever confidence would have
243:      * chosen. A person looked at this message and vouched for it, and asking
244:      * them to confirm it a second time on the review screen would be the app
245:      * disagreeing with a decision it just asked for.
246:      */
247:     suspend fun approveHeld(id: String): Ingest = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
248:         val row = dao.quarantineById(id) ?: return@withContext Ingest.NotFinancial
249: 
250:         // Re-read the stored body with the same parser the live path uses,
251:         // rather than rebuilding from the columns beside it. The quarantine
252:         // table has no reference-number column, and the reference is part of
253:         // the dedup key — so a message approved from its columns would get a
254:         // different key than the one the capture path would have written, and
255:         // the bank's own confirmation of the same purchase would land twice.
256:         val parsed = Parsing.parse(row.body).takeIf { it.amount != null }
257:             ?: ParsedSms(
258:                 amount = row.amount,
259:                 type = row.type ?: "expense",
260:                 rawMerchant = row.rawMerchant,
261:                 occurredAt = row.occurredAt?.let { localTime(it) },
262:             )
263: 
264:         val amount = parsed.amount ?: run {
265:             // Nothing to file. The screen only offers approval when there is
266:             // an amount, so this is a stale tap rather than a normal path.
267:             dao.setQuarantineStatus(id, "approved")
268:             return@withContext Ingest.NotFinancial
269:         }
270: 
271:         val at = parsed.occurredAt ?: row.occurredAt?.let { localTime(it) }
272:             ?: localTime(row.createdAt)
273:         val resolved = resolveMerchant(parsed.rawMerchant, amount, at)
274:         val tx = TransactionEntity(
275:             id = newId(),
276:             amount = amount,
277:             type = parsed.type,
278:             categoryId = resolved.categoryId,
279:             rawMerchant = parsed.rawMerchant,
280:             merchantName = resolved.merchantName,
281:             referenceNumber = parsed.referenceNumber,
282:             occurredAt = millis(at),
283:             source = "sms",
284:             confidence = resolved.confidence,
285:             status = "confirmed",
286:             dedupKey = sha256(Pipeline.dedupKey(parsed, at)),
287:             smsSender = row.sender,
288:             createdAt = System.currentTimeMillis(),
289:         )
290:         val banked = dao.bankHeldMessage(tx, id)
291:         if (banked) raiseFraudAlerts(tx)
292:         // Senders.assess reads confirmedCount back as evidence, so vouching
293:         // for a message is what stops the next one from the same sender
294:         // being held too.
295:         row.sender?.let { dao.noteSenderConfirmed(Senders.normalizeSender(it)) }
296:         if (banked) Ingest.Captured(tx.id, resolved.categoryId == null)
297:         else Ingest.Duplicate(tx.dedupKey!!)
298:     }
299: 
300:     /** Discarded, not deleted: the row survives with a rejected status, so a
301:      *  mistaken reject stays recoverable and a repeat of the same scam is
302:      *  still recognised by its body hash. */
303:     suspend fun rejectHeld(id: String) = dao.setQuarantineStatus(id, "rejected")
304: 
305:     /**
306:      * Discard the message and block whoever sent it, in one decision.
307:      *
308:      * The sender list can do this too, but the moment a person is certain is
309:      * the moment they are reading the scam — sending them off to find the
310:      * same sender in a list afterwards is how a repeat offender keeps its
311:      * welcome.
312:      *
313:      * @return the sender that was blocked, or null if nothing was blocked —
314:      *   the message carried no usable sender, or no stored sender row matched
315:      *   it, which is what a restore that replaced the senders but kept the
316:      *   held messages leaves behind. The message is discarded either way.
317:      */
318:     suspend fun rejectHeldAndBlock(id: String): String? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
319:         val row = dao.quarantineById(id)
320:         dao.setQuarantineStatus(id, "rejected")
321:         val key = row?.sender?.let { Senders.normalizeSender(it) }?.ifEmpty { null }
322:         key?.takeIf { setSenderTrust(it, Senders.TRUST_BLOCKED) }
323:     }
324: 
325:     /** What happened to an incoming message, for the caller to log or show. */
326:     sealed class Ingest {
327:         data class Captured(val id: String, val needsCategory: Boolean) : Ingest()
328:         data class Held(val reason: String, val risk: Int) : Ingest()
329:         data class Duplicate(val id: String) : Ingest()
330:         data object NotFinancial : Ingest()
331:         /** From a bank, read like money moving, and the parser could not
332:          *  read it. Logged as its shape so the user can see and send it. */
333:         data object Unreadable : Ingest()
334:     }
335: 
336:     /**
337:      * The whole SMS pipeline, in the order the decisions have to happen.
338:      *
339:      * Parse first, and only then judge the sender. The parse gate is entirely
340:      * generic on purpose: a sender profile can enrich a message that already
341:      * looks like a transaction, but it must never be able to MAKE something a
342:      * transaction — otherwise a spoofed header could inject rows.
343:      *
344:      * @param live a message arriving now, rather than one the inbox scan is
345:      *   walking past for the fourth time. A rescan must not be able to change
346:      *   anything a first pass already decided: without this the same held
347:      *   scam reports "Seen 6 times" after six launches, and every sender's
348:      *   message counts drift upward on their own.
349:      * @param receivedAt when the message reached the phone. Used only where
350:      *   the message itself carries no date — and there it is load-bearing,
351:      *   because the dedup key falls back to it. Reading the clock instead
352:      *   would give the same undated purchase a new key every day.
353:      * @param pasted the user pasted this into "Paste a bank message". It has
354:      *   no sender, and the person pasting is the one saying a bank sent it —
355:      *   which is what decides whether an unreadable one is worth logging.
356:      */
357:     suspend fun ingestSms(
358:         sender: String?,
359:         body: String,
360:         live: Boolean = true,
361:         receivedAt: LocalDateTime = LocalDateTime.now(),
362:         pasted: Boolean = false,
363:     ): Ingest = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
364:         if (!live) {
365:             // Already banked. Checked first because the alternative is paying
366:             // for a full merchant resolution — which retrains the categoriser
367:             // over the whole ledger — once per already-captured message, on
368:             // every launch.
369:             alreadyBanked(body, receivedAt)?.let { return@withContext Ingest.Duplicate(it) }
370:             dao.quarantineByHash(sha256(body))?.let { seen ->
371:                 return@withContext Ingest.Held(
372:                     seen.reason ?: "Held for you to check.", seen.risk)
373:             }
374:         }
375: 
376:         // The decision sequence itself lives in :core and is covered by 24
377:         // JVM checks. This function only supplies the lookups and writes the
378:         // result — if a rule appears to be decided here, it is in the wrong
379:         // file.
380:         //
381:         // :core has no coroutines dependency at all, so the two lookups
382:         // Pipeline.ingest takes are ordinary functions. Both need the
383:         // database, and each bridges that gap differently below because the
384:         // two lookups cost very different amounts.
385: 
386:         // Pipeline asks for this sender under a key derivable here, so the row
387:         // is read once up front and the callback stays pure. The Capture
388:         // branch below reuses it instead of asking again.
389:         val senderKey = sender?.let { Senders.normalizeSender(it) }
390:         val senderRow = senderKey?.let { dao.senderByName(it) }
391: 
392:         // The two sliders on the settings screen are these. Read per message
393:         // rather than cached, so moving one changes the next message that
394:         // arrives instead of the next launch.
395:         val prefs = dao.settingsRow() ?: SettingsEntity()
396: 
397:         val outcome = Pipeline.ingest(
398:             sender = sender,
399:             body = body,
400:             now = receivedAt,
401:             lookupSender = { key ->
402:                 // Fail closed on a key that was not the one prefetched:
403:                 // handing back some other sender's reputation would let one
404:                 // sender's trust decide another sender's quarantine.
405:                 senderRow?.takeIf { key == senderKey }
406:                     ?.let { SenderRegistry(it.trust, it.confirmedCount, it.quarantinedCount) }
407:             },
408:             resolve = { raw, amount, at ->
409:                 // Prefetching this one would mean resolving every message that
410:                 // reaches the parser. Pipeline calls resolve only after a
411:                 // message has cleared the parse and sender gates, and
412:                 // resolveMerchant retrains the categoriser over the whole
413:                 // ledger — a cost an inbox rescan must not pay for every OTP
414:                 // and promo it walks past. So the laziness is kept and one IO
415:                 // thread is parked for the lookup instead.
416:                 val r = runBlocking { resolveMerchant(raw, amount, at) }
417:                 Pipeline.Resolution(r.merchantName, r.categoryId, r.confidence)
418:             },
419:             autoAt = prefs.autoSaveThreshold,
420:             confirmAt = prefs.confirmThreshold,
421:         )
422: 
423:         when (outcome) {
424:             is Pipeline.Outcome.NotFinancial ->
425:                 if (recordUnreadable(sender, body, live, receivedAt, pasted, senderRow))
426:                     Ingest.Unreadable
427:                 else Ingest.NotFinancial
428: 
429:             is Pipeline.Outcome.Hold -> {
430:                 recordSender(outcome.verdict, captured = false)
431:                 holdMessage(sender, body, outcome.parsed, outcome.verdict)
432:                 Ingest.Held(outcome.reason, outcome.verdict.risk)
433:             }
434: 
435:             is Pipeline.Outcome.Capture -> {
436:                 sender?.let {
437:                     recordSender(Senders.assess(it, body,
438:                         senderRow?.let { r ->
439:                             SenderRegistry(r.trust, r.confirmedCount, r.quarantinedCount) }),
440:                         captured = true)
441:                 }
442:                 val at = outcome.parsed.occurredAt ?: receivedAt
443:                 val tx = TransactionEntity(
444:                     id = newId(),
445:                     amount = outcome.parsed.amount!!,
446:                     type = outcome.parsed.type,
447:                     categoryId = outcome.categoryId,
448:                     rawMerchant = outcome.parsed.rawMerchant,
449:                     merchantName = outcome.merchantName,
450:                     referenceNumber = outcome.parsed.referenceNumber,
451:                     occurredAt = millis(at),
452:                     source = "sms",
453:                     confidence = outcome.confidence,
454:                     status = outcome.status,
455:                     dedupKey = sha256(outcome.dedupKey),
456:                     smsSender = sender,
457:                     createdAt = System.currentTimeMillis(),
458:                 )
459:                 // insert returns -1 when the unique dedupKey already exists,
460:                 // which is what makes rescanning the inbox harmless.
461:                 if (dao.insert(tx) == -1L) Ingest.Duplicate(tx.dedupKey!!)
462:                 else {
463:                     raiseFraudAlerts(tx)
464:                     Ingest.Captured(tx.id, outcome.needsCategory)
465:                 }
466:             }
467:         }
468:     }
469: 
470:     /**
471:      * Run the detectors over one newly filed expense and store what they say.
472:      *
473:      * Only where a row is INSERTED. An edit is the user restating a
474:      * transaction they already have, and re-evaluating there would mean
475:      * fixing a typo in a note raises a duplicate-charge alert against the
476:      * charge's own earlier self.
477:      *
478:      * The whole ledger goes in because every detector measures this charge
479:      * against the user's own distribution — a mean, a spread, a daily average
480:      * — and there is no smaller slice those can be computed from. The write
481:      * is an IGNORE on the alert's unique key, so reaching here twice for the
482:      * same charge still leaves one alert rather than two.
483:      */
484:     private suspend fun raiseFraudAlerts(tx: TransactionEntity) {
485:         if (tx.type != "expense" || tx.isDeleted) return
486:         // The launch scan backfills ninety days of inbox at once, and a
487:         // transaction can also be hand-entered from an old receipt. :core
488:         // decides how old is too old to be worth saying anything about.
489:         if (!Fraud.worthAlerting(localTime(tx.occurredAt), LocalDateTime.now())) return
490:         // Read per transaction rather than cached: a limit typed on the
491:         // settings screen has to apply to the next message that arrives, not
492:         // the next launch.
493:         val limit = (dao.settingsRow() ?: SettingsEntity()).highValueAmount
494:         val alerts = Fraud.evaluate(fraudTx(tx), dao.all().map { fraudTx(it) }, limit)
495:         if (alerts.isEmpty()) return
496:         val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
497:         dao.insertAlerts(alerts.map {
498:             FraudAlertEntity(newId(), it.key, it.txId, it.kind, it.severity, it.message,
499:                 createdAt = now)
500:         })
501:     }
502: 
503:     /** The resolved payee, never the bank's raw string. "First charge at this
504:      *  payee" has to mean a payee: two spellings of one shop would each look
505:      *  like a first visit, and every unresolved capture would look like a
506:      *  payee nobody has ever paid. */
507:     private fun fraudTx(t: TransactionEntity) =
508:         Tx(t.id, t.amount, t.type, localTime(t.occurredAt), t.merchantName, t.categoryId)
509: 
510:     /**
511:      * The dedup key this message would be filed under, if the ledger already
512:      * holds it.
513:      *
514:      * Derived exactly as the capture path derives it — the same parse, the
515:      * same fallback to `now` for an undated message, the same hash — because
516:      * a key computed even slightly differently would answer "no" to a message
517:      * that is already there and hand back a duplicate.
518:      */
519:     private suspend fun alreadyBanked(body: String, receivedAt: LocalDateTime): String? {
520:         val parsed = Parsing.parse(body)
521:         if (parsed.amount == null || !parsed.matched) return null
522:         val key = sha256(Pipeline.dedupKey(parsed, parsed.occurredAt ?: receivedAt))
523:         return if (dao.idByDedupKey(key) != null) key else null
524:     }
525: 
526:     /**
527:      * What saving a pasted message would store, with nothing stored.
528:      *
529:      * Runs on "Read it", before the user has agreed to anything, so every
530:      * call below is a read: the settings row, the categories, the learning
531:      * behind [resolveMerchant] and the dedup index. The decision itself is
532:      * [Paste.preview] in :core. Nothing here keeps the pasted text either —
533:      * the old path held a scam-worded paste in the quarantine table, where
534:      * the held-message export could carry it off the phone.
535:      */
536:     suspend fun previewPaste(body: String, now: LocalDateTime = LocalDateTime.now()): Paste.Preview =
537:         withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
538:             val prefs = dao.settingsRow() ?: SettingsEntity()
539:             // The categories the card's picker shows. An archived one is not
540:             // among them, so suggesting it would select a chip nobody can see.
541:             val sides = dao.allCategories().filterNot { it.isArchived }
542:                 .associateBy({ it.id }, { it.type })
543:             Paste.preview(
544:                 body = body,
545:                 now = now,
546:                 // The same bridge ingestSms uses: :core takes plain functions,
547:                 // these lookups suspend, and this is already an IO thread.
548:                 resolve = { raw, amount, at ->
549:                     val r = runBlocking { resolveMerchant(raw, amount, at) }
550:                     Pipeline.Resolution(r.merchantName, r.categoryId, r.confidence)
551:                 },
552:                 banked = { key -> runBlocking { bankedAs(key) } },
553:                 categoryType = { id -> sides[id] },
554:                 autoAt = prefs.autoSaveThreshold,
555:                 confirmAt = prefs.confirmThreshold,
556:             )
557:         }
558: 
559:     /** The row an unhashed dedup key is already filed as, deleted or not —
560:      *  the unique index refuses a second row either way. */
561:     suspend fun bankedAs(key: String): Paste.Banked? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
562:         val id = dao.idByDedupKey(sha256(key)) ?: return@withContext null
563:         val row = dao.byId(id) ?: return@withContext null
564:         Paste.Banked(row.id, row.amount, row.type, row.merchantName ?: row.rawMerchant,
565:             localTime(row.occurredAt), row.isDeleted)
566:     }
567: 
568:     /**
569:      * Store a pasted message the user has checked: exactly the row
570:      * [Paste.commit] made from what was on the card, and the lesson it says
571:      * the engine should learn. Nothing is decided here.
572:      */
573:     suspend fun savePaste(commit: Paste.Commit): Ingest = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
574:         val tx = TransactionEntity(
575:             id = newId(),
576:             amount = commit.amount,
577:             type = commit.type,
578:             categoryId = commit.categoryId,
579:             rawMerchant = commit.rawMerchant,
580:             merchantName = commit.merchantName,
581:             referenceNumber = commit.referenceNumber,
582:             occurredAt = millis(commit.occurredAt),
583:             // A bank message, whoever carried it in, as it was before there
584:             // was a preview: it belongs with the captures in the review queue
585:             // and in what "Clear unreviewed captures" offers to clear.
586:             source = "sms",
587:             confidence = commit.confidence,
588:             status = commit.status,
589:             // The card had a category picker on it, so the question
590:             // CategoryPrompt exists to raise has already been asked.
591:             categoryPrompted = true,
592:             dedupKey = sha256(commit.dedupKey),
593:             createdAt = System.currentTimeMillis(),
594:         )
595:         // IGNORE on the unique key: the same message already captured by the
596:         // receiver, or saved twice from two cards, lands once.
597:         if (dao.insert(tx) == -1L) return@withContext Ingest.Duplicate(tx.dedupKey!!)
598:         raiseFraudAlerts(tx)
599:         commit.learn?.let { l ->
600:             recordConfirmation(
601:                 rawMerchant = l.rawMerchant,
602:                 merchantName = l.merchantName,
603:                 categoryId = l.categoryId,
604:                 amount = commit.amount,
605:                 occurredAt = commit.occurredAt,
606:                 wasCorrection = l.isCorrection,
607:             )
608:         }
609:         Ingest.Captured(tx.id, commit.categoryId == null)
610:     }
611: 
612:     data class Resolved(val merchantName: String?, val categoryId: String?, val confidence: Int)
613: 
614:     /**
615:      * Name and category for a raw payee string, best evidence first:
616:      * what this user has taught the app, then the built-in seed table, then
617:      * the on-device categoriser, then nothing.
618:      */
619:     suspend fun resolveMerchant(rawMerchant: String?, amount: Double?,
620:                                 occurredAt: LocalDateTime?): Resolved {
621:         val normalized = Engine.normalizeMerchant(rawMerchant)
622:         if (normalized.isEmpty()) return Resolved(null, null, 0)
623: 
624:         // Each source below can name a category the user has since deleted:
625:         // a payee learned before it, a seed filed under a built-in name, a
626:         // model trained on its old rows. Categories.fileable refuses those, so
627:         // the capture is asked about instead of filed where nothing shows it.
628:         val categories = dao.allCategories().map { it.asRow() }
629: 
630:         dao.learningFor(normalized)?.let { row ->
631:             val learning = LearningRow(
632:                 row.rawName, row.merchantName, row.categoryId, row.confirmationCount,
633:                 row.correctionCount, row.sampleCount, row.avgAmount, row.amountMin,
634:                 row.amountMax, parseHistogram(row.hourHistogram))
635:             val b = Engine.score(learning, amount, row.categoryId, occurredAt)
636:             return Resolved(row.merchantName, Categories.fileable(row.categoryId, categories),
637:                 b.total)
638:         }
639: 
640:         Engine.seedLookup(normalized)?.let { (display, categoryName) ->
641:             val cat = categories.firstOrNull { it.name == categoryName }
642:             return Resolved(display, Categories.fileable(cat?.id, categories),
643:                 Engine.SEED_CONFIDENCE)
644:         }
645: 
646:         // Nothing learned and not a seed: ask the categoriser trained on this
647:         // user's own corrections. It declines below its confidence floor,
648:         // which is why a wrong category is rare rather than merely unlikely.
649:         val suggestion = trainedModel()?.predict(normalized)
650:         val filed = Categories.fileable(suggestion?.first, categories)
651:         // The model's confidence was only ever about the category, so a
652:         // refused category leaves no evidence behind it.
653:         return Resolved(titleCase(normalized), filed,
654:             if (filed == null) 0
655:             else suggestion?.let { (it.second * 100).toInt().coerceAtMost(70) } ?: 0)
656:     }
657: 
658:     /** Retrained on demand from the user's own categorised transactions.
659:      *  Cached per call site rather than globally, so a category corrected a
660:      *  moment ago is reflected immediately instead of being stale. */
661:     private suspend fun trainedModel(): Categorizer.Model? {
662:         val rows = dao.all()
663:             .filter { it.categoryId != null }
664:             .map { (it.merchantName ?: it.rawMerchant ?: "") to it.categoryId }
665:         return Categorizer.train(rows)
666:     }
667: 
668:     /** Record that the user accepted (or corrected) a mapping. This is the
669:      *  only thing that ever teaches the engine — there is no other source. */
670:     suspend fun recordConfirmation(rawMerchant: String, merchantName: String,
671:                                    categoryId: String?, amount: Double?,
672:                                    occurredAt: LocalDateTime?,
673:                                    wasCorrection: Boolean) = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
674:         val key = Engine.normalizeMerchant(rawMerchant).ifEmpty { return@withContext }
675:         val existing = dao.learningFor(key)
676:         val hours = parseHistogram(existing?.hourHistogram ?: "").toMutableList()
677:         while (hours.size < 24) hours.add(0)
678:         occurredAt?.let { hours[it.hour % 24] = hours[it.hour % 24] + 1 }
679: 
680:         val samples = (existing?.sampleCount ?: 0) + if (amount != null) 1 else 0
681:         val avg = if (amount != null && samples > 0)
682:             ((existing?.avgAmount ?: 0.0) * (samples - 1) + amount) / samples
683:         else existing?.avgAmount ?: 0.0
684: 
685:         dao.upsertLearning(LearningEntity(
686:             id = existing?.id ?: newId(),
687:             rawName = key,
688:             merchantName = merchantName,
689:             categoryId = categoryId ?: existing?.categoryId,
690:             confirmationCount = (existing?.confirmationCount ?: 0) + if (wasCorrection) 0 else 1,
691:             correctionCount = (existing?.correctionCount ?: 0) + if (wasCorrection) 1 else 0,
692:             sampleCount = samples,
693:             avgAmount = avg,
694:             amountMin = minOfNonZero(existing?.amountMin, amount),
695:             amountMax = maxOf(existing?.amountMax ?: 0.0, amount ?: 0.0),
696:             hourHistogram = hours.joinToString(","),
697:             lastSeenAt = System.currentTimeMillis(),
698:         ))
699: 
700:         val merchantKey = Engine.normalizeMerchant(merchantName)
701:         if (merchantKey.isNotEmpty() && dao.merchantByName(merchantKey) == null) {
702:             dao.insertMerchant(MerchantEntity(newId(), merchantKey, categoryId))
703:         }
704:     }
705: 
706:     /**
707:      * What decides how this merchant's next payment is named and filed, live.
708:      *
709:      * The rules are MerchantBook's; this only supplies the rows. Mapped off
710:      * the main thread because the screen collects it there.
711:      */
712:     fun merchantFiling(name: String, rawNames: Set<String>): Flow<MerchantBook.Filing> =
713:         dao.learningAbout(name, rawNames.toList())
714:             .map { rows -> MerchantBook.filing(rows.map { learningRow(it) }, name, rawNames) }
715:             .flowOn(Dispatchers.Default)
716: 
717:     /** What [fileMerchantUnder] wrote: mappings changed or taught, and past
718:      *  payments moved, which are the two things the user is told. */
719:     data class Refiled(val categoryName: String, val mappings: Int, val moved: Int)
720: 
721:     /**
722:      * File new payments from [name] under [categoryId], and move exactly the
723:      * past payments in [moveIds], which the user ticked.
724:      *
725:      * Planned against the table as it is now, not as the screen last saw it.
726:      * Every write is one transaction, so a mapping never changes while the
727:      * history the user asked to move with it stays where it was.
728:      *
729:      * @return null when the category no longer exists or has been deleted, so
730:      *   nothing was written. The picker offers only live ones, but it can
731:      *   have been deleted while the dialog was open, and moving payments
732:      *   into a category no screen offers is not what the user chose.
733:      */
734:     suspend fun fileMerchantUnder(
735:         name: String,
736:         rawNames: Set<String>,
737:         categoryId: String,
738:         moveIds: List<String>,
739:     ): Refiled? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
740:         val all = dao.allCategories()
741:         Categories.fileable(categoryId, all.map { it.asRow() }) ?: return@withContext null
742:         val category = all.first { it.id == categoryId }
743:         val rows = dao.learningAboutNow(name, rawNames.toList())
744:         val plan = MerchantBook.relearn(rows.map { learningRow(it) }, name, rawNames, categoryId)
745:         val byRaw = rows.associateBy { it.rawName }
746:         val changed = plan.changed.mapNotNull { r ->
747:             byRaw[r.rawName]?.copy(
748:                 categoryId = r.categoryId,
749:                 confirmationCount = r.confirmationCount,
750:                 correctionCount = r.correctionCount,
751:             )
752:         }
753:         val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
754:         val taught = plan.taught.map { t ->
755:             LearningEntity(
756:                 id = newId(), rawName = t.rawName, merchantName = t.merchantName,
757:                 categoryId = t.categoryId, confirmationCount = t.confirmationCount,
758:                 correctionCount = t.correctionCount, sampleCount = t.sampleCount,
759:                 avgAmount = t.avgAmount, amountMin = t.amountMin, amountMax = t.amountMax,
760:                 hourHistogram = t.hourHistogram.joinToString(","), lastSeenAt = now,
761:             )
762:         }
763:         val moved = dao.relearnMerchant(name, changed, taught, moveIds, categoryId)
764:         // Kept as recordConfirmation keeps it, so a backup taken now carries
765:         // the merchant a taught row names.
766:         val merchantKey = Engine.normalizeMerchant(name)
767:         if (taught.isNotEmpty() && merchantKey.isNotEmpty() && dao.merchantByName(merchantKey) == null) {
768:             dao.insertMerchant(MerchantEntity(newId(), merchantKey, categoryId))
769:         }
770:         Refiled(category.name, changed.size + taught.size, moved)
771:     }
772: 
773:     /**
774:      * Forget one learned mapping. The next message under [rawName] is
775:      * resolved as if it had never been learned: the built-in table, then the
776:      * categoriser. Nothing in the ledger changes — every past payment keeps
777:      * its name and category.
778:      *
779:      * @return the row as it was, for [restoreMapping]; null if it was already gone.
780:      */
781:     suspend fun forgetMapping(rawName: String): LearningEntity? = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
782:         dao.forgetLearning(rawName)
783:     }
784: 
785:     /** The undo for [forgetMapping]. IGNORE, so if a capture has taught the
786:      *  same spelling since, that newer row is kept rather than replaced. */
787:     suspend fun restoreMapping(row: LearningEntity) = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
788:         dao.insertLearning(listOf(row))
789:     }
790: 
791:     private fun learningRow(e: LearningEntity) = LearningRow(
792:         e.rawName, e.merchantName, e.categoryId, e.confirmationCount, e.correctionCount,
793:         e.sampleCount, e.avgAmount, e.amountMin, e.amountMax, parseHistogram(e.hourHistogram))
794: 
795:     private suspend fun holdMessage(sender: String?, body: String, parsed: ParsedSms,
796:                                     verdict: RiskAssessment) {
797:         val hash = sha256(body)
798:         val existing = dao.quarantineByHash(hash)
799:         dao.upsertQuarantine(QuarantineEntity(
800:             id = existing?.id ?: newId(),
801:             sender = sender,
802:             body = body,
803:             bodyHash = hash,
804:             risk = verdict.risk,
805:             indicators = verdict.indicators.joinToString(","),
806:             reason = Senders.explain(verdict),
807:             amount = parsed.amount,
808:             type = parsed.type,
809:             rawMerchant = parsed.rawMerchant,
810:             occurredAt = parsed.occurredAt?.let { millis(it) },
811:             // Seeing the same scam repeatedly is itself information.
812:             seenCount = (existing?.seenCount ?: 0) + 1,
813:             status = existing?.status ?: "held",
814:             createdAt = existing?.createdAt ?: System.currentTimeMillis(),
815:         ))
816:     }
817: 
818:     private suspend fun recordSender(v: RiskAssessment, captured: Boolean) {
819:         val key = v.sender.normalized.ifEmpty { return }
820:         val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
821:         val existing = dao.senderByName(key)
822:         dao.upsertSender(SenderEntity(
823:             id = existing?.id ?: newId(),
824:             sender = key,
825:             display = v.sender.bank ?: existing?.display,
826:             kind = v.sender.kind,
827:             entity = v.sender.entity,
828:             bank = v.sender.bank,
829:             // A user decision is never overwritten by a heuristic.
830:             trust = existing?.trust?.takeIf {
831:                 it == Senders.TRUST_TRUSTED || it == Senders.TRUST_BLOCKED
832:             } ?: v.trust,
833:             messageCount = (existing?.messageCount ?: 0) + 1,
834:             capturedCount = (existing?.capturedCount ?: 0) + if (captured) 1 else 0,
835:             confirmedCount = existing?.confirmedCount ?: 0,
836:             quarantinedCount = (existing?.quarantinedCount ?: 0) + if (captured) 0 else 1,
837:             lastRisk = v.risk,
838:             firstSeenAt = existing?.firstSeenAt ?: now,
839:             lastSeenAt = now,
840:         ))
841:     }
842: 
843:     /** The ledger as the core module wants it. */
844:     suspend fun ledger(): List<Tx> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
845:         dao.all().map {
846:             Tx(it.id, it.amount, it.type, localTime(it.occurredAt),
847:                it.merchantName ?: it.rawMerchant, it.categoryId)
848:         }
849:     }
850: 
851:     /**
852:      * Named outcomes rather than a boolean, because the two failures need
853:      * different sentences: a blank name is something the user can fix by
854:      * typing, a duplicate is them already having what they asked for.
855:      */
856:     enum class CategoryResult {
857:         CREATED,
858:         /** The name was a deleted category's, so that category came back. */
859:         RESTORED,
860:         RENAMED,
861:         DUPLICATE,
862:         /** A rename onto a deleted category's name. Said as that rather than
863:          *  DUPLICATE: "already exists" about a category no screen shows is a
864:          *  sentence the user can do nothing with. */
865:         HELD_BY_DELETED,
866:         /** The category was no longer there, so nothing was written. */
867:         GONE,
868:         INVALID,
869:     }
870: 
871:     /**
872:      * Seed the built-in categories, once, into an empty table.
873:      *
874:      * `Engine.SEED_MERCHANTS` files Swiggy under "Food & Dining" by NAME, and
875:      * `resolveMerchant` turns that name into an id by looking it up here — so
876:      * with no rows in this table the seed table silently resolves nothing and
877:      * every captured transaction lands uncategorised.
878:      *
879:      * Only into an empty table. Re-adding them whenever one is missing would
880:      * resurrect a category the user archived, and undo a restore that
881:      * deliberately carried a different set.
882:      */
883:     suspend fun ensureDefaultCategories() = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
884:         if (dao.allCategories().isNotEmpty()) return@withContext
885:         dao.insertCategories(DEFAULT_CATEGORIES.map { (name, type, color) ->
886:             CategoryEntity(id = newId(), name = name, type = type, color = color)
887:         })
888:     }
889: 
890:     suspend fun createCategory(name: String, type: String): CategoryResult =
891:         withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { claimCategory(name, type).first }
892: 
893:     /**
894:      * Adding a category by name, with the id it ended up under — the one
895:      * created, the one already holding the name, or the deleted one the name
896:      * brought back. The review screen files into that id directly: looking
897:      * the name up again afterwards would miss a holder spelled in another
898:      * case, and file nothing.
899:      */
900:     private suspend fun claimCategory(name: String, type: String): Pair<CategoryResult, String?> {
901:         val stored = dao.allCategories()
902:         return when (val claim = Categories.claim(name, stored.map { it.asRow() })) {
903:             Categories.Claim.Blank -> CategoryResult.INVALID to null
904:             is Categories.Claim.Taken -> CategoryResult.DUPLICATE to claim.id
905:             is Categories.Claim.Restore -> {
906:                 val back = claim.row
907:                 if (dao.setArchived(back.id, back.isArchived, back.budgetAmount) > 0)
908:                     CategoryResult.RESTORED to back.id
909:                 else CategoryResult.GONE to null
910:             }
911:             is Categories.Claim.Create -> {
912:                 val fresh = CategoryEntity(
913:                     id = newId(), name = claim.name, type = type,
914:                     color = DEFAULT_CATEGORIES.firstOrNull { it.first == claim.name }?.third
915:                         ?: Budgets.DEFAULT_COLOR,
916:                 )
917:                 // IGNORE on the unique name index, so two taps on a slow device
918:                 // cannot produce two categories with the same name.
919:                 if (dao.insertCategory(fresh) == -1L)
920:                     CategoryResult.DUPLICATE to dao.categoryByName(claim.name)?.id
921:                 else CategoryResult.CREATED to fresh.id
922:             }
923:         }
924:     }
925: 
926:     /** Rename in place. The id is what transactions point at, so renaming
927:      *  never has to touch — or risk orphaning — a single row that uses it. */
928:     suspend fun renameCategory(id: String, name: String): CategoryResult =
929:         withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
930:             val stored = dao.allCategories()
931:             when (val r = Categories.rename(id, name, stored.map { it.asRow() })) {
932:                 is Categories.Rename.To -> {
933:                     dao.updateCategory(stored.first { it.id == id }.copy(name = r.name))
934:                     CategoryResult.RENAMED
935:                 }
936:                 Categories.Rename.Unchanged -> CategoryResult.RENAMED
937:                 Categories.Rename.Taken -> CategoryResult.DUPLICATE
938:                 Categories.Rename.HeldByDeleted -> CategoryResult.HELD_BY_DELETED
939:                 Categories.Rename.Missing -> CategoryResult.GONE
940:                 Categories.Rename.Blank -> CategoryResult.INVALID
941:             }
942:         }
943: 
944:     /**
945:      * Delete a category, which archives it: see Categories in :core for why
946:      * the row is never removed. Not one transaction is touched — they stay
947:      * filed under it — and nothing new can be filed there until the name is
948:      * added again.
949:      *
950:      * @return false when there was no visible category with that id, so the
951:      *   caller does not announce a deletion that did not happen.
952:      */
953:     suspend fun deleteCategory(id: String): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
954:         val row = dao.allCategories().firstOrNull { it.id == id } ?: return@withContext false
955:         val archived = Categories.archive(row.asRow()) ?: return@withContext false
956:         dao.setArchived(archived.id, archived.isArchived, archived.budgetAmount) > 0
957:     }
958: 
959:     /** Null clears the budget, which is a different state from zero — see
960:      *  `Budget.amount` in :core. */
961:     suspend fun setBudget(categoryId: String, amount: Double?) =
962:         dao.setBudget(categoryId, amount)
963: 
964:     /** Read-modify-write of the one settings row, serialised against the
965:      *  restore path so neither can lose the other's change. */
966:     suspend fun updateSettings(mutate: (SettingsEntity) -> SettingsEntity) =
967:         withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
968:             settingsGate.withLock {
969:                 dao.putSettings(mutate(dao.settingsRow() ?: SettingsEntity()))
970:             }
971:         }
972: 
973:     /** Recorded so the introduction is shown once rather than every launch. */
974:     suspend fun markOnboardingSeen() = updateSettings { it.copy(onboardingSeen = true) }
975: 
976:     suspend fun clearUnreviewedCaptures(): Int = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
977:         dao.clearUnreviewedCaptures()
978:     }
979: 
980:     /** How many captures are waiting, for the badge that leads to them. A
981:      *  count query, so a screen that only shows the number does not load the
982:      *  rows behind it. */
983:     fun reviewCount(): Flow<Int> = dao.needingReviewCount()
984: 
985:     /**
986:      * Log a message the pipeline did not file, if [ParseMisses.judge] says
987:      * it is one the parser failed to read. Only its shape is written.
988:      *
989:      * Public for a paste flow that reads before it writes: a paste the user
990:      * was told cannot be read is the same evidence as one ingested directly.
991:      *
992:      * Never throws. This is diagnostics on the way out of the capture path,
993:      * and a full disk here must not turn "nothing was added" into a crash —
994:      * the result says whether the message is in the log, so no caller claims
995:      * a record that does not exist.
996:      */
997:     suspend fun recordUnreadable(
998:         sender: String?,
999:         body: String,
1000:         live: Boolean = true,
1001:         receivedAt: LocalDateTime = LocalDateTime.now(),
1002:         pasted: Boolean = false,
1003:         senderRow: SenderEntity? = null,
1004:     ): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
1005:         try {
1006:             val known = senderRow ?: sender?.let { dao.senderByName(Senders.normalizeSender(it)) }
1007:             val miss = ParseMisses.judge(
1008:                 source = if (pasted) ParseMisses.Source.PASTE else ParseMisses.Source.SMS,
1009:                 sender = sender,
1010:                 body = body,
1011:                 history = known?.let {
1012:                     ParseMisses.History(it.trust, it.capturedCount, it.confirmedCount)
1013:                 },
1014:             ) ?: return@withContext false
1015:             val at = millis(receivedAt)
1016:             misses.record(
1017:                 ParseMissEntity(
1018:                     id = newId(),
1019:                     bodyHash = miss.bodyHash,
1020:                     sender = miss.sender,
1021:                     source = if (miss.source == ParseMisses.Source.PASTE) "paste" else "sms",
1022:                     shape = miss.shape,
1023:                     reason = miss.reason,
1024:                     parserVersion = Parsing.PARSER_VERSION,
1025:                     firstSeenAt = at,
1026:                     lastSeenAt = at,
1027:                 ),
1028:                 live = live,
1029:                 keep = ParseMisses.MAX_ROWS,
1030:                 before = millis(ParseMisses.expiresBefore(LocalDateTime.now())),
1031:             )
1032:         } catch (e: CancellationException) {
1033:             throw e
1034:         } catch (e: Exception) {
1035:             false
1036:         }
1037:     }
1038: 
1039:     /** The unreadable formats, folded and ordered by :core. Folded off the
1040:      *  main thread: the screen collects this on it. */
1041:     fun unreadable(): Flow<List<ParseMisses.Group>> =
1042:         misses.showing(Parsing.PARSER_VERSION)
1043:             .map { rows -> ParseMisses.summarize(rows.map(::missRow)) }
1044:             .flowOn(Dispatchers.Default)
1045: 
1046:     /**
1047:      * What "Share" sends: shapes, counts and senders, nothing else.
1048:      *
1049:      * Nothing leaves the phone on its own. This returns a string; only the
1050:      * user picking a destination in the system share sheet moves it.
1051:      */
1052:     suspend fun unreadableExport(): String = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
1053:         ParseMisses.export(ParseMisses.summarize(
1054:             misses.showingNow(Parsing.PARSER_VERSION).map(::missRow)))
1055:     }
1056: 
1057:     /** Hidden, not deleted — see [ParseMissEntity] for why. The ledger is
1058:      *  not touched. */
1059:     suspend fun clearUnreadable(): Int = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { misses.clearAll() }
1060: 
1061:     private fun missRow(m: ParseMissEntity) = ParseMisses.Row(
1062:         m.sender, m.shape, m.reason, m.seenCount, localTime(m.lastSeenAt))
1063: 
1064:     /**
1065:      * The review queue as questions rather than rows.
1066:      *
1067:      * Grouping and the pre-filled suggestions are [Review] in :core, covered
1068:      * by its own JVM checks; this only supplies the rows and the model. It is
1069:      * a Flow so that answering one group removes it — the rows change, the
1070:      * queue re-emits, and the screen has one fewer question on it without
1071:      * anything having to remember to reload.
1072:      */
1073:     fun reviewQueue(): Flow<List<Review.Group>> = dao.needingReview().map { rows ->
1074:         withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
1075:             val groups = Review.group(rows.map { r ->
1076:                 Review.Item(r.id, r.amount, r.type, localTime(r.occurredAt),
1077:                     r.rawMerchant, r.merchantName, r.smsSender)
1078:             })
1079:             // Archived categories are excluded rather than merely unlikely:
1080:             // the model can still name one, and the picker below the
1081:             // suggestion cannot show it.
1082:             Review.suggest(groups, trainedModel(),
1083:                 dao.allCategories().filterNot { it.isArchived }.map { it.id }.toSet())
1084:         }
1085:     }
1086: 
1087:     /**
1088:      * One category for every capture from one payee, and the engine learns
1089:      * from it.
1090:      *
1091:      * The learning is the half that makes this worth more than a fast way to
1092:      * type the same thing twenty times: a confirmed group is that many
1093:      * pieces of evidence about the payee's amounts and hours, so the next
1094:      * message from them is filed without asking at all.
1095:      *
1096:      * @param newCategoryName used only when [categoryId] is null — the
1097:      *   category the user typed instead of picking, created if this is the
1098:      *   first time they have named it.
1099:      * @return rows actually written, which is what the user is told.
1100:      */
1101:     suspend fun categoriseReviewGroup(
1102:         group: Review.Group,
1103:         categoryId: String? = null,
1104:         newCategoryName: String? = null,
1105:     ): Int = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
1106:         val target = resolveCategory(categoryId, newCategoryName, group.type)
1107:             ?: return@withContext 0
1108:         // Blank means the captures resolved to no payee at all. Writing that
1109:         // as a name would replace "unknown" with "", which is the same
1110:         // absence spelled in a way the ledger cannot tell from a real name.
1111:         val name = group.key.ifBlank { null }
1112:         val written = dao.categoriseGroup(group.ids, target, name)
1113:         for (row in Review.teachFrom(group)) {
1114:             // teachFrom hands back only rows that carry one; the learning
1115:             // table is keyed on it.
1116:             val raw = row.rawMerchant ?: continue
1117:             recordConfirmation(
1118:                 rawMerchant = raw,
1119:                 merchantName = name ?: raw,
1120:                 categoryId = target,
1121:                 amount = row.amount,
1122:                 occurredAt = row.occurredAt,
1123:                 // Nobody had categorised these rows, so there is no earlier
1124:                 // answer for this to be a correction of.
1125:                 wasCorrection = false,
1126:             )
1127:         }
1128:         written
1129:     }
1130: 
1131:     /** "Not a transaction", for a whole group of junk from one sender. Soft,
1132:      *  like every other delete here. */
1133:     suspend fun discardReviewGroup(group: Review.Group): Int = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
1134:         dao.discardGroup(group.ids)
1135:     }
1136: 
1137:     /** The category a review decision lands in: the one tapped, or the one
1138:      *  typed — found if it already exists, created if it does not. */
1139:     private suspend fun resolveCategory(id: String?, typed: String?, type: String): String? {
1140:         // A tapped chip can name a category deleted since the screen drew it,
1141:         // and filing a group there is exactly what deleting it ruled out.
1142:         if (id != null) return Categories.fileable(id, dao.allCategories().map { it.asRow() })
1143:         // Capped at the length the picker can show. A category whose name
1144:         // does not fit on a chip is one the user cannot recognise later.
1145:         val clean = typed?.trim()?.take(MAX_CATEGORY_NAME).orEmpty()
1146:         if (clean.isEmpty()) return null
1147:         return claimCategory(clean, type).second
1148:     }
1149: 
1150:     /**
1151:      * The whole ledger as a backup file.
1152:      *
1153:      * Soft-deleted rows are included: dropping them would turn the undo
1154:      * behind every delete into a one-way door the moment the file was
1155:      * restored, and their ids are what stops a re-import resurrecting
1156:      * something the user threw away.
1157:      */
1158:     suspend fun backupText(now: LocalDateTime = LocalDateTime.now()): String =
1159:         withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
1160:             val prefs = dao.settingsRow() ?: SettingsEntity()
1161:             Backup.write(Backup.Document(
1162:                 createdAt = Backup.stamp(now),
1163:                 categories = dao.allCategories().map { c ->
1164:                     Backup.Category(c.id, c.name, c.type, c.icon, c.color,
1165:                         c.budgetAmount, c.isArchived)
1166:                 },
1167:                 merchants = dao.allMerchants().map { Backup.Merchant(it.id, it.canonicalName, it.categoryId) },
1168:                 learning = dao.allLearning().map { l ->
1169:                     Backup.Learned(l.id, l.rawName, l.merchantName, l.categoryId,
1170:                         l.confirmationCount, l.correctionCount, l.sampleCount, l.avgAmount,
1171:                         l.amountMin, l.amountMax, l.hourHistogram, l.lastSeenAt?.let { localTime(it) })
1172:                 },
1173:                 transactions = dao.everyTransaction().map { t ->
1174:                     Backup.Txn(t.id, t.amount, t.type, localTime(t.occurredAt),
1175:                         localTime(t.createdAt), t.categoryId, t.rawMerchant, t.merchantName,
1176:                         t.notes, t.referenceNumber, t.source, t.confidence, t.status,
1177:                         t.categoryPrompted, t.dedupKey, t.isDeleted)
1178:                 },
1179:                 senders = dao.allSenders().map { s ->
1180:                     Backup.Sender(s.id, s.sender, localTime(s.firstSeenAt), localTime(s.lastSeenAt),
1181:                         s.display, s.kind, s.entity, s.bank, s.trust, s.messageCount,
1182:                         s.capturedCount, s.confirmedCount, s.quarantinedCount, s.lastRisk)
1183:                 },
1184:                 prefs = Backup.Prefs(prefs.currency, prefs.theme, prefs.autoSaveThreshold,
1185:                     prefs.confirmThreshold, prefs.highValueAmount),
1186:             ))
1187:         }
1188: 
1189:     /** Validation only. Nothing is written until [applyRestore], so the user
1190:      *  can be told what is in the file before deciding. */
1191:     suspend fun readBackup(bytes: ByteArray): Backup.Read = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
1192:         Backup.read(bytes)
1193:     }
1194: 
1195:     /**
1196:      * Land a validated backup.
1197:      *
1198:      * :core decides WHAT lands — [Backup.plan] filters out everything this
1199:      * ledger already holds — and its KDoc says it cannot enforce HOW. That
1200:      * guarantee is [SpendDao.applyBackup]: one Room `@Transaction`, in the
1201:      * plan's own order, so a restore interrupted by a crash, a kill or a flat
1202:      * battery leaves the ledger the user already had rather than half of two.
1203:      *
1204:      * The plan is returned so the caller can say what actually happened
1205:      * instead of "done".
1206:      */
1207:     suspend fun applyRestore(doc: Backup.Document, replace: Boolean): Backup.Plan =
1208:         withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
1209:             settingsGate.withLock {
1210:                 val plan = Backup.plan(doc, existingKeys(), replace)
1211:                 val current = dao.settingsRow() ?: SettingsEntity()
1212:                 dao.applyBackup(
1213:                     clearFirst = plan.clearFirst,
1214:                     categories = plan.categories.map { c ->
1215:                         CategoryEntity(c.id, c.name, c.type, c.icon, c.color,
1216:                             c.budgetAmount, c.isArchived)
1217:                     },
1218:                     merchants = plan.merchants.map { MerchantEntity(it.id, it.canonicalName, it.categoryId) },
1219:                     learning = plan.learning.map { l ->
1220:                         LearningEntity(l.id, l.rawName, l.merchantName, l.categoryId,
1221:                             l.confirmationCount, l.correctionCount, l.sampleCount, l.avgAmount,
1222:                             l.amountMin, l.amountMax, l.hourHistogram, l.lastSeenAt?.let { millis(it) })
1223:                     },
1224:                     transactions = plan.transactions.map { t ->
1225:                         TransactionEntity(
1226:                             id = t.id, amount = t.amount, type = t.type,
1227:                             categoryId = t.categoryId, rawMerchant = t.rawMerchant,
1228:                             merchantName = t.merchantName, notes = t.notes,
1229:                             referenceNumber = t.referenceNumber,
1230:                             occurredAt = millis(t.occurredAt), source = t.source,
1231:                             confidence = t.confidence, status = t.status,
1232:                             categoryPrompted = t.categoryPrompted, dedupKey = t.dedupKey,
1233:                             // Not carried by the format: a restored row keeps
1234:                             // its dedup key, which is what identifies the
1235:                             // purchase, but not the header it arrived under.
1236:                             smsSender = null,
1237:                             isDeleted = t.isDeleted, createdAt = millis(t.createdAt),
1238:                         )
1239:                     },
1240:                     senders = plan.senders.map { s ->
1241:                         SenderEntity(s.id, s.sender, s.display, s.kind, s.entity, s.bank,
1242:                             s.trust, s.messageCount, s.capturedCount, s.confirmedCount,
1243:                             s.quarantinedCount, s.lastRisk, millis(s.firstSeenAt),
1244:                             millis(s.lastSeenAt))
1245:                     },
1246:                     // A null field in the file means "the file carried no
1247:                     // usable value", so the device keeps what it has. Whether
1248:                     // onboarding was seen is never taken from a file: the
1249:                     // person holding the phone has plainly been through it.
1250:                     settings = plan.prefs?.let { p ->
1251:                         current.copy(
1252:                             currency = p.currency ?: current.currency,
1253:                             theme = p.theme ?: current.theme,
1254:                             autoSaveThreshold = p.autoSaveThreshold ?: current.autoSaveThreshold,
1255:                             confirmThreshold = p.confirmThreshold ?: current.confirmThreshold,
1256:                             highValueAmount = p.highValueAmount ?: current.highValueAmount,
1257:                         )
1258:                     },
1259:                 )
1260:                 plan
1261:             }
1262:         }
1263: 
1264:     /** The keys already here, so the plan can skip what would collide. The
1265:      *  natural keys go in as well as the ids because the unique indexes
1266:      *  refuse a duplicate name whatever id it arrives under. */
1267:     private suspend fun existingKeys(): Backup.Existing {
1268:         val txs = dao.everyTransaction()
1269:         val cats = dao.allCategories()
1270:         val merchants = dao.allMerchants()
1271:         val learning = dao.allLearning()
1272:         val senders = dao.allSenders()
1273:         return Backup.Existing(
1274:             transactionIds = txs.mapTo(HashSet()) { it.id },
1275:             dedupKeys = txs.mapNotNullTo(HashSet()) { it.dedupKey },
1276:             categoryIds = cats.mapTo(HashSet()) { it.id },
1277:             categoryNames = cats.mapTo(HashSet()) { it.name },
1278:             merchantIds = merchants.mapTo(HashSet()) { it.id },
1279:             merchantNames = merchants.mapTo(HashSet()) { it.canonicalName },
1280:             learningIds = learning.mapTo(HashSet()) { it.id },
1281:             learningRawNames = learning.mapTo(HashSet()) { it.rawName },
1282:             senderIds = senders.mapTo(HashSet()) { it.id },
1283:             senderNames = senders.mapTo(HashSet()) { it.sender },
1284:         )
1285:     }
1286: 
1287:     /**
1288:      * A spreadsheet, not a backup.
1289:      *
1290:      * Deliberately lossy — no learning, no senders, no dedup keys — which is
1291:      * exactly why the settings screen calls it "a report for a spreadsheet"
1292:      * and offers it beside the backup rather than instead of it.
1293:      */
1294:     suspend fun csvExport(): String = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
1295:         val names = dao.allCategories().associateBy({ it.id }, { it.name })
1296:         buildString {
1297:             append("date,type,amount,merchant,category,notes,reference,source,status\n")
1298:             for (t in dao.all()) {
1299:                 append(localTime(t.occurredAt).format(CsvDate)).append(',')
1300:                 append(t.type).append(',')
1301:                 append(csvAmount(t.amount)).append(',')
1302:                 append(csvCell(t.merchantName ?: t.rawMerchant)).append(',')
1303:                 append(csvCell(t.categoryId?.let { names[it] })).append(',')
1304:                 append(csvCell(t.notes)).append(',')
1305:                 append(csvCell(t.referenceNumber)).append(',')
1306:                 append(t.source).append(',')
1307:                 append(t.status).append('\n')
1308:             }
1309:         }
1310:     }
1311: 
1312:     private fun parseHistogram(raw: String): List<Int> =
1313:         raw.split(",").mapNotNull { it.trim().toIntOrNull() }
1314: 
1315:     private fun minOfNonZero(existing: Double?, amount: Double?): Double = when {
1316:         amount == null -> existing ?: 0.0
1317:         existing == null || existing == 0.0 -> amount
1318:         else -> minOf(existing, amount)
1319:     }
1320: 
1321:     private fun titleCase(s: String): String = s.split(" ")
1322:         .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
1323:         .joinToString(" ") { w -> w.lowercase().replaceFirstChar { it.uppercase() } }
1324: }

1326: /** A stored category as the rules in :core's Categories read it. */
1327: fun CategoryEntity.asRow(): Categories.Row =
1328:     Categories.Row(id = id, name = name, type = type, budgetAmount = budgetAmount,
1329:         isArchived = isArchived)

1: package com.jeevavibeapp.spendwise.data

3: import androidx.room.*
4: import androidx.room.migration.Migration

8: /**
9:  * The whole database. Room over SQLite, opened directly in-process.
10:  *
11:  * The app previously reached its own data by making an HTTP request to a
12:  * Python web server running on a loopback port inside itself. Every screen
13:  * cost a socket round-trip and a WSGI dispatch, and the server not starting
14:  * was a class of failure that could take the entire app down. A DAO call is
15:  * a function call.
16:  *
17:  * No foreign keys, deliberately. Three tables carry a `categoryId` pointing
18:  * at [CategoryEntity], and declaring the constraint would mean a restore that
19:  * empties `categories` while rows still reference them aborts the whole
20:  * transaction — losing the ledger to protect a reference. Backup.plan in
21:  * :core already rewrites any categoryId it cannot resolve to null, so a
22:  * dangling reference cannot enter the database in the first place, and the
23:  * screens treat an unknown category as uncategorised rather than an error.
24:  */
25: 
26: @Entity(
27:     tableName = "transactions",
28:     indices = [
29:         Index(value = ["occurredAt"]),
30:         Index(value = ["merchantName"]),
31:         Index(value = ["categoryId"]),
32:         // The dedup key is what stops one purchase becoming two rows when a
33:         // bank sends both an SMS and a UPI confirmation.
34:         Index(value = ["dedupKey"], unique = true),
35:     ],
36: )
37: data class TransactionEntity(
38:     @PrimaryKey val id: String,
39:     val amount: Double,
40:     val type: String,                       // "expense" | "income"
41:     val categoryId: String? = null,
42:     val rawMerchant: String? = null,
43:     val merchantName: String? = null,
44:     val notes: String? = null,
45:     val referenceNumber: String? = null,
46:     /** Epoch millis, device-local wall clock. */
47:     val occurredAt: Long,
48:     val source: String = "manual",          // manual | sms | import | restore
49:     val confidence: Int? = null,
50:     val status: String = "confirmed",       // confirmed | pending_confirmation | needs_review
51:     val categoryPrompted: Boolean = false,
52:     val dedupKey: String? = null,
53:     val smsSender: String? = null,
54:     /** Soft delete. Nothing is ever really removed, so undo always works. */
55:     val isDeleted: Boolean = false,
56:     val createdAt: Long,
57: )

113: /**
114:  * Every preference, in one row.
115:  *
116:  * Room rather than DataStore: the app already has a database, and a second
117:  * storage mechanism would mean a restore that is atomic in one of them and
118:  * not the other — the settings a backup carries would land outside the
119:  * transaction that lands the ledger.
120:  *
121:  * [id] is pinned to [SettingsEntity.ROW] so writing settings is an upsert of
122:  * one known row rather than a read-modify-write that can race with itself.
123:  */
124: @Entity(tableName = "settings")
125: data class SettingsEntity(
126:     @PrimaryKey val id: Int = ROW,
127:     /** Set the first time the introduction is dismissed, so it is shown
128:      *  once rather than on every launch. */
129:     val onboardingSeen: Boolean = false,
130:     val currency: String = "INR",
131:     val theme: String = "system",
132:     /** The two gates Pipeline.ingest is given. Defaulted to the values the
133:      *  core module itself defaults to, so an untouched install behaves
134:      *  exactly as the JVM tests describe. */
135:     val autoSaveThreshold: Int = 80,
136:     val confirmThreshold: Int = 50,
137:     val highValueAmount: Double? = null,
138: ) {
139:     companion object { const val ROW = 1 }
140: }

142: /**
143:  * A fraud signal [com.jeevavibeapp.spendwise.core.Fraud] raised about one
144:  * transaction, and whether the user has looked at it.
145:  *
146:  * [alertKey] is the claim's identity, not the row's — "this transaction is a
147:  * duplicate", "this day was heavy" — and it is unique. Inserting with IGNORE
148:  * therefore makes raising the same signal twice a no-op, which is what keeps
149:  * an inbox rescan, a restore or a fifth charge on an already-flagged day from
150:  * filling the screen with copies of one sentence.
151:  */
152: @Entity(
153:     tableName = "fraud_alerts",
154:     indices = [
155:         Index(value = ["alertKey"], unique = true),
156:         Index(value = ["status"]),
157:     ],

161:     val alertKey: String,
162:     val transactionId: String,
163:     val kind: String,
164:     val severity: String,
165:     val message: String,
166:     /** open | dismissed. Dismissed rows are kept rather than deleted, so the
167:      *  unique key still refuses to raise a signal the user already answered. */
168:     val status: String = "open",
169:     val createdAt: Long,
170: )

185:     val seenCount: Int = 1,
