189: 
190: /**
191:  * An abstract class rather than an interface, because of [applyBackup].
192:  *
193:  * A `@Transaction` method with a body has to be one Room can override. On an
194:  * abstract class Room subclasses it and calls `super`; on a Kotlin interface
195:  * it has to reach for the `DefaultImpls` the compiler may or may not emit
196:  * depending on the `-Xjvm-default` mode in force. The restore is the one
197:  * place in this app where losing the transaction wrapper would be
198:  * unrecoverable, so it does not get to depend on a compiler flag.
199:  */
200: /** Ids per statement in a bulk write. Comfortably under SQLite's 999-variable
201:  *  ceiling on older Android, with room for the other bound arguments. */
202: private const val BULK_IDS = 500
203: 
204: @Dao
205: abstract class SpendDao {
206: 
207:     // Flows, so a screen re-renders when the data changes rather than when
208:     // something remembers to refresh it.
209: 
210:     /** The whole ledger, not a page of it. The report, the forecast and the
211:      *  six-month cash flow are computed from whatever this emits, so a LIMIT
212:      *  here would not shorten a list — it would silently change the
213:      *  arithmetic on every screen that aggregates. */
214:     @Query("SELECT * FROM transactions WHERE isDeleted = 0 ORDER BY occurredAt DESC")
215:     abstract fun stream(): Flow<List<TransactionEntity>>
216: 
217:     @Query("SELECT * FROM transactions WHERE isDeleted = 0 ORDER BY occurredAt DESC")
218:     abstract suspend fun all(): List<TransactionEntity>
219: 
220:     /** Soft-deleted rows included. A backup that dropped them would turn the
221:      *  undo behind every delete into a one-way door the moment the ledger
222:      *  was restored, and their ids are what stops a re-import resurrecting
223:      *  something the user threw away. */
224:     @Query("SELECT * FROM transactions ORDER BY occurredAt DESC")
225:     abstract suspend fun everyTransaction(): List<TransactionEntity>
226: 
227:     @Query("SELECT * FROM transactions WHERE id = :id")
228:     abstract suspend fun byId(id: String): TransactionEntity?
229: 
230:     /** One indexed lookup against the unique dedup key. Insert already
231:      *  refuses a collision, but knowing BEFORE the work is what makes
232:      *  rescanning an inbox cheap instead of merely harmless. */
233:     @Query("SELECT id FROM transactions WHERE dedupKey = :key LIMIT 1")
234:     abstract suspend fun idByDedupKey(key: String): String?
235: 
236:     /** Search across merchant, notes and reference in one pass. */
237:     @Query("""
238:         SELECT * FROM transactions
239:         WHERE isDeleted = 0 AND (
240:             merchantName LIKE '%' || :q || '%' OR
241:             rawMerchant  LIKE '%' || :q || '%' OR
242:             notes        LIKE '%' || :q || '%' OR
243:             referenceNumber LIKE '%' || :q || '%')
244:         ORDER BY occurredAt DESC LIMIT 200""")
245:     abstract fun search(q: String): Flow<List<TransactionEntity>>
246: 
247:     /**
248:      * The review queue: captures the app is not sure it got right.
249:      *
250:      * Uncategorised counts as unreviewed even when confidence was high enough
251:      * to save the row without asking — a transaction filed under no category
252:      * is missing from every budget and every report, which is the same
253:      * unfinished state as one that is waiting to be confirmed.
254:      *
255:      * Captures only. A hand-entered row with no category is a choice the user
256:      * made in the sheet, and putting it in a queue that asks them to fix it
257:      * would be the app arguing with them.
258:      */
259:     @Query("""
260:         SELECT * FROM transactions
261:         WHERE isDeleted = 0 AND source = 'sms'
262:           AND (categoryId IS NULL OR status != 'confirmed')
263:         ORDER BY occurredAt DESC""")
264:     abstract fun needingReview(): Flow<List<TransactionEntity>>
265: 
266:     /** The same set, counted. Read on every screen for the badge, so it does
267:      *  not pay for loading the rows it is counting. */
268:     @Query("""
269:         SELECT COUNT(*) FROM transactions
270:         WHERE isDeleted = 0 AND source = 'sms'
271:           AND (categoryId IS NULL OR status != 'confirmed')""")
272:     abstract fun needingReviewCount(): Flow<Int>
273: 
274:     /**
275:      * One decision, applied to every row of a reviewed group.
276:      *
277:      * `merchantName` names the whole group at once — rows that only ever had
278:      * the bank's raw string get the payee's name here, which is what makes
279:      * the group hold together the next time it is looked at.
280:      *
281:      * Chunked, because SQLite binds one host variable per id and the limit on
282:      * older Android is 999: a payee with a thousand captures is exactly the
283:      * user this screen exists for, and they must not be the one it fails on.
284:      */
285:     @Transaction
286:     open suspend fun categoriseGroup(ids: List<String>, categoryId: String,
287:                                      merchantName: String?): Int =
288:         ids.chunked(BULK_IDS).sumOf { categoriseRows(it, categoryId, merchantName) }
289: 
290:     @Query("""
291:         UPDATE transactions
292:         SET categoryId = :categoryId, categoryPrompted = 1, status = 'confirmed',
293:             merchantName = COALESCE(:merchantName, merchantName)
294:         WHERE id IN (:ids)""")
295:     abstract suspend fun categoriseRows(ids: List<String>, categoryId: String,
296:                                         merchantName: String?): Int
297: 
298:     /** "Not a transaction", for a whole group. Soft, like every other delete
299:      *  here, and chunked for the same reason as [categoriseGroup]. */
300:     @Transaction
301:     open suspend fun discardGroup(ids: List<String>): Int =
302:         ids.chunked(BULK_IDS).sumOf { discardRows(it) }
303: 
304:     @Query("UPDATE transactions SET isDeleted = 1 WHERE id IN (:ids)")
305:     abstract suspend fun discardRows(ids: List<String>): Int
306: 
307:     /** IGNORE, not REPLACE: the unique dedupKey is what makes re-ingesting
308:      *  the same bank message harmless, and REPLACE would overwrite a row the
309:      *  user had already corrected. */
310:     @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.IGNORE)
311:     abstract suspend fun insert(tx: TransactionEntity): Long
312: 
313:     @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.IGNORE)
314:     abstract suspend fun insertTransactions(items: List<TransactionEntity>)
315: 
316:     @Update abstract suspend fun update(tx: TransactionEntity)
317: 
318:     @Query("UPDATE transactions SET isDeleted = :deleted WHERE id = :id")
319:     abstract suspend fun setDeleted(id: String, deleted: Boolean)
320: 
321:     /** Junk captured before the filtering improved. Soft, like every other
322:      *  delete here, and confirmed rows are left alone: the offer the user
323:      *  accepted was to clear what they have NOT reviewed. */
324:     @Query("""
325:         UPDATE transactions SET isDeleted = 1
326:         WHERE isDeleted = 0 AND source = 'sms' AND status != 'confirmed'""")
327:     abstract suspend fun clearUnreviewedCaptures(): Int
328: 
329:     @Query("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(amount), 0) FROM transactions WHERE isDeleted = 0 AND type = :type AND occurredAt >= :from AND occurredAt < :to")
330:     abstract fun totalBetween(type: String, from: Long, to: Long): Flow<Double>
331: 
332:     @Query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM transactions WHERE isDeleted = 0")
333:     abstract fun count(): Flow<Int>
334: 
335:     /** The categories anything new may be filed under. A deleted category is
336:      *  archived rather than removed — see Categories in :core — so this
337:      *  filter is what deleting one actually changes on screen. */
338:     @Query("SELECT * FROM categories WHERE isArchived = 0 ORDER BY name")
339:     abstract fun categories(): Flow<List<CategoryEntity>>
340: 
341:     /** Deleted ones included. A category deleted after things were filed
342:      *  under it still names them — on the report, on their edit sheet — it is
343:      *  only no longer offered for anything new. */
344:     @Query("SELECT * FROM categories ORDER BY name")
345:     abstract fun everyCategory(): Flow<List<CategoryEntity>>
346: 
347:     @Query("SELECT * FROM categories")
348:     abstract suspend fun allCategories(): List<CategoryEntity>
349: 
350:     /** Deleting a category, or bringing one back: the flag and the budget
351:      *  that goes with it, in one statement. Name, type and colour are left
352:      *  as stored, so a row read a moment earlier is never written back over
353:      *  a change made since. Returns the rows matched — zero means the
354:      *  category is gone, and the caller must not say it was changed. */
355:     @Query("UPDATE categories SET isArchived = :archived, budgetAmount = :budget WHERE id = :id")
356:     abstract suspend fun setArchived(id: String, archived: Boolean, budget: Double?): Int
357: 
358:     @Query("SELECT * FROM categories WHERE name = :name LIMIT 1")
359:     abstract suspend fun categoryByName(name: String): CategoryEntity?
360: 
361:     @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.IGNORE)
362:     abstract suspend fun insertCategories(items: List<CategoryEntity>)
363: 
364:     @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.IGNORE)
365:     abstract suspend fun insertCategory(c: CategoryEntity): Long
366: 
367:     @Update abstract suspend fun updateCategory(c: CategoryEntity)
368: 
369:     /** Null clears the budget. That is a different state from zero — see
370:      *  Budget.amount in :core — so the column is nullable and this query
371:      *  writes the null rather than a 0.0 nobody could ever be under. */
372:     @Query("UPDATE categories SET budgetAmount = :amount WHERE id = :id")
373:     abstract suspend fun setBudget(id: String, amount: Double?)
374: 
375:     @Query("SELECT * FROM merchants WHERE canonicalName = :name LIMIT 1")
376:     abstract suspend fun merchantByName(name: String): MerchantEntity?
377: 
378:     @Query("SELECT * FROM merchants")
379:     abstract suspend fun allMerchants(): List<MerchantEntity>
380: 
381:     @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.IGNORE)
382:     abstract suspend fun insertMerchant(m: MerchantEntity)
383: 
384:     @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.IGNORE)
385:     abstract suspend fun insertMerchants(items: List<MerchantEntity>)
386: 
387:     @Query("SELECT * FROM learning WHERE rawName = :rawName LIMIT 1")
388:     abstract suspend fun learningFor(rawName: String): LearningEntity?
389: 
390:     @Query("SELECT * FROM learning")
391:     abstract suspend fun allLearning(): List<LearningEntity>
392: 
393:     @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
394:     abstract suspend fun upsertLearning(l: LearningEntity)
395: 
396:     @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.IGNORE)
397:     abstract suspend fun insertLearning(items: List<LearningEntity>)
398: 
399:     /**
400:      * One merchant's learning: the rows that name it, and the rows keyed on a
401:      * raw name its payments arrived under, which may name someone else.
402:      *
403:      * A Flow, so forgetting or changing a row redraws the card that offered
404:      * it. No index on merchantName: the table holds one row per payee
405:      * spelling, so scanning it is cheaper than maintaining an index on every
406:      * capture that teaches it.
407:      */
408:     @Query("SELECT * FROM learning WHERE merchantName = :name OR rawName IN (:rawNames)")
409:     abstract fun learningAbout(name: String, rawNames: List<String>): Flow<List<LearningEntity>>
410: 
411:     /** The same rows, read at the moment of writing, so a change is planned
412:      *  against the table as it is rather than the screen's last copy of it. */
413:     @Query("SELECT * FROM learning WHERE merchantName = :name OR rawName IN (:rawNames)")
414:     abstract suspend fun learningAboutNow(name: String, rawNames: List<String>): List<LearningEntity>
415: 
416:     @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
417:     abstract suspend fun upsertLearningRows(items: List<LearningEntity>)
418: 
419:     @Query("DELETE FROM learning WHERE rawName = :rawName")
420:     abstract suspend fun deleteLearning(rawName: String): Int
421: 
422:     /** One mapping removed, and handed back as it was so an undo can put the
423:      *  same row back rather than a rebuilt one with its counts reset. */
424:     @Transaction
425:     open suspend fun forgetLearning(rawName: String): LearningEntity? {
426:         val row = learningFor(rawName) ?: return null
427:         deleteLearning(rawName)
428:         return row
429:     }
430: 
431:     /**
432:      * "File this merchant's new payments under…", and whatever past payments
433:      * the user ticked, in one transaction.
434:      *
435:      * Taught rows go in with IGNORE: a capture that taught the same spelling
436:      * a moment ago wins, rather than being overwritten by a row with no
437:      * evidence in it.
438:      */
439:     @Transaction
440:     open suspend fun relearnMerchant(
441:         name: String,
442:         changed: List<LearningEntity>,
443:         taught: List<LearningEntity>,
444:         refileIds: List<String>,
445:         categoryId: String,
446:     ): Int {
447:         upsertLearningRows(changed)
448:         insertLearning(taught)
449:         return refileIds.chunked(BULK_IDS).sumOf { refileRows(it, name, categoryId) }
450:     }
451: 
452:     /** The name is part of the WHERE so a stale or mistaken id cannot move a
453:      *  different merchant's payment: it matches only rows still listed under
454:      *  [name], the way the ledger lists them. */
455:     @Query("""
456:         UPDATE transactions SET categoryId = :categoryId
457:         WHERE isDeleted = 0 AND id IN (:ids)
458:           AND COALESCE(merchantName, rawMerchant) = :name""")
459:     abstract suspend fun refileRows(ids: List<String>, name: String, categoryId: String): Int
460: 
461:     @Query("SELECT * FROM sms_senders WHERE sender = :sender LIMIT 1")
462:     abstract suspend fun senderByName(sender: String): SenderEntity?
463: 
464:     @Query("SELECT * FROM sms_senders ORDER BY lastSeenAt DESC")
465:     abstract fun senders(): Flow<List<SenderEntity>>
466: 
467:     @Query("SELECT * FROM sms_senders")
468:     abstract suspend fun allSenders(): List<SenderEntity>
469: 
470:     @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
471:     abstract suspend fun upsertSender(s: SenderEntity)
472: 
473:     @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.IGNORE)
474:     abstract suspend fun insertSenders(items: List<SenderEntity>)
475: 
476:     /** The user said a message from this sender was real. Senders.assess
477:      *  reads that count back as evidence, so this is the whole feedback loop
478:      *  between the quarantine screen and the next message that arrives. */
479:     @Query("UPDATE sms_senders SET confirmedCount = confirmedCount + 1 WHERE sender = :sender")
480:     abstract suspend fun noteSenderConfirmed(sender: String)
481: 
482:     /** The user's own verdict. Written only through [Repo.setSenderTrust],
483:      *  which holds it to the three values SenderBook allows — the capture
484:      *  path reads this column back and will not overwrite a decision.
485:      *
486:      *  Returns the rows the WHERE matched. An UPDATE that finds no row is not
487:      *  an error to SQLite, so without the count a key that addresses nothing
488:      *  is indistinguishable from a decision that was saved. */
489:     @Query("UPDATE sms_senders SET trust = :trust WHERE sender = :sender")
490:     abstract suspend fun setSenderTrust(sender: String, trust: String): Int
491: 
492:     @Query("SELECT * FROM fraud_alerts WHERE status = 'open' ORDER BY createdAt DESC")
493:     abstract fun openAlerts(): Flow<List<FraudAlertEntity>>
494: 
495:     /** IGNORE, so a signal already raised under the same key is dropped
496:      *  rather than duplicated — see [FraudAlertEntity]. */
497:     @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.IGNORE)
498:     abstract suspend fun insertAlerts(items: List<FraudAlertEntity>)
499: 
500:     @Query("UPDATE fraud_alerts SET status = 'dismissed' WHERE id = :id")
501:     abstract suspend fun dismissAlert(id: String)
502: 
503:     @Query("DELETE FROM fraud_alerts") abstract suspend fun deleteAllFraudAlerts()
504: 
505:     @Query("SELECT * FROM quarantine WHERE status = 'held' ORDER BY createdAt DESC")
506:     abstract fun held(): Flow<List<QuarantineEntity>>
507: 
508:     @Query("SELECT * FROM quarantine WHERE status = 'held' ORDER BY createdAt DESC")
509:     abstract suspend fun allHeld(): List<QuarantineEntity>
510: 
511:     @Query("SELECT * FROM quarantine WHERE id = :id LIMIT 1")
512:     abstract suspend fun quarantineById(id: String): QuarantineEntity?
513: 
514:     @Query("SELECT * FROM quarantine WHERE bodyHash = :hash LIMIT 1")
515:     abstract suspend fun quarantineByHash(hash: String): QuarantineEntity?
516: 
517:     @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
518:     abstract suspend fun upsertQuarantine(q: QuarantineEntity)
519: 
520:     @Query("UPDATE quarantine SET status = :status WHERE id = :id")
521:     abstract suspend fun setQuarantineStatus(id: String, status: String)
522: 
523:     /**
524:      * The user's "it's real" on a held message, banked.
525:      *
526:      * Both halves or neither: a row in the ledger with the message still
527:      * sitting in the alert list invites a second approval and a duplicate,
528:      * and a cleared alert with no transaction silently loses the purchase the
529:      * user just vouched for.
530:      *
531:      * Returns false when the dedup key was already present — the purchase is
532:      * in the ledger either way, so the message is still marked approved.
533:      */
534:     @Transaction
535:     open suspend fun bankHeldMessage(tx: TransactionEntity, quarantineId: String): Boolean {
536:         val inserted = insert(tx) != -1L
537:         setQuarantineStatus(quarantineId, "approved")
538:         return inserted
539:     }
540: 
541:     // Returned as a list rather than a nullable row because a table with no
542:     // row yet is the ordinary first-launch state, and the caller substitutes
543:     // the defaults.
544: 
545:     @Query("SELECT * FROM settings WHERE id = 1")
546:     abstract fun settingsStream(): Flow<List<SettingsEntity>>
547: 
548:     @Query("SELECT * FROM settings WHERE id = 1")
549:     abstract suspend fun settingsRow(): SettingsEntity?
550: 
551:     @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
552:     abstract suspend fun putSettings(s: SettingsEntity)
553: 
554:     @Query("DELETE FROM transactions") abstract suspend fun deleteAllTransactions()
555:     @Query("DELETE FROM categories") abstract suspend fun deleteAllCategories()
556:     @Query("DELETE FROM merchants") abstract suspend fun deleteAllMerchants()
557:     @Query("DELETE FROM learning") abstract suspend fun deleteAllLearning()
558:     @Query("DELETE FROM sms_senders") abstract suspend fun deleteAllSenders()
559: 
560:     /**
561:      * A whole restore, or none of it.
562:      *
563:      * :core validates the file and produces a plan but cannot enforce how it
564:      * lands — its KDoc says so and leaves the guarantee here. On a finance
565:      * app with no cloud copy a half-applied restore is the worst outcome
566:      * available, so every write is one transaction: an interrupted restore
567:      * rolls back to the ledger the user already had.
568:      *
569:      * The order is the plan's own: categories before the merchants, learning
570:      * and transactions that point at them, so a row never lands before the
571:      * category it references.
572:      *
573:      * IGNORE throughout. The plan already excluded everything this ledger
574:      * holds, so a collision here means the two disagree — and dropping the
575:      * incoming row is the only resolution that cannot destroy something the
576:      * user still has.
577:      *
578:      * Every statement below is a plain DAO call on this coroutine. Room
579:      * carries the open transaction in the coroutine context, so anything that
580:      * left it — a `runBlocking`, a hand-rolled thread — would run its writes
581:      * OUTSIDE the transaction and quietly reintroduce the half-applied
582:      * restore this method exists to prevent.
583:      */
584:     @Transaction
585:     open suspend fun applyBackup(
586:         clearFirst: Boolean,
587:         categories: List<CategoryEntity>,
588:         merchants: List<MerchantEntity>,
589:         learning: List<LearningEntity>,
590:         transactions: List<TransactionEntity>,
591:         senders: List<SenderEntity>,
592:         settings: SettingsEntity?,
593:     ) {
594:         if (clearFirst) {
595:             // Quarantine is deliberately not cleared: a backup carries no
596:             // held messages, so emptying that table would delete the only
597:             // copy of something the file cannot put back.
598:             //
599:             // Fraud alerts are cleared, for the opposite reason. Every one of them is
600:             // a claim about a transaction that is about to be deleted, and an
601:             // alert left pointing at a row the restore replaced is a warning
602:             // about a purchase that is no longer there.
603:             deleteAllFraudAlerts()
604:             deleteAllSenders()
605:             deleteAllTransactions()
606:             deleteAllLearning()
607:             deleteAllMerchants()
608:             deleteAllCategories()
609:         }
610:         insertCategories(categories)
611:         insertMerchants(merchants)
612:         insertLearning(learning)
613:         insertTransactions(transactions)
614:         insertSenders(senders)
615:         settings?.let { putSettings(it) }
616:     }
617: }
618: 
619: @Database(
620:     entities = [TransactionEntity::class, CategoryEntity::class, MerchantEntity::class,
621:                 LearningEntity::class, SenderEntity::class, QuarantineEntity::class,
622:                 SettingsEntity::class, FraudAlertEntity::class, ParseMissEntity::class],
623:     version = 4,
624:     exportSchema = true,
625: )
626: abstract class SpendDatabase : RoomDatabase() {
627:     abstract fun dao(): SpendDao
628:     abstract fun misses(): ParseMissDao
629: }
630: 
631: /**
632:  * v1 → v2: preferences moved into the database.
633:  *
634:  * A real migration rather than a destructive fallback, because the fallback
635:  * would answer "we added a settings table" by deleting the ledger. The
636:  * statement has to match what Room generates for [SettingsEntity] column for
637:  * column — Room re-reads the table afterwards and refuses to open a database
638:  * the migration left in a shape it did not expect.
639:  */
640: val MIGRATION_1_2 = object : Migration(1, 2) {
641:     override fun migrate(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) {
642:         db.execSQL(
643:             "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `settings` (" +
644:                 "`id` INTEGER NOT NULL, " +
645:                 "`onboardingSeen` INTEGER NOT NULL, " +
646:                 "`currency` TEXT NOT NULL, " +
647:                 "`theme` TEXT NOT NULL, " +
648:                 "`autoSaveThreshold` INTEGER NOT NULL, " +
649:                 "`confirmThreshold` INTEGER NOT NULL, " +
650:                 "`highValueAmount` REAL, " +
651:                 "PRIMARY KEY(`id`))")
652:     }
653: }
654: 
655: /**
656:  * v2 → v3: fraud alerts.
657:  *
658:  * Additive — a new table and its indexes, nothing touched — but still a real
659:  * migration for the same reason [MIGRATION_1_2] is one: the destructive
660:  * fallback answers "we added a table" by deleting the ledger. The statements
661:  * have to match what Room generates for [FraudAlertEntity] exactly, indexes
662:  * included, or Room refuses to open the database it just upgraded.
663:  */
664: val MIGRATION_2_3 = object : Migration(2, 3) {
665:     override fun migrate(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) {
666:         db.execSQL(
667:             "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `fraud_alerts` (" +
668:                 "`id` TEXT NOT NULL, " +
669:                 "`alertKey` TEXT NOT NULL, " +
670:                 "`transactionId` TEXT NOT NULL, " +
671:                 "`kind` TEXT NOT NULL, " +
672:                 "`severity` TEXT NOT NULL, " +
673:                 "`message` TEXT NOT NULL, " +
674:                 "`status` TEXT NOT NULL, " +
675:                 "`createdAt` INTEGER NOT NULL, " +
676:                 "PRIMARY KEY(`id`))")
677:         db.execSQL(
678:             "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS `index_fraud_alerts_alertKey` " +
679:                 "ON `fraud_alerts` (`alertKey`)")
680:         db.execSQL(
681:             "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS `index_fraud_alerts_status` " +
682:                 "ON `fraud_alerts` (`status`)")
683:     }
684: }

12: import androidx.sqlite.db.framework.FrameworkSQLiteOpenHelperFactory

14: import com.jeevavibeapp.spendwise.core.RecoverySequence
15: import com.jeevavibeapp.spendwise.sms.InboxScanner

17: import java.io.FileInputStream
18: import java.io.FileOutputStream

20: import java.time.LocalDateTime

23: /**
24:  * Wiring. Deliberately a plain object rather than a DI framework: this app
25:  * has one database, one repository and one scanner, and a container would be
26:  * more machinery than the thing it manages.
27:  *
28:  * Every way into the ledger — the launcher, the SMS receiver, the retry
29:  * button — opens it through [RecoverySequence], because the alternative was
30:  * an app that crashed on every launch after one bad write, with the user's
31:  * whole history still on the phone and no way to be told so.
32:  */
33: object AppGraph {
34: 
35:     /** A result rather than a database or an exception, so a caller cannot
36:      *  reach the ledger without first deciding what to do when there is none. */
37:     sealed interface Ledger {
38:         class Ready(val repo: Repo) : Ledger
39:         class Unavailable(val stopped: RecoverySequence.Stopped, val where: Where) : Ledger
40:     }
41: 
42:     /** Absolute paths, because the one thing a person facing a failure screen
43:      *  must be able to pass on is where their data still is. */
44:     class Where(val ledger: String, val snapshots: String, val kept: List<String>)
45: 
46:     /** Read back from disk, not passed along in memory, because whoever
47:      *  recovered may have had no screen to say it on. */
48:     class Notice(
49:         val outcome: Recovery.Outcome,
50:         val restoredFrom: String?,
51:         val withoutLoss: Boolean,
52:         val where: Where,
53:     )
54: 
55:     class LedgerUnavailable(val stopped: RecoverySequence.Stopped) :
56:         IllegalStateException("the ledger could not be opened: ${stopped.outcome}")
57: 
58:     @Volatile private var db: SpendDatabase? = null
59:     @Volatile private var repoRef: Repo? = null
60:     @Volatile private var safeMode: Ledger.Unavailable? = null
61: 
62:     /** Blocks for as long as a recovery takes, so never on the main thread. */
63:     fun open(context: Context): Ledger {
64:         repoRef?.let { return Ledger.Ready(it) }
65:         return synchronized(this) {
66:             repoRef?.let { Ledger.Ready(it) } ?: safeMode ?: openNow(context.applicationContext)
67:         }
68:     }
69: 
70:     /** The failure screen's button. Safe mode is otherwise held until this is
71:      *  pressed — see [openNow] for why. */
72:     fun retry(context: Context): Ledger = synchronized(this) {
73:         safeMode = null
74:         open(context)
75:     }
76: 
77:     fun database(context: Context): SpendDatabase {
78:         val ledger = open(context)
79:         if (ledger is Ledger.Unavailable) throw LedgerUnavailable(ledger.stopped)
80:         return checkNotNull(db)
81:     }
82: 
83:     /** Throws [LedgerUnavailable] rather than handing out a repository over a
84:      *  database that never opened; the SMS receiver already catches it, and
85:      *  the message stays in the inbox for the next launch's scan. */
86:     fun repo(context: Context): Repo = when (val ledger = open(context)) {
87:         is Ledger.Ready -> ledger.repo
88:         is Ledger.Unavailable -> throw LedgerUnavailable(ledger.stopped)
89:     }
90: 
91:     fun inboxScanner(context: Context): InboxScanner =
92:         InboxScanner(context.applicationContext, repo(context))
93: 
94:     fun notice(context: Context): Notice? = LedgerDisk(context.applicationContext).readNotice()
95: 
96:     fun dismissNotice(context: Context) = LedgerDisk(context.applicationContext).clearNotice()
97: 
98:     private fun openNow(app: Context): Ledger {
99:         val disk = LedgerDisk(app)
100:         val result = try {
101:             RecoverySequence.run(disk, LEDGER, SCHEMA, LocalDateTime.now())
102:         } catch (t: Throwable) {
103:             // A bug on the Android side of the sequence. Thrown from here it
104:             // would be the crash on every launch this whole path replaces.
105:             Log.e(TAG, "recovery sequence failed", t)
106:             RecoverySequence.Stopped(Recovery.Outcome.SAFE_MODE, Recovery.Failure.UNKNOWN,
107:                 describe(t), null, emptyList(), emptyList())
108:         }
109:         return when (result) {
110:             is RecoverySequence.Opened -> {
111:                 // Written down rather than held: the SMS receiver can be the
112:                 // one that recovered, with no screen up and a process that
113:                 // may be gone before the user next opens the app.
114:                 if (result.outcome != Recovery.Outcome.NORMAL) disk.recordNotice(result)
115:                 val opened = checkNotNull(disk.opened)
116:                 db = opened
117:                 Ledger.Ready(Repo(opened.dao(), opened.misses()).also { repoRef = it })
118:             }
119:             is RecoverySequence.Stopped -> {
120:                 val unavailable = Ledger.Unavailable(result, disk.where(result.keptAs))
121:                 // Busy clears by itself, so the next caller simply tries
122:                 // again. Anything else waits for the button: each run of a
123:                 // failing migration sets another full copy of the ledger
124:                 // aside, and an SMS every few minutes would fill the phone.
125:                 if (result.outcome == Recovery.Outcome.SAFE_MODE) safeMode = unavailable
126:                 unavailable
127:             }
128:         }
129:     }
130: }
131: 
132: private const val LEDGER = "spendwise.db"
133: private const val TAG = "SpendWiseLedger"
134: 
135: /** Declaring a migration is not enough — Room only runs the ones it is
136:  *  handed, and refuses to open a database it has no path for. Without these
137:  *  a v1 install crashes on first open instead of upgrading. There is no
138:  *  destructive fallback: there is no cloud copy to restore from, so every
139:  *  version must ship a real migration. */
140: private val MIGRATIONS = arrayOf(MIGRATION_1_2, MIGRATION_2_3, MIGRATION_3_4)
141: 
142: /** The schema this build writes, read off the migrations rather than
143:  *  restated: Room's own number cannot be read until the open it is needed to
144:  *  prepare for, and a second copy of it would drift. */
145: private val SCHEMA = MIGRATIONS.maxOf { it.endVersion }
146: 
147: /** A journal or log belongs to the file it sits beside, and is moved or
148:  *  removed with it. */
149: private val SIDE_FILES = listOf("-wal", "-shm", "-journal")
150: 
151: /** Android's default handler deletes a database it finds corrupt. Here the
152:  *  file is the only copy of someone's finances, so damage only raises, and
153:  *  what happens to the file is RecoverySequence's decision. */
154: private val KEEP_FILE = DatabaseErrorHandler {
155:     Log.e(TAG, "SQLite reported corruption; the file is kept for recovery")
156: }
157: 
158: /**
159:  * Room's answer to a corrupt file is SupportSQLiteOpenHelper.Callback's
160:  * default onCorruption: delete it, and carry on with an empty database.
161:  * That is silent total loss on exactly the event recovery exists for, and it
162:  * would happen inside Room before RecoverySequence ever saw a failure. So
163:  * every callback goes through to Room unchanged except that one.
164:  */
165: private object KeepDamagedFiles : SupportSQLiteOpenHelper.Factory {
166:     override fun create(
167:         configuration: SupportSQLiteOpenHelper.Configuration,
168:     ): SupportSQLiteOpenHelper {
169:         val room = configuration.callback
170:         val keeping = object : SupportSQLiteOpenHelper.Callback(room.version) {
171:             override fun onConfigure(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) = room.onConfigure(db)
172:             override fun onCreate(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) = room.onCreate(db)
173:             override fun onUpgrade(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase, oldVersion: Int, newVersion: Int) =
174:                 room.onUpgrade(db, oldVersion, newVersion)
175:             override fun onDowngrade(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase, oldVersion: Int, newVersion: Int) =
176:                 room.onDowngrade(db, oldVersion, newVersion)
177:             override fun onOpen(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) = room.onOpen(db)
178:             override fun onCorruption(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) {
179:                 Log.e(TAG, "Room reported corruption; the file is kept for recovery")
180:             }
181:         }
182:         return FrameworkSQLiteOpenHelperFactory().create(
183:             SupportSQLiteOpenHelper.Configuration.builder(NoDeletes(configuration.context))
184:                 .name(configuration.name)
185:                 .callback(keeping)
186:                 .noBackupDirectory(configuration.useNoBackupDirectory)
187:                 .allowDataLossOnRecovery(configuration.allowDataLossOnRecovery)
188:                 .build()
189:         )
190:     }
191: }
192: 
193: /**
194:  * The Context the open helper is given, so that the helper cannot delete the
195:  * ledger. In androidx.sqlite 2.4.0, the version Room 2.6.1 pins,
196:  * FrameworkSQLiteOpenHelper retries a failed open once after 500 ms, and if
197:  * that retry fails inside Room's onOpen with any SQLiteException — damage, an
198:  * I/O error, or merely a lock — it calls Context.deleteDatabase and opens
199:  * again, which creates an empty ledger. That branch never reads
200:  * allowDataLossOnRecovery; only an error from the open itself is gated by it.
201:  * androidx-main changed this between sqlite 2.5.0-alpha04 and alpha05 (mid
202:  * 2024) so that onOpen errors are also rethrown unless data loss is allowed,
203:  * which Room never asks for. After an upgrade past Room 2.6 this is a second
204:  * lock on a shut door, and still harmless: nothing legitimate deletes the
205:  * ledger through this Context, and Room's pre-packaged-copy helper holds the
206:  * unwrapped one. Refused here, the helper's third attempt runs on the same
207:  * file, its failure surfaces, RecoverySequence classifies it, and the file
208:  * is kept.
209:  */
210: private class NoDeletes(base: Context) : ContextWrapper(base) {
211:     override fun deleteDatabase(name: String): Boolean {
212:         Log.e(TAG, "refused to delete $name; recovery decides what happens to it")
213:         return false
214:     }
215: }
216: 
217: /**
218:  * The files behind [RecoverySequence.Disk], and nothing more: every decision
219:  * about them is made in :core, where the order they are made in is tested.
220:  */
221: private class LedgerDisk(private val app: Context) : RecoverySequence.Disk {
222:     private val live = app.getDatabasePath(LEDGER).absoluteFile
223:     private val dir = checkNotNull(live.parentFile)
224:     private val shelf = File(dir, Recovery.DIR)
225:     private val staging = File(shelf, "incoming.tmp")
226:     private val incoming = File(dir, "$LEDGER.restoring")
227:     private val noticeFile = File(shelf, "last-recovery.properties")
228: 
229:     /** Kept rather than built again: this instance is the one whose open
230:      *  RecoverySequence saw succeed. */
231:     var opened: SpendDatabase? = null
232:         private set
233: 
234:     fun where(keptAs: List<String>) =
235:         AppGraph.Where(live.path, shelf.path, keptAs.map { File(dir, it).path })
236: 
237:     override fun ledgerExists() = live.exists()
238: 
239:     override fun checkLedger(): RecoverySequence.Check = try {
240:         withDatabase(live) { RecoverySequence.Check(firstRow(it, "PRAGMA integrity_check"), it.version) }
241:     } catch (e: SQLiteException) {
242:         RecoverySequence.Check(null, null, raised = describe(e))
243:     } catch (t: Throwable) {
244:         // Not SQLite's verdict on the file, so not evidence of damage either:
245:         // the real open decides instead.
246:         Log.w(TAG, "ledger check did not run", t)
247:         RecoverySequence.Check(null, null)
248:     }
249: 
250:     override fun snapshotNames(): List<String> = shelf.list()?.toList().orEmpty()
251: 
252:     override fun inspectSnapshot(name: String) = inspect(File(shelf, name))
253: 
254:     override fun stageSnapshot(): RecoverySequence.Copy? = try {
255:         shelf.mkdirs()
256:         remove(staging)
257:         // The copy takes the main file alone, so whatever is still in the
258:         // write-ahead log is folded into it first. A checkpoint another
259:         // connection kept from finishing says so in its first column and
260:         // raises nothing; copied anyway, the snapshot would verify while
261:         // missing every row still in the log.
262:         val busy = withDatabase(live) { firstRow(it, "PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)") }
263:         if (busy != "0") throw IOException("checkpoint did not finish (busy=$busy)")
264:         copy(live, staging)
265:         inspect(staging)
266:     } catch (t: Throwable) {
267:         Log.w(TAG, "snapshot not taken", t)
268:         null
269:     }
270: 
271:     override fun publishStaged(name: String) = staging.renameTo(File(shelf, name))
272: 
273:     override fun discardStaged() = remove(staging)
274: 
275:     override fun deleteSnapshot(name: String) {
276:         if (Recovery.isSnapshotName(name)) remove(File(shelf, name))
277:     }
278: 
279:     override fun restore(snapshot: String, keepAs: String): Boolean {
280:         // Copied before the ledger moves, so a full disk fails here with the
281:         // ledger still in place rather than with nothing where it was.
282:         try {
283:             remove(incoming)
284:             copy(File(shelf, snapshot), incoming)
285:         } catch (t: Throwable) {
286:             Log.w(TAG, "restore copy failed", t)
287:             remove(incoming)
288:             return false
289:         }
290:         // Even with no ledger there, a stray log beside where it was would be
291:         // replayed into the copy, so the move runs either way.
292:         if (!setAside(keepAs)) {
293:             remove(incoming)
294:             return false
295:         }
296:         if (incoming.renameTo(live)) return true
297:         // Its logs go back with it: the main file alone would open as it was
298:         // before its last commits, and the log left under the kept name
299:         // would be replaced by the next file set aside in this second.
300:         moveWithLogs(keepAs, LEDGER)
301:         remove(incoming)
302:         return false
303:     }
304: 
305:     override fun setAside(keepAs: String): Boolean = moveWithLogs(LEDGER, keepAs)
306: 
307:     /**
308:      * Moves a database and its -wal, -shm and -journal together, or not at
309:      * all. Left behind, a log would be replayed into whatever takes the
310:      * file's place; deleted instead, a -wal takes with it every commit not
311:      * yet checkpointed. A rename replaces what is at its target, so an
312:      * occupied target refuses the whole move rather than overwrite it.
313:      */
314:     private fun moveWithLogs(from: String, to: String): Boolean {
315:         val parts = listOf("") + SIDE_FILES
316:         if (parts.any { File(dir, to + it).exists() }) return false
317:         val moved = mutableListOf<String>()
318:         for (part in parts) {
319:             val source = File(dir, from + part)
320:             if (!source.exists()) continue
321:             if (!source.renameTo(File(dir, to + part))) {
322:                 for (back in moved.asReversed()) File(dir, to + back).renameTo(File(dir, from + back))
323:                 return false
324:             }
325:             moved += part
326:         }
327:         return true
328:     }
329: 
330:     override fun open(): String? {
331:         var built: SpendDatabase? = null
332:         return try {
333:             built = Room.databaseBuilder(app, SpendDatabase::class.java, LEDGER)
334:                 .addMigrations(*MIGRATIONS)
335:                 .openHelperFactory(KeepDamagedFiles)
336:                 .build()
337:             // Room opens lazily, on whichever query comes first, which would
338:             // put this failure inside a screen or the SMS receiver. Forced
339:             // here, it can be caught, classified and answered.
340:             built.openHelper.writableDatabase
341:             opened = built
342:             null
343:         } catch (t: Throwable) {
344:             Log.w(TAG, "ledger did not open", t)
345:             try { built?.close() } catch (ignored: Throwable) { }
346:             describe(t)
347:         }
348:     }
349: 
350:     fun recordNotice(result: RecoverySequence.Opened) {
351:         try {
352:             shelf.mkdirs()
353:             val notice = Properties()
354:             notice.setProperty("outcome", result.outcome.name)
355:             notice.setProperty("restoredFrom", result.restoredFrom.orEmpty())
356:             notice.setProperty("withoutLoss", result.restoredWithoutLoss.toString())
357:             notice.setProperty("keptAs", result.keptAs.joinToString("/"))
358:             FileOutputStream(noticeFile).use { notice.store(it, null) }
359:         } catch (t: Throwable) {
360:             Log.w(TAG, "recovery notice not written", t)
361:         }
362:     }
363: 
364:     fun readNotice(): AppGraph.Notice? = try {
365:         if (!noticeFile.exists()) null else {
366:             val saved = Properties()
367:             FileInputStream(noticeFile).use { saved.load(it) }
368:             AppGraph.Notice(
369:                 outcome = Recovery.Outcome.valueOf(saved.getProperty("outcome")),
370:                 restoredFrom = saved.getProperty("restoredFrom")?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() },
371:                 withoutLoss = saved.getProperty("withoutLoss") == "true",
372:                 where = where(saved.getProperty("keptAs").orEmpty().split('/').filter { it.isNotEmpty() }),
373:             )
374:         }
375:     } catch (t: Throwable) {
376:         Log.w(TAG, "recovery notice unreadable", t)
377:         null
378:     }
379: 
380:     fun clearNotice() {
381:         noticeFile.delete()
382:     }
383: 
384:     private fun inspect(file: File): RecoverySequence.Copy {
385:         val size = file.length()
386:         return try {
387:             withDatabase(file) { RecoverySequence.Copy(size, firstRow(it, "PRAGMA integrity_check"), tables(it)) }
388:         } catch (e: SQLiteException) {
389:             RecoverySequence.Copy(size, describe(e), emptySet())
390:         } catch (t: Throwable) {
391:             RecoverySequence.Copy(size, null, emptySet())
392:         }
393:     }
394: }
395: 
396: /** Read-write, because a read-only connection cannot roll back a hot journal
397:  *  and would report a healthy ledger that crashed mid-write as unreadable. */
398: private inline fun <T> withDatabase(file: File, block: (SQLiteDatabase) -> T): T =
399:     SQLiteDatabase.openDatabase(
400:         file.path, null,
401:         SQLiteDatabase.OPEN_READWRITE or SQLiteDatabase.NO_LOCALIZED_COLLATORS,
402:         KEEP_FILE,
403:     ).use(block)
404: 
405: private fun firstRow(db: SQLiteDatabase, sql: String): String? =
406:     db.rawQuery(sql, null).use { if (it.moveToFirst()) it.getString(0) else null }
407: 
408: private fun tables(db: SQLiteDatabase): Set<String> =
409:     db.rawQuery("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table'", null).use { rows ->
410:         buildSet { while (rows.moveToNext()) rows.getString(0)?.let(::add) }
411:     }
412: 
413: private fun copy(from: File, to: File) {
414:     FileInputStream(from).use { input ->
415:         FileOutputStream(to).use { output ->
416:             input.copyTo(output)
417:             // On the disk before anything is renamed on the strength of it,
418:             // not in a cache that a power cut would empty.
419:             output.fd.sync()
420:         }
421:     }
422:     if (to.length() != from.length()) {
423:         throw IOException("copied ${to.length()} of ${from.length()} bytes")
424:     }
425: }
426: 
427: private fun remove(file: File) {
428:     file.delete()
429:     for (suffix in SIDE_FILES) File(file.path + suffix).delete()
430: }
431: 
432: /** The whole causal chain, class names included: Room wraps the SQLite error
433:  *  that says what actually happened, and SQLiteDatabaseLockedException says
434:  *  more in its name than in its message. */
435: private fun describe(t: Throwable): String =
436:     generateSequence(t) { it.cause }.take(8)
437:         .joinToString(" <- ") { "${it.javaClass.name}: ${it.message}" }

FILE android/nativeapp/src/main/java/com/jeevavibeapp/spendwise/data/Misses.kt
1: package com.jeevavibeapp.spendwise.data
2: 
3: import androidx.room.*
4: import androidx.room.migration.Migration
5: import androidx.sqlite.db.SupportSQLiteDatabase
6: import com.jeevavibeapp.spendwise.core.ParseMisses
7: import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
8: 
9: /**
10:  * A bank message the parser could not read — as its shape only.
11:  *
12:  * There is no body column, and that is the design rather than an omission:
13:  * the raw message is the most sensitive text on the phone, and the backup
14:  * deliberately carries none. [shape] is [ParseMisses.redact]'s output, made
15:  * before this row exists. [bodyHash] lets a rescan recognise a message it has
16:  * already logged; it is never shown and never exported.
17:  *
18:  * [cleared] is a soft delete for the same reason fraud alerts keep their
19:  * dismissed rows: the launch scan meets the same ninety days of inbox on
20:  * every launch, and a hard delete would have every cleared message back the
21:  * next time the app opened.
22:  */
23: @Entity(
24:     tableName = "parse_misses",
25:     indices = [Index(value = ["bodyHash"], unique = true)],
26: )
27: data class ParseMissEntity(
28:     @PrimaryKey val id: String,
29:     val bodyHash: String,
30:     /** The DLT entity ("HDFCBK"); null when pasted. */
31:     val sender: String? = null,
32:     val source: String,                     // sms | paste
33:     val shape: String,
34:     val reason: String,
35:     /** Rows from another parser are stale: that parser may read them now. */
36:     val parserVersion: String,
37:     val seenCount: Int = 1,
38:     /** Epoch millis, when the message reached the phone. */
39:     val firstSeenAt: Long,
40:     val lastSeenAt: Long,
41:     val cleared: Boolean = false,
42: )
43: 
44: /**
45:  * Separate from [SpendDao] because nothing here is the ledger. Every write is
46:  * bounded by [ParseMisses.MAX_ROWS] and [ParseMisses.MAX_AGE_DAYS], so the
47:  * log cannot grow with the inbox that feeds it.
48:  */
49: @Dao
50: abstract class ParseMissDao {
51: 
52:     @Query("SELECT * FROM parse_misses WHERE bodyHash = :hash LIMIT 1")
53:     abstract suspend fun byHash(hash: String): ParseMissEntity?
54: 
55:     @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.IGNORE)
56:     abstract suspend fun insert(row: ParseMissEntity): Long
57: 
58:     /** The same message arriving again, live. A clear hid what had been
59:      *  seen; a fresh arrival is news, so it shows again. */
60:     @Query("""
61:         UPDATE parse_misses
62:         SET seenCount = seenCount + 1, lastSeenAt = MAX(lastSeenAt, :at), cleared = 0
63:         WHERE id = :id""")
64:     abstract suspend fun seenAgain(id: String, at: Long)
65: 
66:     @Query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM parse_misses")
67:     abstract suspend fun count(): Int
68: 
69:     @Query("SELECT MIN(lastSeenAt) FROM parse_misses")
70:     abstract suspend fun oldest(): Long?
71: 
72:     @Query("DELETE FROM parse_misses WHERE parserVersion != :version")
73:     abstract suspend fun dropOtherParsers(version: String): Int
74: 
75:     @Query("DELETE FROM parse_misses WHERE lastSeenAt < :before")
76:     abstract suspend fun dropSeenBefore(before: Long): Int
77: 
78:     @Query("""
79:         DELETE FROM parse_misses WHERE id NOT IN (
80:             SELECT id FROM parse_misses ORDER BY lastSeenAt DESC, id DESC LIMIT :keep)""")
81:     abstract suspend fun keepNewest(keep: Int): Int
82: 
83:     /**
84:      * Log one miss, bounded, in one transaction.
85:      *
86:      * A rescan ([live] false) finding a row already there changes nothing —
87:      * the same rule the capture path keeps, or every launch would add one to
88:      * the count of a message that arrived once. Pruning runs only on an
89:      * insert, which is the only thing that grows the table.
90:      *
91:      * @return true when the message is in the log after this call.
92:      */
93:     @Transaction
94:     open suspend fun record(row: ParseMissEntity, live: Boolean, keep: Int, before: Long): Boolean {
95:         val existing = byHash(row.bodyHash)
96:         if (existing != null) {
97:             if (live) seenAgain(existing.id, row.lastSeenAt)
98:             return true
99:         }
100:         dropOtherParsers(row.parserVersion)
101:         dropSeenBefore(before)
102:         if (!ParseMisses.admits(count(), oldest(), row.lastSeenAt, keep)) return false
103:         if (insert(row) == -1L) return false
104:         keepNewest(keep)
105:         return true
106:     }
107: 
108:     /** What the screen lists: this parser's, not cleared. */
109:     @Query("""
110:         SELECT * FROM parse_misses
111:         WHERE cleared = 0 AND parserVersion = :version
112:         ORDER BY lastSeenAt DESC""")
113:     abstract fun showing(version: String): Flow<List<ParseMissEntity>>
114: 
115:     @Query("""
116:         SELECT * FROM parse_misses
117:         WHERE cleared = 0 AND parserVersion = :version
118:         ORDER BY lastSeenAt DESC""")
119:     abstract suspend fun showingNow(version: String): List<ParseMissEntity>
120: 
121:     @Query("UPDATE parse_misses SET cleared = 1 WHERE cleared = 0")
122:     abstract suspend fun clearAll(): Int
123: }
124: 
125: /**
126:  * v3 → v4: the unreadable-message log.
127:  *
128:  * Additive, and real for the reason every migration here is real: the
129:  * destructive fallback answers "we added a table" by deleting the ledger.
130:  * Written against what Room 2.6.1 generates for [ParseMissEntity] — the
131:  * conventions are the ones in schemas/…/3.json: a non-null Kotlin type is
132:  * NOT NULL, Boolean and Long are INTEGER, a Kotlin default is not an SQL
133:  * DEFAULT, and the index is `index_<table>_<column>`. Room re-reads the
134:  * table after this runs and refuses to open a database that differs.
135:  */
136: val MIGRATION_3_4 = object : Migration(3, 4) {
137:     override fun migrate(db: SupportSQLiteDatabase) {
138:         db.execSQL(
139:             "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `parse_misses` (" +
140:                 "`id` TEXT NOT NULL, " +
141:                 "`bodyHash` TEXT NOT NULL, " +
142:                 "`sender` TEXT, " +
143:                 "`source` TEXT NOT NULL, " +
144:                 "`shape` TEXT NOT NULL, " +
145:                 "`reason` TEXT NOT NULL, " +
146:                 "`parserVersion` TEXT NOT NULL, " +
147:                 "`seenCount` INTEGER NOT NULL, " +
148:                 "`firstSeenAt` INTEGER NOT NULL, " +
149:                 "`lastSeenAt` INTEGER NOT NULL, " +
150:                 "`cleared` INTEGER NOT NULL, " +
151:                 "PRIMARY KEY(`id`))")
152:         db.execSQL(
153:             "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS `index_parse_misses_bodyHash` " +
154:                 "ON `parse_misses` (`bodyHash`)")
155:     }
156: }

FILE android/nativeapp/build.gradle
1: // The native app: Kotlin + Room + Compose, no interpreter and no server.
2: //
3: // A SEPARATE module from :app on purpose. :app is the shipping Capacitor +
4: // Chaquopy build, and while this one is being finished it must not be able to
5: // break that one — a build error here should cost a red job, not the only
6: // installable APK.
7: plugins {
8:     id 'com.android.application'
9:     id 'org.jetbrains.kotlin.android'
10:     id 'com.google.devtools.ksp'
11:     id 'org.jetbrains.kotlin.plugin.compose'
12: }
13: 
14: android {
15:     namespace 'com.jeevavibeapp.spendwise'
16:     compileSdk rootProject.ext.compileSdkVersion
17: 
18:     defaultConfig {
19:         applicationId "com.jeevavibeapp.spendwise.native"
20:         minSdkVersion rootProject.ext.minSdkVersion
21:         targetSdkVersion rootProject.ext.targetSdkVersion
22:         versionCode 1
23:         versionName "2.0"
24:     }
25: 
26:     // Same two flavours as :app, and for the same reason: Play Protect
27:     // refuses to sideload anything declaring SMS permissions in India,
28:     // Singapore, Thailand and Brazil.
29:     flavorDimensions 'capture'
30:     productFlavors {
31:         sms   { dimension 'capture' }
32:         nosms { dimension 'capture' }
33:     }
34: 
35:     // The same signing policy as :app, and for the same reason; see the long
36:     // comment there. AGP's default debug keystore is generated fresh on every
37:     // CI runner, so signing with it gave each build a different key: no
38:     // update could install over the previous one, and the only way forward
39:     // was an uninstall — which destroys the ledger, because there is no
40:     // cloud copy by design.
41:     signingConfigs {
42:         devDebug {
43:             storeFile file('../app/spendwise.jks')
44:             storePassword 'spendwise'
45:             keyAlias 'spendwise'
46:             keyPassword 'spendwise'
47:         }
48:         release {
49:             def ksFile = System.getenv('SPENDWISE_KEYSTORE_FILE')
50:             if (ksFile) {
51:                 storeFile file(ksFile)
52:                 storePassword System.getenv('SPENDWISE_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD')
53:                 keyAlias System.getenv('SPENDWISE_KEY_ALIAS')
54:                 keyPassword System.getenv('SPENDWISE_KEY_PASSWORD')
55:             }
56:         }
57:     }
58: 
59:     buildTypes {
60:         debug {
61:             signingConfig signingConfigs.devDebug
62:         }
63:         release {
64:             minifyEnabled false
65:             if (System.getenv('SPENDWISE_KEYSTORE_FILE')) {
66:                 signingConfig signingConfigs.release
67:             } else {
68:                 signingConfig signingConfigs.devDebug
69:             }
70:         }
71:     }
72: 
73:     buildFeatures { compose true }
74:     compileOptions {
75:         sourceCompatibility JavaVersion.VERSION_17
76:         targetCompatibility JavaVersion.VERSION_17
77:     }
78:     kotlinOptions { jvmTarget = '17' }
79: }
80: 
81: // Room writes its schema here so migrations can be diffed in review. A finance
82: // app with no cloud copy cannot afford a destructive fallback, so every schema
83: // change has to ship a real migration.
84: //
85: // Top level, not inside defaultConfig: `ksp` is an extension the KSP plugin
86: // adds to the PROJECT, and nesting it in defaultConfig only ever resolved by
87: // Groovy falling back up the owner chain — it is not a DefaultConfig method.
88: ksp {
89:     arg("room.schemaLocation", "$projectDir/schemas")
90: }
91: 
92: dependencies {
93:     implementation project(':core')
94: 
95:     implementation "androidx.core:core-ktx:1.13.1"
96:     implementation "androidx.activity:activity-compose:1.9.2"
97:     implementation "androidx.lifecycle:lifecycle-runtime-ktx:2.8.6"
98: 
99:     def composeBom = platform('androidx.compose:compose-bom:2024.09.03')
100:     implementation composeBom
101:     implementation 'androidx.compose.ui:ui'
102:     implementation 'androidx.compose.material3:material3'
103:     implementation 'androidx.compose.ui:ui-tooling-preview'
104:     debugImplementation 'androidx.compose.ui:ui-tooling'
105: 
106:     implementation "androidx.room:room-runtime:2.6.1"
107:     implementation "androidx.room:room-ktx:2.6.1"
108:     ksp "androidx.room:room-compiler:2.6.1"
109: 
110:     implementation "org.jetbrains.kotlinx:kotlinx-coroutines-android:1.8.1"
111: }

FILE tools/typecheck.sh
1: #!/bin/bash
2: # Type-check the Android sources without the Android SDK.
3: #
4: # Gradle cannot run here: the SDK is not installed and dl.google.com is
5: # unreachable. kotlinc can, so the real app sources are compiled against the
6: # hand-written declarations in tools/compose-stubs — see that directory's
7: # 00-README.md for what this does and does not prove.
8: #
9: # Usage:  tools/typecheck.sh [-v] [file.kt ...]
10: #   no args   every Android source, compiled as one unit
11: #   files     only those, still compiled with the stubs and :core
12: #   -v        print the full error text as well as the per-file summary
13: set -uo pipefail
14: cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
15: ROOT=$PWD
16: 
17: KH=${KOTLIN_HOME:-/opt/kotlin}
18: KOTLINC=${KOTLINC:-kotlinc-jvm}
19: STUBS=$ROOT/tools/compose-stubs
20: APP=$ROOT/android/nativeapp/src/main/java
21: 
22: VERBOSE=0
23: if [ "${1:-}" = "-v" ]; then VERBOSE=1; shift; fi
24: 
25: for jar in "$KH/kotlin-stdlib.jar" "$KH/coroutines.jar"; do
26:   [ -f "$jar" ] || { echo "missing $jar — set KOTLIN_HOME" >&2; exit 2; }
27: done
28: 
29: 
30: if [ "$#" -gt 0 ]; then SOURCES=("$@")
31: else mapfile -t SOURCES < <(find "$APP" -name '*.kt' | sort); fi
32: 
33: mapfile -t STUB_SOURCES < <(find "$STUBS" -name '*.kt' | sort)
34: 
35: OUT=$(mktemp -d)
36: trap 'rm -rf "$OUT"' EXIT
37: 
38: # :core is compiled fresh, here, every run — never read from core/build/classes.
39: # Reading the shared directory had two failure modes. A concurrent
40: # run-tests.sh could be halfway through replacing it. Worse, it could simply
41: # be STALE: change a :core signature, run this without re-running the core
42: # tests first, and the app was checked against the old API and passed. That is
43: # a false green, and nothing about it looks wrong. :core takes a few seconds
44: # to compile; that is the price of the check meaning what it says.
45: mapfile -t CORE_SOURCES < <(find "$ROOT/core/src/main/kotlin" -name '*.kt' | sort)
46: CORE_CLASSES=$OUT/core-classes
47: if ! "$KOTLINC" -cp "$KH/kotlin-stdlib.jar" -nowarn -d "$CORE_CLASSES" \
48:       "${CORE_SOURCES[@]}" >"$OUT/core.log" 2>&1; then
49:   echo "── :core does not compile, so the app cannot be checked against it" >&2
50:   grep -v JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS "$OUT/core.log" | grep "error:" | head -10 >&2
51:   echo "   fix :core first: ./core/run-tests.sh" >&2
52:   exit 2
53: fi
54: 
55: # Everything in one invocation: the app files reference each other, so
56: # compiling them separately would report their own declarations as missing.
57: $KOTLINC -cp "$KH/kotlin-stdlib.jar:$KH/coroutines.jar:$CORE_CLASSES" \
58:     -nowarn -d "$OUT/classes" "${STUB_SOURCES[@]}" "${SOURCES[@]}" \
59:     > "$OUT/raw" 2>&1
60: status=$?
61: grep -v JAVA_TOOL "$OUT/raw" | grep 'error:' > "$OUT/errors" || true
62: 
63: # A stub error means the harness is wrong, not the app. Reported separately
64: # so it can never be mistaken for a defect in the code under test.
65: grep    "^$STUBS/" "$OUT/errors" > "$OUT/harness" || true
66: grep -v "^$STUBS/" "$OUT/errors" > "$OUT/app"     || true
67: 
68: total=$(wc -l < "$OUT/app")
69: harness=$(wc -l < "$OUT/harness")
70: 
71: # A compiler that never ran prints no "error:" line, and every count above is
72: # then zero — which reads exactly like success. Anything that stops kotlinc
73: # from starting (missing binary, bad classpath, OOM) would otherwise be
74: # reported as "no errors", so a non-zero exit with nothing parsed is fatal.
75: if [ "$status" -ne 0 ] && [ "$total" -eq 0 ] && [ "$harness" -eq 0 ]; then
76:   echo "!! the compiler exited $status but printed no 'error:' line."
77:   echo "   The check did NOT run. This is a broken harness, not clean code."
78:   echo
79:   grep -v JAVA_TOOL "$OUT/raw" | head -20 | sed 's|^|   |'
80:   exit 2
81: fi
82: 
83: echo "── type-check: $(printf '%s\n' "${SOURCES[@]}" | wc -l) source files, ${#STUB_SOURCES[@]} stub files"
84: echo
85: 
86: if [ "$harness" -gt 0 ]; then
87:   echo "!! $harness error(s) INSIDE THE HARNESS — fix tools/compose-stubs, not the app:"
88:   sed "s|$ROOT/||" "$OUT/harness"
89:   echo
90: fi
91: 
92: if [ "$total" -eq 0 ]; then
93:   echo "no errors in $(printf '%s\n' "${SOURCES[@]}" | wc -l) app source files"
94: else
95:   echo "$total error(s):"
96:   echo
97:   sed "s|^$ROOT/||" "$OUT/app" | sed 's/:[0-9]*:[0-9]*: error:.*//' | sort | uniq -c |
98:     sort -rn | awk '{ printf "  %5d  %s\n", $1, $2 }'
99:   echo
100:   if [ "$VERBOSE" -eq 1 ]; then
101:     sed "s|^$ROOT/||" "$OUT/app"
102:   else
103:     echo "  (re-run with -v for the full list)"
104:   fi
105: fi
106: 
107: [ "$total" -eq 0 ] && [ "$harness" -eq 0 ]

FILE core/src/test/kotlin/AuditTest.kt
1: import com.jeevavibeapp.spendwise.core.*
2: import java.time.LocalDate
3: import java.time.LocalDateTime
4: import java.time.YearMonth
5: 
6: /**
7:  * Probes for the findings of the independent audit of main @ 2fa8dc0, run
8:  * against the native port. Each section names the finding it answers. A
9:  * finding the port already fixes stays here as a regression check; one it
10:  * does not fix gets its failing case here first, then its fix.
11:  */
12: private var passed = 0
13: private var failed = 0
14: private fun check(name: String, ok: Boolean, detail: String = "") {
15:     if (ok) passed++ else { failed++; println("  FAIL  $name${if (detail.isNotEmpty()) "  [$detail]" else ""}") }
16: }
17: 
18: private val NOW = LocalDateTime.of(2026, 7, 8, 12, 0)
19: 
20: private fun ingest(body: String, now: LocalDateTime = NOW) = Pipeline.ingest(
21:     "AD-HDFCBK", body, now, { null }, { _, _, _ -> Pipeline.Resolution("Payee", "food", 90) })
22: 
23: private fun key(body: String, now: LocalDateTime = NOW): String? =
24:     (ingest(body, now) as? Pipeline.Outcome.Capture)?.dedupKey
25: 
26: fun main() {
27:     println("=== OTP / failed / reversed / declined: money that did not move ===")
28:     val notMoved = listOf(
29:         "OTP 456789 for txn of Rs.2,500 on your A/c XX1234. Do not share. Ref 512345678901",
30:         "Use 4455 as OTP to pay Rs 999 at FLIPKART from A/c XX1234",
31:         "Txn of Rs.1,200.00 from A/c XX1234 to SWIGGY failed on 08-07-26. UPI Ref 512345678901",
32:         "Your payment of Rs.350.00 to ZOMATO has failed. A/c XX1234 UPI Ref 512345678901",
33:         "Rs.500.00 debited from A/c XX1234 on 08-07-26 has been reversed. Ref 512345678901",
34:         "Txn of Rs.5000 on Card XX1234 at AMAZON was declined on 08-07-26. Ref 553201998877",
35:     )
36:     for (sms in notMoved) {
37:         val r = ingest(sms)
38:         check("not banked: ${sms.take(55)}", r !is Pipeline.Outcome.Capture,
39:             "got=${r::class.simpleName}")
40:     }
41: 
42:     println("=== account masks are never the amount or the payee ===")
43:     data class Mask(val sms: String, val amount: Double, val merchant: String?)
44:     val masks = listOf(
45:         Mask("Rs.450.00 debited from a/c **1234 on 05-Jan-24 to ZOMATO Ref 883120114455 UPI", 450.0, "ZOMATO"),
46:         Mask("Dear UPI user A/C X1234 debited by 199.0 on date 08Jul26 trf to SWIGGY Refno 553201998877", 199.0, "SWIGGY"),
47:         Mask("INR 2,300.00 debited from YES BANK A/c XX3344 on 08-Jul-26 towards MYNTRA. Ref 883120114455", 2300.0, "MYNTRA"),
48:         Mask("Rs 899 debited from A/c ending 7788 on 08-07-26 to NETFLIX UPI Ref 519023481234", 899.0, "NETFLIX"),
49:         Mask("Rs.340.00 spent using Card xx5566 at DMART on 08-07-26", 340.0, "DMART"),
50:         // No payee in the message at all. The payer side ("from A/c XX1234")
51:         // or the payee side ("to your A/c") must not stand in for one: every
52:         // payee-less debit from the account would share that name, and one
53:         // confirmation would teach the engine to file all of them the same way.
54:         Mask("Rs.100.00 debited from A/c XX1234 on 08-07-26. UPI Ref 512345678901", 100.0, null),
55:         Mask("Rs.5,000.00 credited to your A/c XX9012 on 08-07-26. UPI Ref 512345678901", 5000.0, null),
56:         Mask("INR 62,500.00 credited to A/c XX1234 on 01-07-26 towards SALARY JUL26. Ref 998877665544", 62500.0, null),
57:         Mask("Rs.750.00 debited from Acct **4321 on 08-07-26. Ref 512345678901", 750.0, null),
58:     )
59:     for (m in masks) {
60:         val p = Parsing.parse(m.sms)
61:         check("amount from: ${m.sms.take(45)}", p.amount == m.amount, "want=${m.amount} got=${p.amount}")
62:         check("payee from: ${m.sms.take(45)}", p.rawMerchant == m.merchant,
63:             "want=${m.merchant} got=${p.rawMerchant}")
64:     }
65:     // Keys these messages were banked under before the payee fix, computed
66:     // with the parser at 6bd77fb5. The launch scan finds banked rows by
67:     // recomputing exactly this string, so it must not move.
68:     val bankedBefore = listOf(
69:         "Rs.100.00 debited from A/c XX1234 on 08-07-26. UPI Ref 512345678901" to
70:             "100.0|A C XX1234|512345678901|2026-07-08",
71:         "Rs.5,000.00 credited to your A/c XX9012 on 08-07-26. UPI Ref 512345678901" to
72:             "5000.0|YOUR|512345678901|2026-07-08",
73:         "INR 62,500.00 credited to A/c XX1234 on 01-07-26 towards SALARY JUL26. Ref 998877665544" to
74:             "62500.0|A C XX1234|998877665544|2026-07-01",
75:     )
76:     for ((sms, want) in bankedBefore) {
77:         check("dedup key unchanged: ${sms.take(45)}", key(sms) == want, "want=$want got=${key(sms)}")
78:     }
79: 
80:     println("=== #9: a refund that also says 'paid' is money coming in ===")
81:     // The debit verb in these describes the original payment, not this
82:     // message. Filed as an expense, a refund doubles the spend it undoes.
83:     val refunds = listOf(
84:         "Refund of Rs.500.00 for your order paid via UPI has been credited to your A/c XX1234 on 08-07-26. Ref 512345678901" to
85:             "500.0|YOUR|512345678901|2026-07-08",
86:         "Rs.250.00 credited to A/c XX1234 on 08-07-26 as refund of the amount paid to AMAZON. UPI Ref 512345678901" to
87:             "250.0|A C XX1234|512345678901|2026-07-08",
88:         "You have received a refund of Rs.120 for the bill paid at DMART. Credited to A/c XX1234 UPI Ref 512345678901" to
89:             "120.0|DMART|512345678901|2026-07-08",
90:     )
91:     for ((sms, banked) in refunds) {
92:         val p = Parsing.parse(sms)
93:         check("captured: ${sms.take(50)}", p.matched)
94:         check("income: ${sms.take(50)}", p.type == "income", "got=${p.type}")
95:         check("dedup key unchanged: ${sms.take(45)}", key(sms) == banked, "want=$banked got=${key(sms)}")
96:     }
97:     check("the refund keeps its payee", Parsing.parse(refunds[2].first).rawMerchant == "DMART")
98:     // The rule must not swallow debits that merely mention a credit.
99:     check("ICICI 'debited …; SWIGGY credited' is still a debit",
100:         Parsing.parse("ICICI Bank Acct XX823 debited for Rs 320.00 on 08-Jul-26; SWIGGY credited. UPI:519023481234").type == "expense")
101:     check("a debit that mentions an earlier refund is still a debit",
102:         Parsing.parse("Rs.300.00 debited from A/c XX1234 on 08-07-26 to MYNTRA, refund of Rs.300 credited earlier. UPI Ref 512345678901").type == "expense")
103:     check("a plain payment is still a debit",
104:         Parsing.parse("You paid Rs.150 to Ramesh Kumar using Google Pay. UPI transaction ID: 519023481234").type == "expense")
105:     check("a refund with no 'paid' was already income",
106:         Parsing.parse("Rs.599.00 credited to your A/c XX1234 on 08-07-26 from AMAZON refund. Ref 553201998877").type == "income")
107: 
108:     println("=== #11: times are the bank's wall clock, never shifted to UTC ===")
109:     val late = Parsing.parse("Rs.450.00 debited from A/c XX1234 on 08-07-26 00:30:15 to ZOMATO Ref 883120114455")
110:     check("00:30 on the 8th stays 00:30 on the 8th",
111:         late.occurredAt == LocalDateTime.of(2026, 7, 8, 0, 30), "got=${late.occurredAt}")
112:     check("and its dedup key is filed under the 8th",
113:         key("Rs.450.00 debited from A/c XX1234 on 08-07-26 00:30:15 to ZOMATO Ref 883120114455")
114:             ?.endsWith("2026-07-08") == true)
115:     val firstOfMonth = Tx("t1", 100.0, "expense", LocalDateTime.of(2026, 7, 1, 0, 10), "Zomato", "food")
116:     check("a spend at 00:10 on the 1st counts toward the new month",
117:         Budgets.monthCategorySpend(listOf(firstOfMonth), YearMonth.of(2026, 7),
118:             LocalDate.of(2026, 7, 1))["food"] == 100.0)
119:     check("and toward today, not yesterday",
120:         Budgets.dailySeries(listOf(firstOfMonth), LocalDate.of(2026, 7, 1), days = 1)
121:             .single().amount == 100.0)
122: 
123:     println("=== #12: one payee name, one answer ===")
124:     // Learning is one row per raw name (unique index on learning.rawName),
125:     // so two learned mappings cannot tie. A dead heat in the categoriser is a
126:     // 50/50 guess, below its floor, so it declines rather than picking one.
127:     val tie = Categorizer.train(listOf("ALPHA SHOP" to "food", "ALPHA SHOP" to "travel"))
128:     check("an exact categoriser tie declines instead of guessing",
129:         tie?.predict("ALPHA SHOP") == null, "got=${tie?.predict("ALPHA SHOP")}")
130: 
131:     println()
132:     println("=".repeat(60))
133:     println("$passed passed, $failed failed")
134:     if (failed > 0) kotlin.system.exitProcess(1)
135: }
